### [1] [KernelGenBench: A Multi-Source and Multi-Chip Benchmark for LLM-based Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27231)
*Peiyu Zang,Jian Tao,Jialing Zhang,Yichen Yuan,Wentao Zhang,Guang Liu,Yonghua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: LLM生成kernel的benchmark，跨算子源和芯片平台，直接相关自动代码优化与agent评估

TL;DR: 提出了KernelGenBench，一个统一的基准，用于评估LLM和智能体生成的Triton内核在多种算子来源和异构硬件平台上的性能，并揭示了智能体方法的优势、跨平台性能下降问题以及高昂的生成成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内核开发高度专业化和劳动密集，LLM和智能体框架为自动生成提供了可能，但缺乏一个全面的基准来严格评估LLM生成的内核在多样化算子来源和异构硬件平台上的表现。

Method: 构建KernelGenBench基准，包含两个子基准：KernelGenBench-MS（多源，210个算子，三个来源）和KernelGenBench-MC（多芯片，110个算子，六个硬件平台），并进行了大规模评估（消耗超过150亿个token），比较了基于智能体的方法和纯LLM采样方法。

Result: 基于智能体的方法始终优于纯LLM采样；cuBLAS算子最具挑战性；生成性能在不同硬件平台间差异显著，如AutoKernel从NVIDIA的87%降到平台E的25%；自主内核生成成本高昂，专用智能体方法平均每个成功算子消耗511万个token，比简单LLM采样高出几个数量级。

Conclusion: KernelGenBench为评估LLM生成的内核提供了统一基准，揭示了现有方法的优缺点，强调跨平台性能可移植性和成本效率仍是重大挑战，为未来内核自动生成研究提供了方向。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）显著增加了对高效加速器内核的需求，但内核开发仍然是一项高度专业化且劳动密集型的任务。近年来LLM和智能体框架的兴起为自动内核生成提供了一条有前景的途径。然而，尽管进展迅速，目前仍缺乏一个全面的基准来严格评估LLM生成的内核在多样化算子来源或异构硬件平台上的表现。我们提出了KernelGenBench，一个统一基准，用于系统评估LLM和智能体生成的Triton内核在多样化算子来源和异构硬件平台上的性能。它包含两个互补的子基准：KernelGenBench-MS（多源），评估来自三个来源的210个算子（超越了标准的以PyTorch为中心的任务）；以及KernelGenBench-MC（多芯片），使用110个算子的子集衡量跨六个异构硬件平台的性能可移植性。我们的大规模评估消耗了超过150亿个token，结果表明：（1）基于智能体的方法始终优于纯LLM采样方法，而cuBLAS算子是所有方法中最具挑战性的；（2）生成性能在不同硬件平台间差异显著，即使是最近的内核专用智能体也面临严重的跨平台性能下降（例如，AutoKernel从NVIDIA上的87%下降到平台E上的25%）；（3）自主内核生成仍然成本高昂，专用智能体方法平均每个成功算子消耗511万个token（AKO4all达到519万个），比简单的LLM采样方法高出数个数量级。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have significantly increased the demand for efficient accelerator kernels, but kernel development remains a highly specialized and labor-intensive task. The recent rise of LLMs and agentic frameworks offers a promising pathway toward automatic kernel generation. However, despite rapid progress, there is still no comprehensive benchmark to rigorously evaluate LLM-generated kernels across diverse operator sources or heterogeneous hardware platforms. We present KernelGenBench, a unified benchmark for systematically evaluating LLM- and agent-generated Triton kernels across diverse operator sources and heterogeneous hardware platforms. It comprises two complementary sub-benchmarks: KernelGenBench-MS (Multi-Source), evaluating 210 operators from three sources beyond standard PyTorch-centric tasks, and KernelGenBench-MC (Multi-Chip), measuring performance portability across six heterogeneous hardware platforms using a 110-operator subset. Our large-scale evaluation, consuming over 15 billion tokens, shows: (1) agent-based methods consistently outperform pure LLM sampling methods, while cuBLAS operators are the most challenging across all methods; (2) generation performance varies significantly across hardware platforms, with even recent kernel-specialized agents experiencing severe cross-platform degradation (e.g., AutoKernel drops from 87% on NVIDIA to 25% on Platform E); (3) autonomous kernel generation remains highly cost-intensive, with specialized agent methods averaging 5.11 million tokens per successful operator (AKO4all reaches 5.19 million), orders of magnitude higher than simple LLM sampling approaches.

</details>


### [2] [PAUSE: A User-Centric Benchmark for Personal AI Assistants in Unified Service Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27354)
*Haoyu Chen,Xirui Shi,Yuyao Wang,Jerry Chen,Di Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 个人AI助手在统一服务环境中的用户中心benchmark，涉及状态推理、约束和多轮交互，评测框架可迁移到agent任务

TL;DR: 提出了PAUSE基准，用于在状态化、服务集成环境中评估个人AI助手，并发现现有先进模型在需要状态推理和配置感知的任务上完成率不足70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准往往将服务上下文碎片化或抽象掉用户状态，无法在现实服务设置中评估以用户为中心的个人助手行为。

Method: 引入PAUSE基准，涵盖状态化、服务集成环境、用户模拟、多体系评估框架以及用户中心的合成流水线。

Result: 最先进的专有模型在需要状态推理和配置感知的场景中任务完成率低于70%，并出现一致且可解释的失败模式。

Conclusion: PAUSE揭示了实际部署中的关键挑战，并提供可扩展的合成流水线以支持基准扩展和未来研究。

中文摘要：个人AI助手日益被部署为面向任务的、工具增强的智能体，在统一的服务环境中运行，以支持用户的日常活动。在现实场景中，此类助手必须对持久化用户状态进行推理，尊重特定于用户的配置和权限，并在多个服务之间维持长周期、约束感知的交互。然而，现有基准测试常常将服务上下文碎片化或抽象掉用户状态，限制了其在现实服务环境中评估以用户为中心的个人助手行为的能力。我们引入了PAUSE，一个用于在状态化、服务集成环境中评估个人AI助手的以用户为中心的基准。PAUSE通过要求智能体在协调跨异构用户自有资源的操作的同时，维持与环境状态的一致性以及对多轮交互的授权约束，捕捉了真实世界助手部署的核心挑战。该基准通过逼真的用户模拟整合了显式的用户-智能体交互，使得评估超越静态工具执行。为了支持有原则且可复现的评估，PAUSE采用了一种与任务特性对齐的多体系评估框架。开放式服务管理任务使用语义和轨迹级行为指标进行评估，而约束密集型任务则允许确定性的、基于状态的验证。基准结果显示，即使是最先进的专有模型，在需要状态推理和配置感知的场景中，任务完成率也未能达到70%，揭示了一致且可解释的失败模式。最后，我们提出了一个以用户为中心的合成流水线，能够可扩展地生成连贯的服务环境、用户配置和可靠标注的任务，支持基准的可扩展性和未来研究。

Original abstract: Personal AI assistants are increasingly deployed as task-oriented, tool-augmented agents that operate within unified service environments to support everyday user activities. In realistic settings, such assistants must reason over persistent user state, respect user-specific configurations and permissions, and sustain long-horizon, constraint-aware interactions across multiple services. Existing benchmarks, however, often fragment service contexts or abstract away user state, limiting their ability to evaluate user-centric personal assistant behavior in realistic service settings. We introduce PAUSE, a user-centric benchmark for evaluating personal AI assistants in stateful, service-integrated environments. PAUSE captures core challenges of real-world assistant deployment by requiring agents to coordinate actions across heterogeneous user-owned resources while maintaining consistency with environment state, authorization constraints over multi-turn interactions. The benchmark incorporates explicit user-agent interaction via realistic user simulation, enabling evaluation beyond static tool execution. To support principled and reproducible evaluation, PAUSE adopts a multi-regime evaluation framework aligned with task characteristics. Open-ended service management tasks are assessed using semantic and trajectory-level behavioral metrics, while constraint-intensive tasks admit deterministic, state-based verification. Benchmark results show that even state-of-the-art proprietary models fail to reach 70% task completion on scenarios requiring stateful reasoning and configuration awareness, revealing consistent and interpretable failure patterns. Finally, we present a user-centric synthesis pipeline that enables scalable generation of coherent service environments, user configurations, and reliably annotated tasks, supporting benchmark extensibility and future research.

</details>


### [3] [SkillMentor: LLM Agent Self-Evolution via Learning Blind-Spot Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27360)
*Xiaoyi Bao,Yuanzhen Xie,Yunzhi Tan,Jinghang Gu,Zhongqing Wang,Chu-Ren Huang,Bo Hu,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出可学习的盲点诊断机制，训练Mentor策略生成诊断任务并转化为技能，实现agent自我演化，直接相关agent方法

TL;DR: 提出诊断能力本身可学习，通过SkillMentor学习生成诊断任务并识别失败模式，无需更新执行器或人工监督，即可提升智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化方法只关注学习行动，忽略发现未知的能力；通常假设失败发现是给定的，本研究探讨能否学习盲点诊断本身。

Method: 将诊断与执行分离，排除执行器适应和人类监督，提出SkillMentor，用强化学习训练Mentor策略来生成诊断任务、识别反复失败模式并整理为可复用纠正技能。

Result: 在AppWorld和BFCLv3上，SkillMentor平均提升执行器性能44.2%。

Conclusion: 盲点诊断是可学习的，能在不更新执行器权重或依赖人工数据的情况下实现智能体自我进化。

中文摘要：智能体自我进化主要关注学习如何行动，却忽略了一个同样重要的能力：学习发现智能体不知道什么。现有方法通常假设失败发现是给定的，专注于在识别出失败后如何进行修复。我们提出疑问：盲点诊断本身能否被学习？因此，我们将诊断作为一种独立于执行的能力来研究，并排除了两种替代的进步来源：执行器适应和人类监督。在这些约束下，性能不能通过执行器更新或标注示例来提高，迫使所有改进都源于学到的诊断能力。我们提出了SkillMentor，它通过强化学习训练一个Mentor策略，以生成诊断任务、识别反复出现的失败模式，并将其整理为可重用的纠正技能。在AppWorld和BFCLv3上，SkillMentor平均将执行器性能提升了44.2%。这些结果表明，盲点诊断是一种可学习的能力，使得在无需更新执行器权重或依赖人工整理数据的情况下实现自我进化成为可能。

Original abstract: Agent self-evolution has primarily focused on learning how to act, while overlooking an equally important capability: learning to discover what an agent does not know. Existing approaches typically assume that failure discovery is given, focusing on how to repair failures once they are identified. We ask whether blind-spot diagnosis itself can be learned. We thus study diagnosis as an agent capability separate from execution, and exclude two alternative sources of progress: executor adaptation and human supervision. Under these constraints, performance cannot improve through executor updates or annotated examples, forcing all improvements to originate from the learned diagnostic capability. We propose SkillMentor, which trains a Mentor policy via reinforcement learning to generate diagnostic tasks, identify recurrent failure modes, and curate them into reusable corrective skills. Across AppWorld and BFCLv3, SkillMentor improves executor performance by an average of 44.2%. These results suggest that blind-spot diagnosis is a learnable capability, enabling self-evolution without updating executor weights or relying on human-curated data.

</details>


### [4] [Bridging Inference-Time Scaling and Episodic Memory with Action-Centric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27415)
*Xu Zheng,Chaohao Lin,Zhuomin Chen,Weijieying Ren,Haifeng Chen,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 用动作中心图建模历史推理，结合时序差分学习为agent提供正面建议和风险规避，可迁移到任何长程agent记忆设计

TL;DR: 提出GAMER框架，将推理时扩展与情景记忆结合，通过动作中心图存储历史经验，使用双流时序差分价值函数引导决策，减少冗余搜索并提升效率，在多个基准上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时扩展方法无状态、重复搜索，而记忆机制依赖LLM推理导致成本高。需要一种与LLM解耦的高效记忆机制，既能利用历史经验又能降低计算开销。

Method: 构建动态动作中心图表示历史推理；用双流TD学习估计动作正/负价值；推理时通过图搜索双向优化决策（正建议、负规避）。

Result: 在多个基准上，相比朴素基线，成功率提升20.81%，进度率提升6.17%，且比记忆基线的token/金钱消耗更少。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模为动作中心图并解耦记忆机制，GAMER显著提高了推理扩展的效率和性能，为智能体记忆与推理结合提供了新途径。

中文摘要：推理时扩展的最新进展显著解锁了大型语言模型（LLMs）的复杂推理能力。然而，对于智能体而言，这些方法存在一个关键的效率问题：它们以无状态方式运行，并参与冗余的搜索过程。现有的记忆机制主要依赖LLMs的推理能力，导致计算成本过高。在本文中，我们提出了一种新颖的框架，名为GAMER（基于图的动作中心记忆与情景推理），它弥合了推理扩展与情景记忆之间的差距。我们的方法将历史推理建模为动态的“动作中心图”。通过将记忆机制与LLMs解耦，我们的方法相比记忆机制基线，可以提供更少的记忆上下文，从而节省token/金钱开销。为了有效提取图中的知识，我们使用双流时序差分学习机制，根据过去的成功和失败来估计动作节点的正（建议）和负（规避）价值。在推理阶段，该学习到的价值函数双向优化决策，使得正值提供动作建议，而负值指示高风险动作。通过在图上进行高效搜索，我们的方法显著提升了推理扩展的效率。在多个基准上的实验表明，与朴素基线相比，GAMER在成功率/进度率上实现了20.81%/6.17%的显著性能提升。

Original abstract: Recent advancements in inference-time scaling have significantly unlocked the complex reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models~(LLMs). However, for agents, these approaches suffer from a critical inefficiency, operating in a stateless manner and engaging in redundant search processes. Existing memory mechanisms largely rely on the reasoning capabilities of LLMs, leading to prohibitive computational costs. In this paper, we propose a novel framework, \textit{GAMER}~(Graph-based Action-centric Memory with Episodic Reasoning), that bridges the gap between inference scaling and episodic memory. Our approach models historical reasoning as a dynamic \textit{Action-Centric Graph}. By decoupling the memory mechanism from LLMs, our method can save token/money usage by providing less memory context than memory mechanism baselines. To extract knowledge from the graph effectively, we use a dual-stream Temporal Difference learning mechanism to estimate the positive~(suggestion) and negative~(avoidance) value of action nodes based on past successes and failures. During the inference phase, this learned value function optimizes decision-making bi-directionally, so that positive values provide action suggestions, while negative values indicate high-risk actions. By performing efficient searches on the graph, our method significantly improves the efficiency of inference scaling. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that \textit{GAMER} achieves superior performance by \textbf{20.81\%/6.17\%} for success/progress rate compared to vanilla baselines.

</details>


### [5] [Leveraging Trajectory Graphs for Pre-Execution Error Diagnosis in Agentic LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27443)
*Xu Zheng,Zhuomin Chen,Chaohao Lin,Hua Wei,Haifeng Chen,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 基于轨迹图神经网络预测执行前错误，提供早期预警并触发自我纠正，对长程agent可靠性有直接启发

TL;DR: 提出Trajectory Graph Copilot框架，通过图神经网络对历史轨迹建模，在动作执行前诊断潜在错误，使LLM智能体自我纠正，从而提升长期任务成功率，平均通过率提升14.69%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长期交互任务中常因动作空间巨大而出现复合错误，单一错误动作导致整个轨迹失败，且步数预算有限，现有方法依赖昂贵微调。

Method: 受软件调试中执行日志分析启发，构建历史轨迹的概率图，利用图神经网络（Graph Debugger）识别导致失败的序列动作模式，作为主动诊断沙箱在动作执行前发出早期警告，促使智能体自我纠正。

Result: 在四个基准测试和三个LLM智能体上的实验显示，平均通过率提升14.69%。

Conclusion: 动作前错误诊断能有效防止代价高昂的错误，显著增强LLM智能体在长期任务中的完成能力，无需昂贵微调。

中文摘要：基于大型语言模型（LLM）的智能体在广泛的复杂交互任务中表现出卓越的性能。然而，它们常常在具身人工智能等领域常见的长期交互任务中遇到困难。这些环境中的复杂性和巨大的动作空间会导致复合错误，其中单一次优动作可能使整个轨迹偏离轨道，导致智能体将有限的步数预算浪费在低效或不可恢复的路径上。为了在不进行昂贵微调的情况下克服这一问题，我们从软件调试中汲取灵感，在软件调试中，通过分析执行日志来预先捕获错误。我们提出了	extit{Trajectory Graph Copilot}，一种新颖的框架，充当LLM智能体的“副驾驶”，在潜在动作错误执行之前对其进行诊断。其核心是	extit{Graph Debugger}，它将历史轨迹建模为概率图，并使用图神经网络来识别经常导致失败的序列动作模式。作为主动诊断沙箱，我们的方法对潜在有缺陷的动作提供早期警告，促使智能体自我纠正。这种动作前错误诊断可防止代价高昂的错误，显著增强智能体成功完成长期任务的能力。在四个基准测试和三个LLM智能体上的广泛实验表明，平均通过率提高了$14.69\%$。

Original abstract: Large Language Model~(LLM)-based agents have demonstrated exceptional performance across a wide range of complex interactive tasks. However, they often struggle with long-horizon interactive tasks common in domains, such as embodied AI. The complexity and vast action spaces in these settings lead to compounding errors, where a single suboptimal action can derail an entire trajectory, causing the agent to exhaust its limited step budget on inefficient or unrecoverable paths. To overcome this without costly fine-tuning, we draw inspiration from software debugging, where execution logs are analyzed to preemptively catch errors. We propose \textit{Trajectory Graph Copilot}, a novel framework that acts as a ``copilot'' for LLM agents by diagnosing potential action errors before they are executed. At its core,\textit{Graph Debugger} models historical trajectories as a probabilistic graph and uses a Graph Neural Network to identify sequential action patterns that frequently lead to failure. Functioning as a proactive diagnostic sandbox, our method provides early warnings on potentially flawed actions, prompting the agent to self-correct. This pre-action error diagnosis prevents costly mistakes, significantly enhancing the agent's ability to complete long-horizon tasks successfully. The extensive experiments on four benchmarks with three LLM agents demonstrate a $14.69\%$ pass ratio improvement on average.

</details>


### [6] [VAmoS Bench: Voice Agent Simulation Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27453)
*Joshua Meyer,Sahar Shayegan,Ritiz Tambi,Ali Khan,Sun Kim,Victor Shih,Mehdi Jamei,Andi Partovi*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 语音agent端到端模拟benchmark，含真实SQL工具和断言验证，评测协议可借鉴到其他工具型agent

TL;DR: VAmoS Bench是一个用于在状态化客户支持任务中端到端评估语音代理的模拟基准，通过完整轨迹上的二元断言来衡量包含率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音代理基准主要衡量组件质量和对话属性（如词错误率、延迟、自然度、轮流发言），而较少衡量代理是否独立正确处理电话（即包含率），因此需要一个新的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: VAmoS Bench提供了100个场景，每个场景包含具有私人目标的模拟呼叫者、预填充的PostgreSQL后端、五个可执行真实SQL的工具，以及约三分之一的对抗性压力场景；每个场景定义二元断言，由评分器根据呼叫者和代理的完整对话及动作轨迹（包括工具调用、参数和返回行）进行评估。

Result: 该基准的第一个版本专注于金融服务，支持不断发展的排行榜；能够捕获代理声称执行了操作但未更新数据库，或做出正确数据库更改但泄露受保护信息等错误。

Conclusion: 通过端到端衡量包含率和正确性，VAmoS Bench为完整语音代理系统提供了新的评估范式。

中文摘要：生产级语音代理涵盖级联式、语音到语音和混合架构。语音代理基准通常衡量组件质量和对话属性，如词错误率、延迟、自然度和轮流发言。较少有基准衡量代理是否独立正确处理了电话呼叫。联络中心将此称为“包含率”：自动化系统无需转接给人工即可解决的电话呼叫比例。在某些电话呼叫中，正确的结果可能是拒绝或重定向。为弥补这一空白，我们引入了VAmoS Bench（语音代理模拟基准）。它在有状态客户支持任务中端到端地衡量完整的语音代理系统。代理是Riley，一家虚构银行的信用卡支持代表，可以冻结、取消、替换或激活信用卡。100个场景中的每一个都提供了一个带有私人目标的模拟呼叫者和一个预填充的PostgreSQL后端。该平台利用每个场景来填充和激活一个隔离的模拟，其中呼叫者通过音频联系Riley；大约三分之一的场景施加对抗性压力。代理可以使用五个工具，这些工具对后端执行真实的SQL。每个场景还定义了二元断言。评分器根据呼叫者和代理所说的内容以及代理所做的操作（包括工具调用、参数和返回的行）的完整轨迹来评估这些断言。这可以捕获声称已更改信用卡但未更新数据库的代理，以及做出正确数据库更改但泄露受保护信息的代理。这第一个基准版本专注于金融服务。其评估协议支持不断发展的排行榜：可以在同一版本上评估额外的语音代理，而后续版本可以扩展任务和场景。

Original abstract: Production voice agents span cascaded, speech-to-speech, and hybrid architectures. Voice-agent benchmarks typically measure component quality and conversational properties such as word error rate, latency, naturalness, and turn-taking. Fewer measure whether the agent handled a phone call correctly on its own. Contact centers refer to this as ``containment'': the share of phone calls the automated system resolves without handing off to a human. On some phone calls the right outcome is refusal or a redirect. To address this gap, we introduce VAmoS Bench, the Voice Agent Simulation Bench. It measures complete voice-agent systems end to end in a stateful customer-support task. The agent is Riley, a credit-card support representative for a fictional bank who can freeze, cancel, replace, or activate a card. Each of 100 scenarios supplies a simulated caller with a private goal and a seeded PostgreSQL backend. The platform uses each scenario to populate and activate an isolated simulation in which the caller reaches Riley over audio; roughly one-third apply adversarial pressure. The agent can use five tools that execute real SQL against the backend. Each scenario also defines binary assertions. A grader evaluates them against the complete trace of what the caller and agent said and what the agent did, including tool invocations, arguments, and returned rows. This catches an agent that claims to have changed a card without updating the database, as well as one that makes the right database change while disclosing protected information. This first benchmark version focuses on financial services. Its evaluation protocol supports an evolving leaderboard: additional voice agents can be evaluated on the same version, while later versions can expand the tasks and scenarios.

</details>


### [7] [Skill Use or Skill Theater? Evaluating the Reasoning Backroom in Skill-Augmented Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27484)
*Jinwei Hu,Yi Qi,Xinmiao Huang,Youcheng Sun,Yi Dong,Xiaowei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 通过反事实干预评估技能增强agent中声明使用与实际因果依赖的差距，对agent评测和技能审计有重要方法论价值

TL;DR: 提出BACKTRACE框架和BACKROOMBench基准，发现技能增强代理存在声称使用与因果影响之间的系统性偏差，即“推理后台”，并证明观察性检测器无法识别真实依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估通常依赖可见推理或代理自我归因来判断技能使用，但这些信号不能反映技能是否真正改变了决策，导致来源归属不可靠。

Method: 设计BACKTRACE评估框架，对每个技能条件答案配对无技能反事实，干预技能含义、措辞、身份、内容和分配，并在答案生成后收集归因；实例化为BACKROOMBench测试床，覆盖多领域、多条件、单/多代理及多模型。

Result: 发现普遍来源失败：声称使用稳定但因果依赖变化，存在静默吸收和表演性使用；行为受程序内容影响而非技能身份；观察性检测器无法识别真实依赖；多代理中技能影响可脱离来源存活。

Conclusion: 推理后台是AI来源追踪中的普遍问题，审计此类系统必须采用干预式方法，而非仅依赖观察性信号。

中文摘要：可复用技能正成为扩展语言代理任务流程的标准接口。然而，评估者通常从可见推理或代理自身的归因来推断技能的使用。这些信号仅显示代理表面使用了什么，而非技能是否改变了其决策。我们提出一个问题：技能增强代理是否表现出一种“推理后台”（Reasoning Backroom），即声称的技能使用与干预测量的影响之间存在系统性差距。我们引入了BACKTRACE，一个评估框架，它将每个技能条件下的回答与匹配的无技能反事实配对，对技能的含义、措辞、身份、内容和分配进行干预，并且仅在答案提交后才引发归因。我们将该框架实例化为BACKROOMBench，一个经过验证的测试平台，涵盖受控逻辑和竞赛数学、多种技能条件、单代理和多代理设置以及多样化的模型家族。我们的评估揭示了普遍存在的来源失败。跨模型和领域，声称的技能使用通常保持稳定，而因果依赖和符号效用则发生变化，产生既有静默吸收也有表演性使用。行为效应更可靠地跟随程序性内容而非显示的技能身份，而陈述的归因则强烈响应工件的可用性。基于直接技能使用声明、文本提及、轨迹相似性和LLM评判者的观察性检测器无法识别哪些决策实际上依赖于技能。在多代理系统中，技能影响即使在来源丢失后仍可在通信中存活，而无技能团队仍会提及从未提供的技能和来源。这些发现确立了“推理后台”作为一个普遍的AI来源问题，其审计需要干预。

Original abstract: Reusable skills are becoming a standard interface for extending language agents with task procedures. Yet evaluators usually infer skill use from visible reasoning or the agent's own attribution. These signals show what the agent appears to use, not whether the skill changed its decision. We ask whether skill-augmented agents exhibit a \textbf{Reasoning Backroom}, a systematic gap between stated skill use and intervention-measured influence. We introduce BACKTRACE, an evaluation framework that pairs each skill-conditioned answer with a matched no-skill counterfactual, intervenes on skill meaning, wording, identity, content, and assignment, and elicits attribution only after the answer is committed. We instantiate the framework as BACKROOMBench, a verified testbed spanning controlled logic and competition mathematics, multiple skill conditions, single-agent and multi-agent settings, and diverse model families. Our evaluation reveals a pervasive provenance failure. Across models and domains, stated skill use often remains stable while causal reliance and signed utility vary, producing both silent uptake and performative use. Behavioral effects follow procedural content more reliably than displayed skill identity, whereas stated attributions respond strongly to artifact availability. Observational detectors based on direct skill-use claims, text mentions, trace similarity, and an LLM judge do not identify which decisions actually depend on the skill. In multi-agent systems, skill influence can survive communication even after its source is lost, while no-skill teams still name skills and sources that were never supplied. These findings establish the Reasoning Backroom as a general AI provenance problem whose audit requires intervention.

</details>


### [8] [Automated Transcript Analysis for Detecting Flaws in Agentic Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27518)
*Jeff Mohl,Nelson Gardner-Challis,Magda Dubois,Harry Coppock,Benjamin Allan-Rahill,Kaelan Yim,Damian Sójka,James Mann,Justin Olive*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 自动化扫描agentic benchmark转录本以检测有效性缺陷，直接提升agent评测的可靠性与审计可扩展性

TL;DR: 本文开发了AI扫描器来自动检测智能体基准测试中的四类有效性问题，并在多个基准上验证了其可行性，同时揭示了性能差异和开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动审计基准测试（如SWE-Bench-Verified）虽能发现有效性问题，但难以扩展，且自动化方法能否可靠识别这些缺陷尚不明确。

Method: 开发AI扫描器检测四类问题：真实答案获取、工具失败、猜测脆弱性、答案格式模糊性；为每类制定评分标准，并在Inspect Evals基准的保留测试集上对比人工标签进行评估。

Result: 扫描器在五个广泛使用的基准中发现若干已证实的质量问题，包括人工难以随机发现的情况；但并非全部被识别，且性能在不同基准、标准和模型间差异大。

Conclusion: 结果证明自动化转录分析可扩展用于基准质量审计，但需解决标准化差距等挑战以提升扫描器的可靠性。

中文摘要：前沿模型的能力通常通过智能体基准测试来评估。要信任这些结果，基准测试必须准确衡量其声称的目标，并且不能存在使结果无效的缺陷。此前对SWE-Bench-Verified等基准测试的手动审计在转录中发现了若干有效性问题。然而，手动审查难以扩展，且不清楚自动化方法能否可靠地发现损害基准有效性的缺陷。在本文中，我们开发了AI扫描器来检测四类有效性问题：真实答案获取、工具失败、猜测脆弱性和答案格式模糊性。我们为每一类制定了评分标准以指导人工标注，并在Inspect Evals基准的保留测试集上评估了扫描器与人工标签的一致性。我们的扫描器在五个广泛使用的基准测试中识别出若干经证实的质量问题，包括一些随机人工检查难以发现的情况。并非所有案例都被识别出来，且扫描器的性能在不同基准、标准和模型之间差异显著。我们指出了若干待解决的开放挑战，以改进扫描器，从而支持更强的质量保证主张，包括评估领域中导致扫描器性能下降的更广泛标准化差距。综合来看，这些结果证明了使用自动化转录分析来更广泛地审计基准质量的概念可行性。

Original abstract: Capabilities of frontier models are often assessed using agentic benchmarks. To trust these results, benchmarks must accurately measure what they claim to and be free from invalidating flaws. Previous manual audits of benchmarks such as SWE-Bench-Verified have uncovered several validity issues in transcripts. However, manual review is difficult to scale, and it is unclear whether automated methods can reliably surface flaws that compromise benchmark validity. In this paper, we developed AI scanners to detect four types of validity issues: ground truth access, tool failure, guessing vulnerability, and answer format ambiguity. We produced grading rubrics for each to instruct human labeling, and evaluated the scanners against human labels on a held-out test set of Inspect Evals benchmarks. Our scanners identified several verified quality issues in five widely used benchmarks, including cases unlikely to be caught by random manual inspection. Not all cases were identified, and scanner performance varied substantially across benchmarks, criteria and models. We highlight several open challenges to be addressed to improve scanners for stronger quality assurance claims, including broader standardization gaps in the evaluation field that degrade scanner performance. Together, these results serve as a proof of concept for using automated transcript analysis to audit benchmark quality more broadly.

</details>


### [9] [Evaluating Agentic Bioinformatics through Function, Evidence, and Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27556)
*Phuc Pham,Truong-Son Hy*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出Function-Evidence-Validation框架评估agent生成工作流的可审计性与可复现性，可迁移至coding agent评测。

TL;DR: 提出FEV框架，以工作流轨迹为分析单元，评估109个生物信息学代理系统，发现规划与执行进展快于可重放性、验证等，主张以工作流正确性评估代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述按应用、架构等组织生物学代理，但未操作化代理生成工作流的可问责性；流畅响应和基准性能不足以证明科学可信度。

Method: 引入功能-证据-验证（FEV）框架，区分演示操作、可追溯支持和用例验证，并映射109个代理系统及28个基准资源，覆盖128篇出版物。

Result: 发现规划和工具介导执行比可重放性、来源、科学评估、外部验证和前瞻性测试进展更快，跨领域普遍存在此差距。

Conclusion: 代理生物信息学应通过工作流正确性而非仅最终答案来评估，FEV为比较系统和设计透明、可审计、科学可信的工作流提供实用基础。

中文摘要：大规模语言模型代理越来越多地规划、执行和解释生物学分析，但流畅的响应、成功的工具调用和基准性能本身并不能建立科学可信度。现有综述主要按应用、架构和代理能力来组织生物学代理，但并未共同操作化代理生成工作流的可问责性。我们通过将可检查的工作流轨迹（而非架构或最终输出单独）作为主要分析单元来解决这一差距。我们引入了功能-证据-验证（FEV）框架，该框架区分了演示的工作流操作、行动和声明的可追溯支持以及特定于用例的验证。使用FEV，我们绘制了109个代理或代理邻近系统以及28个基准或评估资源，代表基因组学、单细胞和空间组学、蛋白质科学、药物发现、计算病理学和一般生物信息学自动化中的128篇独特出版物。跨领域，规划和工具介导的执行进展速度快于可重放性、来源、稳健的科学评估、外部验证和前瞻性实证测试。因此，我们主张代理生物信息学应通过工作流正确性而非仅最终答案正确性来评估。FEV为比较系统和设计透明、可审计且科学可信的生物信息学工作流提供了实用基础。

Original abstract: Large language model agents increasingly plan, execute, and interpret biological analyses, yet fluent responses, successful tool calls, and benchmark performance alone do not establish scientific credibility. Existing reviews primarily organize biological agents by application, architecture, and agentic capability, but do not jointly operationalize the accountability of agent-generated workflows. We address this gap by treating the inspectable workflow trajectory, rather than architecture or final output alone, as the primary unit of analysis. We introduce the Function--Evidence--Validation (FEV) framework, which separates demonstrated workflow operations, traceable support for actions and claims, and use-case-specific validation. Using FEV, we map 109 agentic or agent-adjacent systems and 28 benchmark or evaluation resources, representing 128 unique publications across genomics, single-cell and spatial omics, protein science, drug discovery, computational pathology, and general bioinformatics automation. Across domains, planning and tool-mediated execution have advanced more rapidly than replayability, provenance, robust scientific assessment, external validation, and prospective empirical testing. We therefore argue that agentic bioinformatics should be assessed through workflow correctness rather than final-answer correctness alone. FEV provides a practical basis for comparing systems and designing transparent, auditable, and scientifically accountable bioinformatics workflows.

</details>


### [10] [DeepResearch Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27562)
*Yong Huang,Yulu Huang,for the team Collaboration*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 完整的通用深度研究agent系统，涵盖多步规划、工具协调、RL优化与数据合成，对agent harness设计有参考价值。

TL;DR: DeepResearch Agent是一个基于稀疏激活架构的30B参数大模型系统，每token仅激活3B参数，推理速度快3.2倍，在多个智能体基准上达到SOTA，并开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在深度信息检索、多步推理和自主研究任务上表现不足，且密集模型推理开销大。

Method: 采用稀疏激活架构、128K上下文层次注意力、ReAct与IterResearch双模式推理、多工具协调、GRPO强化学习优化、自动化数据合成流程。

Result: 在多个基准上达到SOTA，如Humanity's Last Exam 87.3%，BrowserComp中文85.3%，WebWalkerQA 91.2%，工具使用准确率92.1%，推理速度提升3.2倍。

Conclusion: DeepResearch Agent通过稀疏激活和多种优化机制实现了高效、高精度的自主研究能力，并完全开源，可支持多种应用场景。

中文摘要：DeepResearch Agent 系统是一个大型语言模型系统，专为深度信息检索、多步推理和自主研究任务而设计。该系统基于稀疏激活架构，总参数为300亿，每个token仅激活30亿参数，在多个智能体搜索基准上实现了最先进的性能，同时与同等规模的传统密集模型相比，推理速度提高了3.2倍。系统支持128K token的上下文窗口，并采用分层注意力机制，在标准长上下文方法的基础上，准确率提升了18.7%，召回率提升了23.4%。双模式推理引擎提供用于基本多步问题求解的ReAct范式和用于高性能迭代研究的IterResearch模式（最多支持20个推理步骤），共同在单次传递基线之上实现了31.2%的准确率提升。多工具协调集成了检索、计算、网络搜索和文件解析模块，工具使用准确率达到92.1%。基于GRPO算法的强化学习优化框架提供token级策略梯度，将训练稳定性提高了35%，并将收敛速度加快了42%。具有基于种子扩展的自动化数据合成流程，数据可用率达到92.5%。基准测试结果包括：在Humanity's Last Exam上达到87.3%，在BrowserComp中文上达到85.3%，在WebWalkerQA上达到91.2%。该系统完全开源，包括数据合成、训练和推理代码，并支持学术研究、商业分析、研发支持和教育等应用。

Original abstract: The DeepResearch Agent System is a large language model system engineered for deep information retrieval, multi-step reasoning, and autonomous research tasks. Built upon a sparse activation architecture with 30 billion total parameters of which only 3 billion are activated per token, the system achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple agent search benchmarks while delivering 3.2 times faster inference compared to dense counterparts of equivalent scale. The system supports a 128K-token context window with hierarchical attention mechanisms that yield 18.7% accuracy and 23.4% recall improvements over standard long-context approaches. A dual-mode reasoning engine provides both a ReAct paradigm for basic multi-step problem solving and an IterResearch mode for high-performance iterative research with up to 20 reasoning steps, collectively delivering a 31.2% accuracy improvement over single-pass baselines. Multi-tool coordination integrates retrieval, computation, web search, and file parsing modules to achieve 92.1% tool-use accuracy. A reinforcement learning optimization framework based on the GRPO algorithm provides token-level policy gradients that improve training stability by 35% and accelerate convergence by 42%. An automated data synthesis pipeline with seed-based expansion achieves a 92.5% usability rate. Benchmark results include 87.3% on Humanity's Last Exam, 85.3% on BrowserComp Chinese, and 91.2% on WebWalkerQA. The system is fully open-sourced, including data synthesis, training, and inference code, and supports applications in academic research, business analysis, R&D support, and education.

</details>


### [11] [What makes prompts a graph: necessary and sufficient conditions for prompt graph engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27578)
*Sandeco Macedo*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 为prompt graph工程给出操作化定义，厘清了agent工作流图结构的边界，对agent harness抽象有启发。

TL;DR: 本文提出了“提示图工程”的操作化定义，将提示词视为显式、可执行、可改进图中的节点，并给出了包含/排除测试及六个真实系统的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实系统已广泛使用图结构组织提示词，但缺乏统一的参考定义和词汇，阻碍了精确沟通与研究。

Method: 通过概念分析和灰色文献重构思想谱系，提出四个条件，并将其操作化为包含/排除测试，应用到六个系统。

Result: 该测试在六个系统（LangGraph, DSPy, Prompt Flow, AutoGen, CrewAI, Claude Code子代理）上一致地包含和排除，验证了定义的适用性。

Conclusion: 贡献在于为日常实践提供操作化定义和共享词汇，并沿四个设计张力轴提出研究议程。

中文摘要：提示词早已不再只是孤立的字符串。在真实系统中，一次模型调用会馈送给另一次调用，检索与生成交替进行，路由器进行分支，聚合器合并并行结果。实践已经收敛到一种单一结构来将其整合：图。LangGraph、DSPy 和 Prompt Flow 等框架公开地展示了这一结构，研究系统也已自动对其进行优化。然而，词汇表却落后了。图被不同地命名为单次采样策略内部的推理拓扑、多智能体对话或编排工件，而提示工程仍让人联想到编写一个好的字符串。所缺少的是一个参考定义，将提示词视为显式、可执行、可改进的图中的节点。我们通过对具有持久标识符的来源进行概念分析，并辅以主要的灰色文献来构建该定义。我们重构了这一思想的谱系，从数据流图和构建系统，经过提示链接和思维拓扑（链、树、图），到被编译和优化为工件的图。然后，我们提出了提示图工程的定义性定义，陈述其四个条件（显式结构、结构与提示内容分离、可执行语义、图作为一等工程工件），并将其操作化为包含与排除测试。我们与六个邻近概念划清界限，并将测试应用于六个真实系统（LangGraph、DSPy、Prompt Flow、AutoGen、CrewAI 和 Claude Code 子代理）；它一致地包含和排除。我们最后提出了一个沿四个设计张力轴组织的研究议程。贡献在于提供了一个操作化定义和共享词汇表，用于一种行业每天已在实践但尚未精确命名的实践。

Original abstract: Prompts stopped being isolated strings some time ago. In real systems, one model call feeds another, retrieval interleaves with generation, routers branch, and aggregators merge parallel results. Practice converged on a single structure to hold this together: the graph. Frameworks such as LangGraph, DSPy, and Prompt Flow expose it openly, and research systems already optimize it automatically. The vocabulary, however, lags behind. Graph names, variously, a reasoning topology inside one sampling strategy, a multi-agent conversation, or an orchestration artifact, while prompt engineering still evokes writing one good string. What is missing is a reference definition treating prompts as nodes of an explicit, executable, improvable graph. We build that definition through conceptual analysis over sources with persistent identifiers, complemented by primary grey literature. We reconstruct the genealogy of the idea, from dataflow graphs and build systems, through prompt chaining and the thought topologies (chain, tree, graph), to graphs compiled and optimized as artifacts. We then propose a constitutive definition of prompt graph engineering, state its four conditions (explicit structure, separation between structure and prompt content, executable semantics, and the graph as a first-class engineering artifact), and operationalize them as an inclusion and exclusion test. We draw the boundary against six neighboring concepts and apply the test to six real systems (LangGraph, DSPy, Prompt Flow, AutoGen, CrewAI, and Claude Code subagents); it includes and excludes consistently. We close with a research agenda organized along four design tension axes. The contribution is an operational definition and a shared vocabulary for a practice that industry already exercises daily without naming precisely.

</details>


### [12] [HALO: Heterogeneous Admission through Localized Obligations for Safe Agentic Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27636)
*Taewoo Park,Kyeonghyun Yoo,Kiseok Kim,Seunghyun Yoo,Hwangnam Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: HALO是一种运行时协议，通过保留仍受支持的组件并重新检查操作，避免了因部分组件过时而拒绝整个响应的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的响应处理要么整体拒绝导致浪费，要么独立检查导致依赖关系缺失，因此需要一种细粒度的准入协议。

Method: HALO协议根据组件的声明先决条件保留受支持组件，在调度前重新检查每个操作，并仅允许用新候选替换被阻止的操作。

Result: HALO在96项准入期望和20项协议测试中全部通过；在结构化重放中保留了248/248个组件（其中128/128个不受无关变化影响），而整体策略保留0个；在PX4/Gazebo冷启动中阻止了所有过时路线且完成所有新恢复。

Conclusion: HALO提供了一种有效的运行时准入机制，在动态环境中避免丢弃可用组件，确保依赖完整性和操作新鲜度。

中文摘要：近期的智能体AI系统可能返回包含通知、请求、交接和操作的异构响应。在外部使用之前条件可能发生变化，因此来自同一响应的组件不必保持同时受支持。拒绝整个响应会丢弃有用的组件，而独立检查组件可能使依赖者缺少其先决条件。我们提出了带有局部义务的异构准入（HALO），一种运行时协议，它保留那些声明的先决条件也仍然受支持的受支持组件，在调度前重新检查每个具体操作，并允许被阻止的操作仅由新的候选者替换。HALO匹配了所有96项准入期望，并通过了全部20项协议测试。在结构化响应重放中，它保留了248/248个受支持的组件，包括128/128个不受无关变化影响的组件，而整个响应策略保留了0/248。在十次冷启动PX4/Gazebo会话中，HALO阻止了每个测试的过时路线，未观察到匹配的过时设定点，并完成了所有新的恢复。

Original abstract: Recent agentic AI systems may return a heterogeneous response containing notices, requests, handoffs, and actions. Conditions can change before external use, so components from the same response need not remain supported together. Rejecting the whole response discards useful components, whereas checking components independently can leave a dependent without its prerequisite. We present Heterogeneous Admission with Localized Obligations (HALO), a runtime protocol that preserves supported components whose declared prerequisites also remain supported, rechecks each exact action before dispatch, and allows blocked actions to be replaced only by fresh candidates. HALO matched all 96 admission expectations and passed all 20 protocol tests. In structured-response replay, it retained 248/248 supported components, including 128/128 unaffected by unrelated changes, while a whole-response policy retained 0/248. Across ten cold-start PX4/Gazebo sessions, HALO blocked every tested stale route, observed no matching stale setpoint, and completed all fresh recoveries.

</details>


### [13] [Old Tricks, New Models: How Simple Image Transformations Break Modern AI-based Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28187)
*Marco Alecci,Francesco Marchiori,Iyiola Emmanuel Olatunji,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé,Jacques Klein*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文对三个商业图像审核API进行了大规模黑盒评估，发现简单的图像转换即可绕过它们，表明基础模型审核服务并非稳健的安全边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于传统审核分类器覆盖有限，而商业基础模型审核API声称更强大，但尚不清楚这种转变是否能提升图像审核的稳健性，因此需要实证检验。

Method: 对三个商业图像审核服务进行黑盒评估，使用七种模型无关图像转换，跨多个提供商、数据集、伤害类别、感知相似性约束和转换强度进行测试。

Result: 三个服务均可被低成本转换绕过；固定转换如颜色反转和灰度转换能造成不安全到安全的判定改变但内容仍可被人类识别；稳健性因数据集和伤害类别而异，多模态内容和自残漏洞显著。

Conclusion: 用基础模型API替代传统分类器不能本身提供可靠安全边界，必须在现实转换下评估，并作为分层审核管道的一部分而非独立过滤器部署。

中文摘要：虽然自动化内容审核系统已成为大规模筛选有害内容的关键工具，但传统的任务特定分类器往往提供有限的政策覆盖范围和上下文理解。最近，基于大型基础模型构建的商业多模态审核API被引入，承诺提供更广泛、更强大的安全过滤器。在这项工作中，我们分析了这种转变是否也带来了更稳健的图像审核。我们对三个成熟的商业图像审核服务进行了大规模黑盒评估，并比较了它们的稳健性。通过跨多个提供商、数据集、伤害类别、感知相似性约束和转换强度评估七种简单、模型无关的图像转换，我们发现：（1）所有三个商业服务都可以通过廉价的图像转换绕过，这些转换不需要梯度、代理模型或对目标系统的了解；（2）即使是固定的转换，如颜色反转和灰度转换，也会导致不安全到安全的决策改变，同时保留人类仍可识别的内容；（3）它们的稳健性在不同数据集和伤害类别之间差异很大，其中多模态内容和自残表现出明显的脆弱性。这得出的结论是，用基于基础模型的API替代传统审核分类器本身并不能提供可靠的安全边界。此类系统必须在现实转换下进行评估，并作为分层审核管道的一个组成部分部署，而不是作为独立的安全过滤器。

Original abstract: While automated content-moderation systems have become essential for screening harmful content at scale, conventional task-specific classifiers often provide limited policy cov- erage and contextual understanding. Recently, commercial multimodal moderation APIs built on large foundation models have been introduced with the promise of providing broader and more capable safety filters. In this work, we analyze whether this shift also yields more robust image moderation. We conduct a large-scale black-box evaluation on three established commercial image-moderation services and compare their robustness. By evaluating seven simple, model-agnostic image transformations across multiple providers, datasets, harm categories, perceptual-similarity constraints, and transformation intensities, we find that: (1) all three commercial services can be bypassed using inexpensive image transformations that require no gradients, surrogate models, or knowledge of the target system; (2) even fixed transformations such as color inversion and grayscale conversion induce unsafe-to-safe decision changes while preserving content that remains recognizable to humans; (3) their robustness varies substantially across datasets and harm categories, with multimodal content and self-harm exhibiting pronounced vulnerabilities. This yields the conclusion that replacing conventional moderation classifiers with foundation-model-based APIs does not, by itself, provide a reliable security boundary. Such systems must be evaluated under realistic transformations and deployed as one component of a layered moderation pipeline rather than as standalone safety filters.

</details>


### [14] [World Action Planner: Generalizable Decision-Making with Action-Conditioned World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27599)
*Xiangcheng Zhang,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出以世界模型想象展开驱动规划搜索与迭代精炼的通用决策框架，可迁移至长程agent规划。

TL;DR: 提出World Action Planner，结合VLM推理和世界模型物理基础，通过优化与搜索迭代规划，在组合任务和零样本泛化上显著超越VLA、WAM等端到端策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习策略在特定训练环境中有效，但难以泛化到新场景和任务，需要更通用的机器人规划方法。

Method: 利用VLM的推理能力生成初始行动计划，并基于多任务姿态-图像条件世界模型进行想象推演，通过优化和搜索迭代改进计划。

Result: 在组合任务、新布局和零样本泛化场景中取得优越性能，显著超过VLA和WAM等最先进端到端策略模型。

Conclusion: 验证了结合VLM推理与世界模型物理基础的规划系统能显著提升机器人的泛化能力，为通用智能体构建提供了有效途径。

中文摘要：构建适用于多种应用的通用智能体仍然是一个基本挑战。虽然基于模仿学习的策略在特定训练环境中表现出色，但它们通常无法泛化到新的场景和任务。在这项工作中，我们提出了World Action Planner，一个机器人规划系统，它利用视觉语言模型（VLMs）的推理能力和多任务姿态-图像条件世界模型的物理基础。我们的系统使智能体能够提出初始行动计划，并通过优化和搜索在想象的世界模型 rollout 上进行推理，迭代地完善这些计划。我们证明了我们的方法在组合任务、新布局和零样本泛化场景中取得了优越的性能，显著优于最先进的端到端策略模型（如VLA和WAM）。项目网站：worldactionplanner.github.io

Original abstract: Building generalizable agents for diverse applications remains a fundamental challenge. While imitation learning-based policies succeed in specific training environments, they often fail to generalize to novel scenes and tasks. In this work, we propose World Action Planner, a robot planning system that leverages the reasoning capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and the physical grounding of a multi-task pose-image conditioned world model. Our system enables an agent to propose initial action plans and iteratively refine them via optimization and search, reasoning over imagined world model rollouts. We demonstrate that our approach achieves superior performance across compositional tasks, new layouts, and zero-shot generalization scenarios, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art end-to-end policy models such as VLAs and WAMs. Project website at worldactionplanner.github.io

</details>


### [15] [Rehearse: Stepping Back from the Confidence Cliff in Self-Improving Autoresearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27687)
*Jiazhen Ji,Shouhong Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 直接研究改进代码的autoresearch agent在执行前判断可靠性的置信度悬崖，提出Rehearse技能显著提升自改进效率。

TL;DR: Rehearse通过提出多个想法并在执行前比较，以及使用集中的结果记忆，缓解了自动研究中后期选择性准确率下降的'置信度悬崖'问题，在相同训练预算下提升了最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动研究循环中，智能体在执行前判断修改是否有效的可靠性随迭代下降，尤其是在成功修改积累后，选择性准确率从82.8%降至56.9%，导致效率降低，亟需改善后期判断能力。

Method: Rehearse是一种轻量级技能，其流程为：提出多个想法、在执行前比较它们、运行最有希望的方案，并利用对类似过去尝试和结果的集中记忆进行判断。

Result: 在296对同基线修改上，LLM法官在严格共识时准确率达79.5%；后期选择性准确率从82.8%降至56.9%，而Rehearse将后期选择性准确率提升至83.5%，并在4,000次预算训练运行中改善了nanochat、图像分类和时间序列预测的端点指标。

Conclusion: 通过集中的结果记忆和先比较后执行的策略，可以显著提升自动研究循环在后期阶段的判断准确性，从而在有限训练预算下获得更好的最终性能。

中文摘要：自动研究通过提出修改、运行完整训练任务并保留能够改进指标的修改来改进机器学习代码。该循环的效率不仅取决于生成想法，还取决于智能体在花费一次训练运行之前判断提议的修改是否可能有效的能力。我们研究了在执行前判断的可靠性如何在自动研究轨迹中变化。在公开的AutoSOTA日志（Li等，2026；清华大学FIB实验室，2026）中，有帮助的修改比例从前两次迭代的70%下降到第6次及以后的43%。在来自39个基于论文的AutoSOTA任务的296个同基线修改对中，每个修改对包含一个改进了指标的修改和一个未改进的修改，且测量结果被隐藏，一个LLM法官在给定候选理由但没有先前尝试历史的情况下，在严格共识返回裁决的修改对上达到79.5%的准确率。然而，在完整的366对基准上，这种能力在循环后期显著减弱。随着成功修改的积累，选择性准确率——即严格共识裁决条件下的准确率——从82.8%下降到56.9%，而法官仍然愿意做出决定。我们将这种操作模式称为置信度悬崖。Rehearse将循环变更实现为自动研究循环的一种轻量级技能：提出几个想法，在执行前进行比较，运行最有希望的，并用对类似过去尝试和结果的集中记忆进行判断。这种集中的结果记忆将后期选择性准确率提高到83.5%。在三个循环中的4,000次预算训练运行中，Rehearse在相同的训练运行预算下改善了nanochat、图像分类和时间序列预测的端点指标。

Original abstract: Autoresearch improves machine-learning code by proposing changes, running full training jobs, and keeping changes that improve the metric. The efficiency of this loop depends not only on generating ideas, but also on the agent's ability to decide, before spending a training run, whether a proposed modification is likely to work. We study how the reliability of this pre-execution judgment changes over the course of an autoresearch trajectory. In public AutoSOTA logs (Li et al., 2026; Tsinghua FIB Lab, 2026), the fraction of helpful modifications falls from 70% in the first two iterations to 43% by iteration 6+. On 296 same-baseline modification pairs from 39 paper-derived AutoSOTA tasks, each containing one modification that improved the metric and one that did not, with measured outcomes hidden, an LLM judge given candidate rationales but no prior-attempt history reaches 79.5% accuracy on the pairs where strict consensus returns a verdict. On the full 366-pair benchmark, however, this ability weakens substantially late in the loop. As successful changes accumulate, selective accuracy - accuracy conditioned on a strict-consensus verdict - falls from 82.8% to 56.9%, while the judge remains willing to decide. We call this operational pattern the confidence cliff. Rehearse implements the loop change as a lightweight skill for autoresearch loops: propose several ideas, compare them before execution, run the most promising, and judge with a focused memory of similar past attempts and outcomes. This focused outcome memory raises late selective accuracy to 83.5%. Across 4,000 budgeted training runs over three loops, Rehearse improves the endpoint under the same training-run budget on nanochat, image classification, and time-series forecasting.

</details>


### [16] [Guiding Large Language Models with Genetic Programming-Evolved Heuristic Knowledge for Dynamic Multi-Mode Project Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27698)
*Yuan Tian,Yi Mei,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 用GP进化的启发式知识以不同表示注入指导LLM在线决策，其知识传递与稳定性分析可迁移到heuristic-guided优化agent。

TL;DR: 本文提出从高质量遗传规划规则中提取知识，通过四种注入机制引导大语言模型进行在线项目调度决策，评估表明该指导能提升性能、稳定性和词元效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本LLM决策缺乏领域知识、消耗大量词元且结果不稳定，而GP演化规则包含调度知识；现有LLM-GP混合方法方向相反，因此本文反向利用GP知识引导LLM。

Method: 提取高质量GP规则的知识，通过特征选择、特征提示、规则引用、规则遵循四种方式注入LLM，在动态多模式项目调度中评估。

Result: GP指导通常优于无指导LLM；简化上下文或明确逻辑比突出特征更有效；特征选择词元效率最高，规则遵循性能强但成本高；指导提升决策稳定性并改变理由中的特征关注。

Conclusion: GP知识可以有效引导LLM决策，表示形式至关重要，未来可探索更优的知识注入方式以平衡性能与成本。

中文摘要：在动态多模式项目调度中，活动具有可替代的执行模式和不确定的持续时间，而优先关系和有限资源约束了它们的执行。启发式优先规则支持快速的在线决策，但其设计需要大量的领域专业知识。遗传规划（GP）超启发式方法可以自动演化此类规则。与此同时，大语言模型（LLM）提供了用于解释调度信息和解释决策的灵活接口。然而，零样本LLM决策可能缺乏领域知识，消耗大量词元，并且在重复查询中表现不稳定。因此，GP演化规则为引导LLM决策提供了潜在的调度知识来源。与现有使用LLM支持启发式演化的LLM-GP混合方法不同，我们反向转移知识，利用从高质量GP规则中提取的知识来引导在线LLM决策者。我们从高质量GP规则中提取知识，并通过特征选择、特征提示、规则引用和规则遵循来注入这些知识。我们根据调度性能、词元消耗、决策稳定性以及生成的理由中表达的特征关注度来评估这些机制。GP衍生的指导通常能改善无指导的LLM，但其表示形式很重要。简化决策上下文或提供明确的决策逻辑比突出重要特征更有效。特征选择提供了最佳的词元效率，而规则遵循以更高的词元成本实现了强大的性能。指导还提高了决策稳定性，并改变了生成的理由中表达的特征。

Original abstract: In dynamic multi-mode project scheduling, activities have alternative execution modes and uncertain durations, while precedence relations and limited resources constrain their execution. Heuristic priority rules support fast online decisions, but their design requires substantial domain expertise. Genetic programming (GP) hyper-heuristics can automatically evolve such rules. Large language models (LLMs), meanwhile, provide a flexible interface for interpreting scheduling information and explaining decisions. However, zero-shot LLM decisions may lack domain knowledge, consume many tokens, and vary across repeated queries. GP-evolved rules therefore provide a potential source of scheduling knowledge for guiding LLM decisions. Unlike existing LLM--GP hybrids that use LLMs to support heuristic evolution, we transfer knowledge in the reverse direction, using knowledge extracted from high-quality GP rules to guide an online LLM decision maker. We extract knowledge from high-quality GP rules and inject it through Feature Selection, Feature Hint, Rule Reference, and Rule Follow. These mechanisms are evaluated in terms of scheduling performance, token consumption, decision stability, and the feature focus expressed in generated rationales. GP-derived guidance generally improves the unguided LLM, but its representation matters. Simplifying the decision context or supplying explicit decision logic is more effective than highlighting important features. Feature Selection offers the best token efficiency, whereas Rule Follow achieves strong performance at greater token cost. Guidance also improves decision stability and changes the features expressed in generated rationales.

</details>


### [17] [SpatialCLI: Learning to Reason With Spatial Tools, Then Without Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27703)
*Yang Zhou,Zixuan Huang,Sunzhu Li,Zhuo Yang,Chen Zhang,Shunian Chen,Caijun Yan,Jianyao Xu,Shunyu Liu,Weijie Fu,Peiliang Li,Xiaozhi Chen,Yuxiang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 三阶段框架教VLM使用空间工具并逐步内化能力，工具学习与内化范式可迁移到coding agent的技能获取。

TL;DR: 提出SpatialCLI框架，通过调用空间工具、学习和内化三个阶段，使VLM获得专家级感知能力，在MindCube上大幅提升性能，并引入SpatialCLI-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用VLM在具身任务中能理解整体任务但忽略关键视觉细节，而专业视觉模型能捕捉细节却无法进行任务级决策，存在能力不匹配。

Method: SpatialCLI三阶段：Call（暴露专家视觉模型作为空间工具）、Learn（冷启动SFT和代理RL优化工具使用）、Internalize（将成功工具轨迹语言化以内化感知能力）。

Result: 在MindCube上，Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct从29.3%提升至使用工具时84.6%，超过GPT-5.6 Sol（72.1%）；内化后无工具时仍达73.8%。

Conclusion: SpatialCLI能有效弥合VLM与专家视觉模型之间的能力差距，通过工具调用与内化显著提升具身代理的组合感知和任务决策能力。

中文摘要：视觉语言模型（VLMs）越来越多地被用于具身代理中，以解释视觉输入、推理空间关系，并基于该推理做出任务级决策。然而，仍然存在一个基本的能力不匹配：通用VLM能够推理整体任务，但常常遗漏决定成功的关键视觉细节，而专业视觉模型能够捕捉这些细节，却无法将其转化为任务级决策。在这项工作中，我们提出了SpatialCLI，一个教导VLM使用空间工具并逐步内化它们所提供的专家感知能力的框架。SpatialCLI分三个阶段进行：(1) 调用（Call）将专家视觉模型暴露为空间工具，以增强VLM的感知；(2) 学习（Learn）使用冷启动SFT和代理强化学习来改进工具使用；(3) 内化（Internalize）将成功的工具使用轨迹语言化，以内化专家感知能力。我们进一步引入了SpatialCLI-Bench，一个包含516个示例的组合感知基准，涵盖定位、分割、深度和姿态。在MindCube上，SpatialCLI将Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct从29.3%提升到使用工具时的84.6%，超过了使用工具的GPT-5.6 Sol（72.1%），同时在内化后无工具情况下保持了73.8%的性能。

Original abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in embodied agents to interpret visual inputs, reason about spatial relationships, and make task-level decisions based on that reasoning. However, a fundamental capability mismatch remains: general VLMs can reason about the overall task but often miss the visual details that determine success, while specialist vision models can capture those details but cannot translate them into task-level decisions. In this work, we propose SpatialCLI, a framework that teaches VLMs to reason with spatial tools and progressively internalize the specialist perceptual capabilities they provide. SpatialCLI proceeds in three stages: (1) Call exposes specialist vision models as spatial tools to augment the VLM's perception; (2) Learn uses Cold-Start SFT and agentic RL to improve tool use; and (3) Internalize verbalizes successful tool-use trajectories to internalize specialist perceptual capabilities. We further introduce SpatialCLI-Bench, a 516-example benchmark for compositional perception across localization, segmentation, depth, and pose. On MindCube, SpatialCLI raises Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct from 29.3% to 84.6% with tools, surpassing GPT-5.6 Sol with tools (72.1%), while retaining 73.8% without tools after internalization.

</details>


### [18] [Albilich: Steerable Proof-State Orchestration for LLM-Based Mathematical Research with CAS Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27705)
*Ting Gong,Michael Ruofan Zeng,Yong Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 开源数学研究agentic harness，集成CAS、检索与持久上下文，长程证明编排设计对自动优化agent有参考。

TL;DR: Albilich 是一个开源的数学自动研究智能体框架，结合长视界推理、计算机代数系统、文献检索和 SQLite 上下文管理，在 RealMath 和 Kourovka 问题上表现出色，证明了其作为可扩展 AI 辅助数学研究环境的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在数学研究中能提供有用想法，但长视界证明尝试难以协调、评估和复现，需要一个系统性的框架来支持自动研究。

Method: 构建 Albilich，结合长视界推理、CAS、文献检索和 SQLite 持久化上下文管理，并通过消融实验评估各组件的贡献。

Result: 在 RealMath 上解决 10/10（有 CAS）和 9/10（无 CAS）；在 Kourovka 问题上产出反例和加强版证明；消融显示 CAS 减少 32.0% token，缺少顾问智能体导致验证器拒绝率升高且无法合成证明路线。

Conclusion: Albilich 是一个可人工引导、CAS 增强的环境，支持可扩展的 AI 辅助数学研究。

中文摘要：大型语言模型能够为数学研究贡献有用的想法，然而长视界的证明尝试仍然难以协调、评估和复现。我们提出了 Albilich，一个开源的智能体框架，用于数学自动研究，它结合了长视界推理、计算机代数系统（CAS）、文献检索和基于 SQLite 的持久化上下文管理。我们在 RealMath 基准（Zhang et al. 2025）和来自 Kourovka 笔记（Khukhro and Mazurov 2026）的群论开放问题上评估了 Albilich。它在使用 CAS 的情况下解决了 RealMath 上的 10/10 个问题，在没有 CAS 的情况下解决了 9/10。在 Kourovka 问题上，Albilich 对问题 21.142 产生了一个反例，并对问题 20.2 的加强版本给出了一个证明。对问题 17.91 的消融实验表明，启用 CAS 时 token 减少了 32.0%。对问题 21.142 的消融实验表明，在没有顾问智能体的情况下，验证器拒绝率更高，并且无法合成证明路线。这些结果支持 Albilich 作为一个可人工引导、CAS 增强的环境，用于可扩展的 AI 辅助数学研究。

Original abstract: Large language models can contribute useful ideas to mathematical research, yet long-horizon proof attempts remain difficult to coordinate, evaluate, and reproduce. We present Albilich, an open-source agentic harness for autoresearch in mathematics that combines long-horizon reasoning, computer algebra systems (CAS), literature retrieval, and persistent SQLite-based context management.
  We evaluate Albilich on the RealMath benchmark (Zhang et al. 2025) and on open problems in group theory from the Kourovka Notebook (Khukhro and Mazurov 2026). It solved 10/10 problems on RealMath with CAS and 9/10 with no CAS. On the Kourovka problems, Albilich produced a counterexample to Problem 21.142 and a proof of a strengthening of Problem20.2. Anablation on Problem 17.91 demonstrates 32.0% token reduction when CAS is enabled. An ablation on Problem 21.142 demonstrates higher verifier-rejection rate and failure to synthesize proof routes in the absence of the advisor agent. These results support Albilich as a human-steerable, CAS-boosted environment for scalable AI-assisted mathematical research.

</details>


### [19] [MECA: A Mechanism-Centered Agent for Constructing Well-Specified and Valuable Mathematical Conjectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27709)
*Wentao Long,Yunfei Zhang,Chenyi Li,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 多agent通过机制驱动的探索与批评构建猜想，其迭代式问题精炼与验证循环可迁移到agent任务构造。

TL;DR: 提出了机制中心的多代理框架MECA，通过探索和批评代理协同构造数学猜想，生成定义明确且有研究价值的猜想，且对现有自动证明器仍有挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放问题和猜想常过于宽泛或定义不充分，难以与证明或反驳策略关联，自动构造有价值且明确的数学猜想是一大挑战。

Method: MECA采用多代理框架，探索代理提出并测试机制，批评代理评估有效性，根据反馈调整假设和结论，将广泛研究方向转化为有支持的精确猜想。

Result: 在两项评估中，MECA优于生成-修改基线，构造了100个半开放问题，生成的猜想对自动证明器仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: 机制中心的细化能产生定义明确、值得研究的猜想，并保留未解决核心，对当前自动证明器构成挑战。

中文摘要：自动构造定义明确且有价值的数学猜想仍然是人工智能辅助数学发现中的一个核心挑战。许多现有的开放问题和猜想往往过于宽泛、定义不充分，或难以与合理的证明或反驳策略联系起来。我们将数学机制视为一种结构或推理原则，它将候选问题的假设与其目标结论联系起来，例如不等式、不变量、分解或归约为中间命题。我们提出了MECA（机制中心猜想代理，MEchanism-centered Conjecture Agent），一个多代理框架，通过联合开发候选陈述及其支持机制来构造猜想。探索代理提出机制，测试其适用性，并相应地修改候选猜想，而批评代理评估其数学有效性和研究价值。它们的反馈指导对假设、范围和结论的修改。通过这一过程，MECA将广泛的研究方向转化为具有实质性数学支持的精确猜想，同时保留一个清晰识别的未解决核心。我们在两个互补的设置中评估MECA。首先，我们将其与一个生成-修改基线进行比较，用于从目标条件化但文章盲的源材料中重建预先选择的目标论文结论。其次，我们从文献衍生的种子和现有开放问题中构造了100个半开放问题，并通过自动定理证明器的独立证明和反驳尝试对其进行评估。我们的结果表明，机制中心的细化产生了定义明确且具有研究价值的猜想，这些猜想对当前的自动定理证明器仍然具有挑战性。

Original abstract: Automatically constructing well-specified and valuable mathematical conjectures remains a central challenge in AI-assisted mathematical discovery. Many existing open problems and conjectures are often too broad, underspecified, or difficult to connect to plausible proof or refutation strategies. We view a mathematical mechanism as a structure or reasoning principle that connects the assumptions of a candidate problem to its target conclusion, such as an inequality, invariant, decomposition, or reduction to an intermediate claim. We present MECA (MEchanism-centered Conjecture Agent), a multi-agent framework that constructs conjectures by jointly developing candidate statements and their supporting mechanisms. Explorer agents propose mechanisms, test how they apply, and revise the candidate conjecture accordingly, while critic agents assess their mathematical validity and research value. Their feedback guides changes to the assumptions, scope, and conclusion. Through this process, MECA transforms broad research directions into precise conjectures with substantive mathematical support while retaining a clearly identified unresolved core. We evaluate MECA in two complementary settings. First, we compare it with a generate-and-revise baseline on reconstructing preselected target-paper conclusions from target-conditioned but article-blind source materials. Second, we construct 100 semi-open problems from literature-derived seeds and existing open problems and evaluate them through independent proof and refutation attempts by automated provers. Our results indicate that mechanism-centered refinement produces well-specified and research-worthy conjectures that remain challenging for current automated provers.

</details>


### [20] [Baikal: Structured Search for Deep Research over Data Lakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27726)
*Dhruv Agarwal,Rishitha Guttapalle Mohan,Aarti Kumari,Ashi Sinha,Athulya Anil,Kavitha Srinivas,Horst Samulowitz,Andrew McCallum*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 将深度研究建模为预算化搜索，用区域聚类与奖励引导的探索-利用平衡提升报告质量，可迁移到长程信息收集agent。

TL;DR: Baikal通过将数据湖证据聚类为语义区域并自适应搜索，在固定预算下平衡探索与利用，显著提升深度研究报告质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究方法在数据湖上迭代检索时容易过度利用局部证据，无法在固定预算下覆盖不同语义区域。

Method: 将深度研究构建为预算搜索问题，聚类证据为语义区域，并在区域内生成子问题，用发现质量作为奖励更新区域价值，采用多种区域选择策略如随机、LLM引导、贝叶斯ε-greedy和UCB。

Result: 在HybridQA和TAT-QA数据湖上，Baikal最佳配置比最强基线分别提高28%和36%的报告得分，并提升接地性、多样性和实用性。

Conclusion: 结构化语义区域的探索对异构数据湖上的系统研究和发现具有重要价值。

中文摘要：在数据湖上进行深度研究需要LLM智能体跨数千个异构表格和段落调查证据，以综合生成报告。现有方法执行迭代检索和生成，让累积的上下文决定下一步调查什么，这可能在固定预算下过度利用局部有希望的证据，而无法覆盖不同的语义区域。为解决这一问题，我们将数据湖上的深度研究构建为预算受限的搜索问题，并提出了Baikal——一个将异构证据聚类为语义区域，然后自适应地搜索这些区域以平衡探索与利用的框架。在每个选定的区域内，Baikal生成并调查基于区域接地（region-grounded）的子问题，将发现质量作为奖励来更新区域级别的价值估计，并在从随机和LLM引导选择到贝叶斯ε-greedy和UCB的策略下指导搜索。我们在HybridQA和TAT-QA数据湖上各用15个查询评估Baikal，这两个数据湖分别包含10,993和2,757个表，以及227K个维基百科段落和13K个财务报告段落。我们使用一个新的评估标准来评估研究质量，涵盖接地性、相关性、多样性和实用性，并使用GPT-5-mini对Baikal和强基线（包括DeepSearcher以及带有检索和聚类变体的OpenCode研究智能体）进行评分。在两个数据湖上，Baikal在多种区域选择策略下都表现强劲；其最佳配置在HybridQA上比最强基线将报告得分提高28%，在TAT-QA上提高36%。我们的分析将这些收益归因于组织和探索语义证据区域，这提高了接地性和多样性，并在相同的子问题预算下产生了更有用的发现。这些结果证明了结构化语义探索对于在异构数据湖上进行系统研究和发现的价值。

Original abstract: Deep research over data lakes requires an LLM agent to investigate evidence across thousands of heterogeneous tables and passages to synthesize a report. Existing methods perform iterative retrieval and generation, letting accumulated context determine what to investigate next, which can overexploit locally promising evidence and fail to cover distinct semantic regions under a fixed budget. To address this, we cast deep research over data lakes as a budgeted search problem and present Baikal - a framework that clusters heterogeneous evidence into semantic regions, then searches over them adaptively to balance exploration and exploitation. Within each selected region, Baikal generates and investigates region-grounded subquestions, using finding quality as rewards to update region-level value estimates and guide search under policies ranging from random and LLM-guided selection to Bayesian $ε$-greedy and UCB. We evaluate Baikal on 15 queries each over HybridQA and TAT-QA data lakes containing 10,993 and 2,757 tables, respectively, together with 227K Wikipedia passages and 13K financial report passages. We assess research quality with a new rubric covering groundedness, relevance, diversity, and utility, and use GPT-5-mini to score Baikal and strong baselines, including DeepSearcher and an OpenCode research agent with retrieval and clustering variants. Across both data lakes, Baikal performs strongly under several region-selection policies; its best configuration improves report scores over the strongest baselines by 28% on HybridQA and 36% on TAT-QA. Our analyses attribute these gains to organizing and exploring semantic evidence regions, which improves groundedness and diversity and yields more useful findings under the same subquestion budget. These results demonstrate the value of structured semantic exploration for systematic research and discovery over heterogeneous data lakes.

</details>


### [21] [VeriSkill: A Self-Evolution Framework for Program Verification Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27733)
*Changguo Jia,Tianqi Zhao,Zhiyou Xiao,Weiming Zhang,Minghui Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 为程序验证构建技能自进化框架，可靠归因验证反馈中的技能缺陷并迭代精炼，与代码优化agent高度同构。

TL;DR: 提出了VeriSkill，一个针对程序验证的自我进化框架，通过将验证失败归因于技能缺陷并提炼诊断性签名，迭代改进技能，在多个工具和模型上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能自我进化方法难以处理程序验证，因为无法可靠识别技能特定失败，也难以从模糊的验证器反馈中提取可操作信号。

Method: VeriSkill将验证失败归因于技能缺陷，提炼诊断性签名为可复用经验，并仅接受能提高验证性能且保持程序语义的修订，迭代精炼候选技能。

Result: 在多种验证工具、代理框架和LLM后端上，VeriSkill consistently 优于所有基线。

Conclusion: VeriSkill为程序验证提供了有效的自我进化方法，展示了通过归因和诊断性提炼来提升LLM代理验证能力的可行性。

中文摘要：使用LLM代理自动化程序验证需要生成规范、注解、辅助引理和工具调用，所有这些都依赖于可复用的技能。自然的解决方法是技能自我进化：从轨迹中提炼技能并通过反馈进行改进。然而，现有的进化方法在程序验证任务中难以奏效，因为它们无法可靠地识别特定于技能的失败，也无法从模糊的验证器反馈中提取可操作的信号。在本文中，我们提出了VeriSkill，一个专为程序验证构建的自我进化框架。它将验证失败归因于技能缺陷，将诊断性签名提炼为可复用的经验，并迭代地改进候选技能，只接受能提高验证性能且保持程序语义的修订。实验表明，VeriSkill在多种验证工具、代理框架和LLM后端上 consistently 优于所有基线。

Original abstract: Automating program verification with LLM agents requires generating specifications, annotations, auxiliary lemmas, and tool invocations, all of which depend on reusable skills. A natural remedy is skill self-evolution: distilling skills from trajectories and refining them through feedback. However, existing evolution methods struggle with program verification tasks because they cannot reliably identify skill-specific failures or extract actionable signals from opaque verifier feedback. In this paper, we propose VeriSkill, a self-evolution framework built for program verification. It attributes verification failures to skill deficiencies, distills diagnostic signatures into reusable lessons, and iteratively refines candidate skills, admitting only revisions that improve verification performance while preserving program semantics. Experiments show that VeriSkill consistently outperforms all baselines across multiple verification tools, agent frameworks, and LLM backends.

</details>


### [22] [MemTxn: A Transaction Boundary for Source-Supported Updates and Complete-State Recovery in Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27834)
*Hanshuai Cui,Zhiqing Tang,Zhi Yao,Fanshuai Meng,Qianli Ma,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出agent记忆的事务边界与状态恢复机制，对长程coding agent的可靠持久化有直接借鉴价值。

TL;DR: 提出MemTxn，一个用于持久内存智能体的事务治理层，提供更新验证、冲突解决和故障恢复，显著提升可靠性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持久内存系统缺乏事务边界，导致错误持续存在并破坏未来行为，需要可靠更新与恢复机制。

Method: MemTxn作为答案模型外的治理层，使用Ordered PatchTest验证写入、Temporal Resolver选择冲突版本、持久化快照日志恢复状态。

Result: 在审计中接受60个支持项、拒绝179个硬负样本；多键故障下恢复完整状态；FactConsolidation上平均F1最高，比Dense高17.06-24.07点。

Conclusion: MemTxn为持久内存智能体提供了有效的事务治理，增强了可靠性和性能，具有广泛适用性。

中文摘要：持久化内存使长期运行的大型语言模型智能体能够在会话和任务之间重用信息。然而，可写内存中的错误可能会持续存在并破坏未来的行为。现有系统改善了存储和检索，但并未提供可靠更新和恢复的事务边界。因此，我们提出了MemTxn，一个位于答案模型之外的治理层。MemTxn验证更新是否由其来源支持。它还在事实冲突时选择可见版本，并在故障后恢复应用可见状态。该系统使用有序补丁测试（Ordered PatchTest）来验证写入，使用时间解析器（Temporal Resolver）来选择版本，并使用持久化快照日志来恢复状态。在逐项不相交审计中，MemTxn接受了所有60个受支持的原始项，并拒绝了所有179个硬负样本。在LongMemEval-S和LoCoMo状态上的持久多键故障下，它无需知道实际物理写入集即可恢复完整的声明活动映射。在MemoryAgentBench事实整合（FactConsolidation）上，MemTxn在所有十二种答案模型配置中取得了最高的平均F1分数。在五个代表性设置中，它比Dense高出17.06至24.07个百分点。

Original abstract: Persistent memory lets long-running large language model agents reuse information across sessions and tasks. Yet errors in writable memory can persist and corrupt future behavior. Existing systems improve storage and retrieval, but they do not provide a transaction boundary for reliable updates and recovery. We therefore propose MemTxn, a governance layer outside the answer model. MemTxn verifies whether an update is supported by its source. It also selects the visible version when facts conflict and restores the application-visible state after a fault. The system uses Ordered PatchTest to validate writes, a Temporal Resolver to select versions, and a durable snapshot journal to recover state. On an item-disjoint audit, MemTxn accepts all 60 supported originals and rejects all 179 hard negatives. Under persistent multi-key faults on LongMemEval-S and LoCoMo states, it restores the complete declared active map without knowing the actual physical write set. On MemoryAgentBench FactConsolidation, MemTxn achieves the highest average F1 across all twelve answer-model configurations. It outperforms Dense by 17.06--24.07 points in five representative settings.

</details>


### [23] [Search as Computation Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27871)
*Alexander Tuisov*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 将搜索视为计算资源分配并关联值信息理论，为agent规划中计算预算分配提供统一视角。

TL;DR: 将内部计算资源分配形式化为终端计算分配问题，建立了计算价值与信息论的联系，并统一了多种搜索算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多算法在做出最终决策前会消耗内部计算资源，但评估仅基于最终输出质量，缺乏对计算分配环节的通用形式化框架。

Method: 提出终端计算分配问题的贝尔曼方程，分析固定预算、定价计算和精确认证下的最优分配，并揭示VOC与互信息、知识梯度等概念的关系，最后通过显式模型推导加权A*等算法。

Result: 证明了互信息等于对数损失下的短视VOC，简单遗憾下VOC为知识梯度量；信息增益可导致任意差的计算排序但给出单边上限；最大化近似VOC可恢复加权A*。

Conclusion: 该理论提供了一个统一的决策框架，但不主张单一采集规则普遍最优，为不同计算拓扑下的算法设计提供了理论依据。

中文摘要：许多算法在返回决策之前会消耗一种内部资源，并且仅根据该最终输出的质量进行评估。我们将此类过程形式化为终端计算分配问题：昂贵的计算产生观察结果，更新对潜在环境的信念，并且仅通过终端决策损失产生影响。贝尔曼方程刻画了在固定预算、定价计算和精确认证下的最优分配。然后我们将计算价值（VOC）与信息联系起来。在对数损失下，互信息等于短视的VOC，而在简单遗憾下，VOC是一个知识梯度量；此外，信息增益可能使计算排名任意地差，尽管它给出了VOC的一个单边上限。老虎机拉臂、树模拟和节点扩展展示了同一模型在不同计算拓扑下的应用。最后，在显式的前沿解析和启发式误差模型下，最大化近似VOC恢复了加权A*，其中A*和贪心最佳优先搜索是极限情况。该理论识别了一个共享的决策问题，而没有断言某一采集规则是普遍最优的。

Original abstract: Many algorithms spend an internal resource before returning a decision and are evaluated only by the quality of that terminal output. We formalize such procedures as terminal computation-allocation problems: costly computations produce observations, update beliefs about a latent environment, and matter only through terminal decision loss. Bellman equations characterize optimal allocation under fixed budgets, priced computation, and exact certification. We then relate value of computation (VOC) to information. Mutual information equals myopic VOC under log loss, whereas under simple regret VOC is a knowledge-gradient quantity; moreover, information gain can rank computations arbitrarily poorly, although it gives a one-sided upper bound on VOC. Bandit pulls, tree simulations, and node expansions illustrate the same model under different computation topologies. Finally, under an explicit frontier-resolution and heuristic-error model, maximizing approximate VOC recovers weighted A*, with A* and greedy best-first search as limiting cases. The theory identifies a shared decision problem without asserting that one acquisition rule is universally optimal.

</details>


### [24] [An Empirical Study of Coordination Mode as the First-Class Citizen in From-Scratch Multi-Agent Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27877)
*Yanyu Ren,Yunfeng Bai,Xizheng Wang,Li Chen,Dan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 真实多agent从零编码基准与执行引擎，直接研究协作拓扑对速度-成本-质量的影响，高度契合coding agent。

TL;DR: MSEval是一个基于真实全栈项目的多智能体编码评估基准，通过LegoGent执行引擎和TAgent自动评分器，发现组织拓扑对速度-成本-质量权衡的影响不亚于模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体编码基准依赖合成环境，忽略实际成本、混淆推理与通信，且仅奖励表面完成度，无法反映真实软件开发场景。

Method: MSEval利用10个真实全栈项目，通过LegoGent测试10种协作拓扑（使用周期性同步和CI/CD部署），并由TAgent动态探测实现，同时测量功能成功率、延迟和前缀缓存令牌成本。

Result: 在100次运行中，拓扑变化可使分数波动超过30分并将墙钟时间翻倍；结构化流水线在质量和速度上最优，而过度管理监督会降低性能。

Conclusion: MSEval为多智能体协作的实际软件构建能力提供了严格且可复现的评估标准，有助于指导团队拓扑设计。

中文摘要：多智能体vibe coding有望加速软件开发，但现有基准依赖于合成环境，忽略了实际的时间和货币成本，将推理与通信混为一谈，并且只奖励表面的完成度。我们引入了多智能体从零开始评估基准MSEval，在真实世界任务上评估多智能体编码。基于10个领域中的10个真实全栈项目，MSEval使用分层需求和确定性评分标准来评估性能。其执行引擎LegoGent测试了10种协作拓扑，其中智能体通过周期性同步间隔进行协调，并通过原生CI/CD管道进行部署。同时，自动评分器TAgent动态探测实现，以联合衡量功能成功率、延迟和前缀缓存令牌成本。在100次运行中，MSEval揭示出组织拓扑在影响速度-成本-质量权衡方面与模型能力相当。对于相同的任务和模型，改变拓扑会使分数波动超过30分，并使墙钟时间翻倍。结构化流水线收敛最快且质量最高，而繁重的管理监督会降低性能。最终，MSEval为衡量多智能体团队实际如何构建软件建立了一个严格、可复现的标准。该基准已发布于https://github.com/robinren03/MSEval。

Original abstract: Multi-agent vibe coding promises to accelerate software development, yet existing benchmarks rely on synthetic environments that ignore practical time and monetary costs, conflate reasoning with communication, and reward only superficial completion. We introduce multi-agent from-scratch evaluation benchmark, MSEval, evaluating multi-agent coding on real-world tasks. Grounded in 10 authentic, full-stack projects across 10 domains, MSEval scores performance using hierarchical requirements and deterministic rubrics.
  Its execution engine, LegoGent, tests 10 collaboration topologies where agents coordinate via periodic sync intervals and deploy through native CI/CD pipelines. Concurrently, the automated grader TAgent dynamically probes implementations to jointly measure functional success, latency, and prefix-cached token cost. Across 100 runs, MSEval reveals that organizational topology rivals model capability in shaping the speed--cost--quality trade-off. For identical tasks and models, varying the topology shifts scores by over 30 points and doubles wall-clock time. Structured pipelines converge fastest with the highest quality, whereas heavy managerial oversight degrades performance. Ultimately, MSEval establishes a rigorous, reproducible standard for measuring how multi-agent teams actually build software. The benchmark is released at https://github.com/robinren03/MSEval.

</details>


### [25] [Not All Tokens Deserve Equal Credit: Counterfactual Sensitivity Credit Reallocation for Long-CoT Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27888)
*Qiangqiang He,Zhongheng Wu,ZiJian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 改进长CoT推理的token级credit分配，对agent策略训练的奖励塑形有可迁移启发。

TL;DR: 本文发现基于特权自蒸馏的密集奖励信号不可靠，其偏移方向与答案正确性无关，幅度主要反映反事实敏感性。据此提出CSCR方法，通过降低高敏感token的信用并重归一化优势，在长思维链推理上稳定超越GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法（如GRPO）均匀分配token优势，忽视token贡献差异；OPSD假设特权自蒸馏的似然偏移编码可靠答案信息，但该假设未经严格验证。作者希望检验这一前提，并基于诊断结果设计更优的信用分配方法。

Method: 首先进行对照实验：固定轨迹，分别在正确与错误两种结果条件下重新评分，分析token偏移方向与幅度。发现偏移方向与答案对齐性无关，幅度集中于可替换表面token。据此提出CSCR：降低高敏感token的信用，重新归一化token级优势，保留原始信用预算和验证器方向，作为GRPO的简单扩展。

Result: 在长思维链数学推理基准上，CSCR在相同策略更新次数下持续优于GRPO基线。消融实验显示：特权诱导方向不可靠、适度降权最有效、过强调制破坏优化稳定性。

Conclusion: 特权自蒸馏产生的密集信号不能可靠指示token对最终答案的贡献，其幅度主要反映反事实敏感性。CSCR通过基于敏感性的信用再分配，有效提升强化学习训练效率，验证了诊断的正确性。

中文摘要：基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）是提升大型语言模型长思维链推理能力的关键。无评论家方法（如GRPO）将响应级奖励转换为优势值，并均匀地广播到所有token上，忽视了它们对最终结果的贡献差异。相反，在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）通过最小化无特权策略与特权自教师之间的前向KL散度来提供密集的分布监督，其隐含假设是所产生的似然偏移编码了可靠的、与答案对齐的信息。我们通过固定每条采样轨迹并在两种相反的结果条件下重新评分来检验这一前提，一种条件断言正确性，另一种断言错误性。大多数受影响的token在两种条件下都朝相同方向偏移，几乎没有符号反转，并且所诱导的优化信号存在大量重叠。大的偏移也集中在高度可替换的表面形式token上，而携带问题特定推理内容的token则不那么敏感。这些发现表明，特权偏移无法提供可靠的、与答案对齐的方向，而其幅度主要反映反事实敏感性而非token级别的学习价值。基于这些观察，我们提出了反事实敏感性信用再分配（CSCR），这是GRPO的一个简单扩展，它减少高敏感token的信用，并重新归一化token级优势，以保留原有的信用预算和验证器确定的方向。在长思维链数学推理基准上，CSCR在相同策略更新次数下始终优于GRPO基线。定向消融进一步证实了我们的诊断：特权诱导的方向不可靠，适度降权最有效，而更强调制会破坏优化稳定性。

Original abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is central to improving long-CoT reasoning in large language models. Critic-free methods such as GRPO convert response-level rewards into advantages and uniformly broadcast them across tokens, overlooking their unequal contributions to the final outcome. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) instead provides dense distributional supervision by minimizing the forward KL divergence between an unprivileged policy and a privileged self-teacher, implicitly assuming that the resulting likelihood shifts encode reliable answer-aligned information. We test this premise by fixing each sampled trajectory and re-scoring it under two opposing outcome conditions, one asserting correctness and the other incorrectness. Most affected tokens shift in the same direction under both conditions, with few sign reversals and substantial overlap in the induced optimization signals. Large shifts also concentrate on highly substitutable surface-form tokens, whereas tokens carrying problem-specific reasoning content are less sensitive. These findings show that privileged shifts fail to provide reliable answer-aligned directions, while their magnitudes primarily reflect counterfactual sensitivity rather than token-level learning value. Based on these observations, we propose Counterfactual Sensitivity Credit Reallocation (CSCR), a simple extension of GRPO that reduces credit for highly sensitive tokens and renormalizes token-level advantages to preserve both the original credit budget and verifier-determined direction. On long-CoT mathematical reasoning benchmarks, CSCR consistently outperforms GRPO baseline with the same number of policy updates. Targeted ablations further corroborate our diagnosis: privilege-induced directions are unreliable, moderate downweighting is most effective, and stronger modulation destabilizes optimization.

</details>


### [26] [IFHierBench: Hierarchical Instruction Following for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27912)
*Yuetian Mao,Chunyang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 分层指令遵循基准，指令遵循是agent基础能力，其层级约束与检查器设计对评估agent输出鲁棒性有参考价值。

TL;DR: IFHierBench是一个分层指令跟随基准，测试模型对嵌套约束的遵循能力，发现最强模型准确率仅略超50%，且随约束深度增加急剧下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令跟随基准将约束集视为扁平列表，无法检查输出特定部分的约束满足情况，而现实应用需要分层输出的每个部分都满足具体约束，因此需要新的分层基准。

Method: 构建IFHierBench，包含600个提示，跨越四个约束树深度和35种不同约束，每个提示配对一个确定性检查器，用于验证每个范围内的约束满足情况。评估七个领先的专有和开放权重模型。

Result: 最强模型提示级准确率仅略微超过50%，且准确率随约束深度增加而急剧下降，表明可靠遵循嵌套约束仍是当前LLM的重大差距。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在可靠遵循嵌套约束方面存在明显不足，需要未来训练方法考虑更细粒度的约束遵守，以提升指令跟随能力。

中文摘要：指令跟随能力对于在现实世界应用中部署大型语言模型至关重要，因为下游组件依赖于满足特定约束的输出。现代部署越来越倾向于在单次LLM调用中处理整个任务，用一个提示指定分层输出，其整体工件、结构部分和嵌套字段必须各自满足具体约束。现有的指令跟随基准将约束集视为统一应用于响应的扁平列表，因此无法将检查范围限定到输出的特定部分。我们引入了IFHierBench，一个分层指令跟随基准，包含600个提示，跨越四个约束树深度和35种不同约束，每个提示配有一个确定性检查器，用于验证每个范围内的满足情况。评估七个领先的专有和开放权重模型，我们发现即使是最强的模型也仅略微超过50%的提示级准确率，并且准确率随着约束深度的增加而急剧下降。可靠地遵循嵌套约束仍然是当前LLM的一个重大差距，这促使未来的训练方法考虑更细粒度的约束遵守，以实现更好的指令跟随能力。

Original abstract: Instruction-following ability is critical for deploying large language models in real-world applications, where downstream components depend on the output satisfying specific constraints. Modern deployments increasingly handle the full task in a single LLM call, with one prompt specifying a layered output whose overall artifact, structural sections, and nested fields must each satisfy concrete constraints. Existing instruction-following benchmarks treat the constraint set as a flat list applied uniformly to the response, so they cannot scope a check to a particular section of the output. We introduce IFHierBench, a hierarchical instruction-following benchmark of 600 prompts stratified across four constraint-tree depths and 35 distinct constraints, each prompt paired with a deterministic checker that verifies satisfaction at every scope. Evaluating seven leading proprietary and open-weight models, we find that even the strongest model only marginally exceeds 50% prompt-level accuracy and that accuracy degrades sharply as constraint depth grows. Reliably following nested constraints remains a substantial gap for current LLMs, motivating future training methods that consider constraint adherence at finer granularity to achieve better instruction-following ability.

</details>


### [27] [Meta-Task: Turning Terminal Task Synthesis into a Terminal Task for Scalable Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27929)
*Zhihong Pan,Jiyuan He,Kai Zhang,Yupeng Han,Ze Liu,Yuze Zhao,Yongcong Ye,Zhaohua Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 将终端任务合成本身作为可执行任务循环，为agent训练数据自动生成与验证提供可扩展框架，可直接迁移到coding agent。

TL;DR: 提出 Meta-Task，将终端任务合成转化为智能体在真实容器中迭代生成、执行和验证任务的过程，显著提升数据质量与多样性，仅用少量合成轨迹便在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上超越同期方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终端任务合成方法可靠性弱（生成与执行脱节）且多样性有限（依赖现有代码库），难以获取大规模高质量训练数据。

Method: 将任务合成建模为 Terminal-Bench 格式任务，智能体在真实容器中迭代生成、执行和验证；多阶段机制动态设计任务规范；可选的学术/现实材料支持；LLM-as-Judge 过滤保证质量。

Result: 在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上，使用 3,221 条合成轨迹微调 Qwen3-14B 和 Qwen3-32B，分别达到 22.5% 和 31.8% 的 Avg Pass@1，优于同期方法且数据量更少。

Conclusion: Meta-Task 通过将生成过程作为可执行任务闭环，有效提升了合成数据的可靠性与多样性，为终端智能体训练提供了低成本高效益的数据合成方案。

中文摘要：大规模训练终端智能体需要多样化、可验证的终端任务和高质量的交互轨迹，然而获取此类数据仍然是一项重大挑战。现有的合成方法面临两个关键限制：（1）由于任务生成与实际执行之间的脱节导致的可靠性不足，以及（2）由于依赖现有代码库而导致的多样性和可扩展性有限。我们提出了 Meta-Task，一个将终端任务合成重新定义为 Terminal-Bench 格式任务本身的框架：智能体在真实容器环境中运行，迭代地生成、执行和验证任务，从而在生成循环内部检查合成组件的内部一致性和可执行性。在此基础上，我们沿多个维度解耦目标任务需求，引入一种多阶段机制以在实际生成任务之前动态设计新颖的任务规范，并整合可选的外部材料支持以增强多样性和真实性。我们还应用 LLM-as-Judge 过滤来确保最终训练数据的质量。在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上的实验表明，仅使用 3,221 条 Meta-Task 合成轨迹进行微调，Qwen3-14B 和 Qwen3-32B 分别达到 22.5% 和 31.8% 的平均 Pass@1，以显著更少的训练数据超越了同期方法。

Original abstract: Training terminal agents at scale requires diverse, verifiable terminal tasks and high-quality interaction trajectories, yet acquiring such data remains a significant challenge. Existing synthesis methods face two key limitations: (1) weak reliability caused by the disconnect between task generation and real execution, and (2) limited diversity and scalability due to dependence on existing repositories. We propose Meta-Task, a framework that redefines terminal task synthesis as a Terminal-Bench-format task itself: an agent operates within a real container environment to iteratively generate, execute, and verify tasks, so that synthesized components are checked for internal consistency and executability within the generation loop itself. Building upon this, we decouple the target task requirements along multiple dimensions, introduce a multi-phase mechanism that dynamically designs novel task specifications before producing the actual tasks, and incorporate optional external material support to enhance diversity and realism. We additionally apply LLM-as-Judge filtering to ensure the quality of the final training data. Experiments on Terminal-Bench 2.0 show that fine-tuning on only 3,221 Meta-Task synthesized trajectories achieves 22.5% and 31.8% Avg Pass@1 for Qwen3-14B and Qwen3-32B respectively, outperforming concurrent approaches with significantly less training data.

</details>


### [28] [From Scoring to Acting: Outcome-Verified Comparative Self-Distillation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27937)
*Xu Xia,Jinghua Piao,Min Yang,Xiaochong Lan,Jiaju Chen,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出outcome-verified对比式自我蒸馏训练LLM agent，在ALFWorld/WebShop有验证，方法可迁移到coding agent。

TL;DR: 提出结果验证的对比自蒸馏（OVCSD），通过前缀树组织失败轨迹、从学生状态调用技能教师，并在状态对齐分歧处进行局部对比学习，在ALFWorld和WebShop上显著提升LLM智能体的成功率，且训练时特权交互开销很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPSD方法用动作级分数监督学生，但教师偏好未经环境验证，且未充分利用学生和教师轨迹的关系信息，导致监督信号受限。

Method: OVCSD将失败的学生轨迹组织为前缀树，从学生到达的状态自适应调用技能条件教师，保留经结果验证的成功延续；在第一个状态对齐分歧处进行局部对比学习，并蒸馏分歧后的教师后缀。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上，跨三种模型规模一致优于基线，分别比最强基线提升高达29.7和5.4的绝对成功率，且训练期特权交互增加少于3%。

Conclusion: 结果验证的教师监督与对比学习能有效提升自我蒸馏效果，表明结合环境反馈与轨迹对比可增强LLM智能体的技能内化。

中文摘要：近期关于LLM智能体的工作正从外部能力激发转向能力内化，使智能体无需在推理时进行检索即可保留有用技能。在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）提供了一个有前景的方向，但许多现有方法通常通过沿着学生生成的轨迹对动作进行评分来监督学生。这种监督有两个局限性：教师偏好未经环境结果验证，且动作级评分未充分利用学生轨迹、教师轨迹及其行为关系中的信息。因此，我们提倡结果验证的教师监督以及教师在学生轨迹上的对比学习。基于这一观点，我们提出了结果验证的对比自蒸馏（OVCSD）。OVCSD将失败的学生轨迹组织成前缀树，从学生到达的状态自适应地调用技能条件教师，并仅保留经结果验证的成功延续。然后，它在第一个状态对齐分歧处应用局部对比学习，并蒸馏分歧后的教师后缀以转移完成行为。在ALFWorld和WebShop上跨三种模型规模的实验表明，OVCSD一致优于无技能强化学习和现有自蒸馏基线，在ALFWorld和WebShop上分别比最强基线实现了高达29.7和5.4的绝对成功率提升，同时在训练期间增加了不到3%的特权交互。

Original abstract: Recent work on LLM agents is shifting from external capability elicitation to capability internalization, enabling agents to retain useful skills without retrieval at inference time. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) offers a promising direction, but many existing methods typically supervise students by scoring actions along student-generated trajectories. Such supervision has two limitations: teacher preferences are not validated by environment outcomes, and action-level scores underuse information from student rollouts, teacher rollouts, and their behavioral relationship. We therefore advocate outcome-verified teacher supervision and comparative learning over teacher-student trajectories. Based on this view, we propose Outcome-Verified Comparative Self-Distillation (OVCSD). OVCSD organizes failed student rollouts into a prefix tree, adaptively invokes a skill-conditioned teacher from student-reached states, and retains only outcome-verified successful continuations. It then applies localized comparative learning at the first state-aligned divergence and distills the post-divergence teacher suffix to transfer completion behavior. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop across three model scales show that OVCSD consistently outperforms skill-free RL and existing self-distillation baselines, achieving up to 29.7 and 5.4 absolute success-rate gains over the strongest baselines on ALFWorld and WebShop, respectively, while adding less than 3% privileged interaction during training.

</details>


### [29] [MARS-RA: Rank Aggregation for Credit Assignment via Multimodal Comparisons in Embodied Multi-Agent Cooperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27967)
*Dawei Wang,Di Zhao,Xinyuan Liu,Marci Chi Ma,Xiaoyang Liu,Chengming Zhou,Gary Ushaw,Richard Davison*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 用多模态模型生成成对比较进行多智能体信用分配，相对估计的思想对agent奖励塑形有借鉴。

TL;DR: MARS-RA 将信用分配转化为基于大模型生成的成对比较的排名聚合问题，通过相对比较增强鲁棒性，并用潜力奖励塑造引导合作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在合作多智能体强化学习中，信用分配在具体化 AI 环境下因反馈有限、延迟及活跃智能体数量动态变化而尤为困难，传统绝对估计方法易受噪声和动态参与影响。

Method: 提出 MARS-RA 框架，利用大型多模态模型生成贡献导向的智能体两两比较，将信用分配转化为排名聚合问题，并将比较结果转换为贡献分数用于基于潜力的奖励塑造，同时提供理论收敛性与鲁棒性证明。

Result: 在多种具有挑战性的任务上，实验表明 MARS-RA 能有效引导智能体合作，且 Shapley 值可作为解释性参考。

Conclusion: MARS-RA 通过相对估计的排名聚合方式，有效应对动态和噪声环境中的信用分配问题，提升合作性能。

中文摘要：信用分配是合作多智能体强化学习中的一个基本挑战，尤其是在以有限和延迟反馈以及动态变化的活跃智能体数量为特征的具体化人工智能环境中。我们提出了 MARS-RA，一个将信用分配重新表述为排名聚合问题的框架，使用由大型多模态模型生成的基于贡献的智能体两两比较。这种从绝对估计到相对估计的转变确保了对噪声和动态智能体参与的鲁棒性，将比较结果转换为用于基于潜力的奖励塑造的贡献分数。我们为所提出框架的收敛性和鲁棒性提供了理论证明，并表明 Shapley 值可以用作解释性参考。在不同类型的具有挑战性任务上的实验结果表明，MARS-RA 可以引导智能体走向有效合作。

Original abstract: Credit assignment is a fundamental challenge in cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning, particularly in embodied AI settings characterized by limited and delayed feedback as well as dynamically changing numbers of active agents. We propose MARS-RA, a framework that reformulates credit assignment as a rank aggregation problem using contribution-based pairwise comparisons among agents generated by large multimodal models. This shift from absolute to relative estimation ensures robustness against noise and dynamic agent participation, converting comparison results into contribution scores for potential-based reward shaping. We provide theoretical justification for the convergence and robustness of the proposed framework, and show that Shapley values can be used as an interpretive reference. Experimental results on challenging tasks of different types indicate that MARS-RA can guide agents toward effective cooperation.

</details>


### [30] [Share the Judge, Learn the Deferral: Where Specialization Helps LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27984)
*Weining Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 研究评估者专业化与deferral路由的架构权衡，对agent评测与可靠性设计有启发。

TL;DR: 通过99,952个示例和RewardBench 2实验，本文表明领域专业化应放在评估器的信任规则（如级联路由）中，而不是权重中，并提出了“先共享学习，有数据再分裂，在审计边界放置适配”的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统中评估器的判断质量成为瓶颈，但领域专业化应放在权重中还是信任规则中尚不清楚，本文试图回答这一架构问题。

Method: 使用99,952个公开的、基于评分标准的示例，比较了多种评估器配置：基于正确评分标准的微调、八个标准族LoRA评估器、单个秩64适配器、从共享初始化或从头训练的LoRA，以及RewardBench 2上带有正确性头路由的0.6B-4B-8B级联，并进行了严格的95%风险审计。

Result: 正确的评分标准提升2.11点，无关评分标准损失2.66点；划分到8个LoRA损失10.05点，覆盖率从24.44%降至5.43%；从共享初始化恢复至76.85%，高于从头训练19.94点；级联在RewardBench 2上达到89.40%准确率，优于8B单独（84.75%），且使用更少的计算资源。

Conclusion: 支持有条件的设计规则：在数据足够之前共享判断的学习，将领域特定适配放在经过审计的发布边界（如延迟规则）中，而非评估器权重中。
