中文摘要：智能体系统拉大了生成候选输出与审查候选输出之间的差距。本文提出了一个实际的架构问题：领域专业化应该内置于评估器的权重中，还是内置于决定其判断何时可信的规则中？我们研究了99,952个公开的、基于评分标准的示例。提供正确的评分标准相比仅响应的对照组，锁定测试准确率提高了2.11个百分点；用无关的评分标准替换则损失2.66个百分点。然而，将同一训练语料库分配给八个标准族LoRA评估器，却损失了10.05个百分点，并在5%风险目标下将审计覆盖率从24.44%降至5.43%。用单个秩为64的适配器匹配该存储库的存储容量并不会重现这一损失。这一结果也无法用学习率或优化器步数来解释。从共享的已训练评估器初始化族适配器，将测试准确率恢复到76.85%，比相同学习率下的从头训练高出19.94个百分点（95%区间为18.88-21.02）。当专业化控制的是延迟决策而非判断时，结果发生了变化。在RewardBench 2上，学习到的正确性头将示例路由到0.6B-4B-8B级联，而不改变任何奖励分数。在20个锁定重分区中，级联达到了89.40%的准确率，而8B单独为84.75%，归一化参数计算量为0.415。每次运行都通过了精确的单侧95%风险审计；基于边际的规则保持接近84.8%的准确率，同时使用至少0.94的计算量。这些结果提出了一个有条件的规则：共享判断的学习，直到有足够的数据支持分裂，并将领域特定的适配放在经过审计的发布边界中。

Original abstract: Agentic systems have widened the gap between producing candidate outputs and reviewing them. This paper asks a practical architectural question: should domain specialization be built into an evaluator's weights, or into the rule that decides when its judgment can be trusted? We study 99,952 public, rubric-conditioned examples. Supplying the correct rubric improves locked-test accuracy by 2.11 points over a response-only control; replacing it with an unrelated rubric costs 2.66 points. Dividing the same training corpus among eight criterion-family LoRA judges, however, loses 10.05 points and cuts audited coverage at a 5% risk target from 24.44% to 5.43%. Matching the bank's stored capacity with one rank-64 adapter does not reproduce this loss. Nor is the result explained by learning rate or optimizer steps. Initializing the family adapters from a shared, trained judge recovers test accuracy to 76.85%, 19.94 points above scratch training at the same learning rate (95% interval 18.88-21.02). The result changes when specialization governs deferral rather than judgment. On RewardBench 2, learned correctness heads route examples through a 0.6B-4B-8B cascade without changing any reward score. Across 20 locked repartitions, the cascade attains 89.40% accuracy, compared with 84.75% for 8B alone, at 0.415 normalized parameter compute. Every run passes an exact one-sided 95% risk audit; margin-based rules remain near 84.8% accuracy while using at least 0.94 compute. These results suggest a qualified design rule: share the learning of judgment until there is enough data to justify a split, and place domain-specific adaptation in an audited release boundary.

</details>


### [31] [SKIMIX: Multi-Agent Harness-Time Scaling with Skill Mixture for Dynamic Harness Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27994)
*Jia Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 多agent技能混合与harness-time scaling框架，涉及技能检索、路由与进化，可迁移到coding agent设计。

TL;DR: 提出SKIMIX多智能体框架，通过技能检索、路由和进化协作，在数学推理上显著提升，但在多项选择任务上效果有限或负面，且扩展非单调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体依赖大型技能库时，手动选择和组合技能困难，且现有方法难以自动维护和扩展，需要一种自适应协作机制。

Method: SKIMIX框架包含三个核心组件：基于嵌入的技能检索（选择相关技能）、子模抗稀释路由（避免信息冗余，有效分配任务）和自适应技能进化（根据反馈动态调整技能库）。

Result: 在六个推理基准上，SKIMIX显著提升开放式数学推理性能，但在多项选择任务上提升有限甚至下降；智能体数量增加时性能非单调变化，且主要收益集中在第一轮迭代细化。

Conclusion: 任务特征决定多智能体技能集成的有效性，建议根据任务类型调整协作策略，为大规模智能体系统设计提供实践指导。

中文摘要：AI智能体越来越依赖大型技能库，但选择、组合和维护技能仍然困难。我们提出SKIMIX，一个多智能体框架，其中拥有不同技能组合的智能体通过迭代细化进行协作。SKIMIX结合了基于嵌入的技能检索、子模抗稀释路由和自适应技能进化。在六个推理基准上，多智能体协作显著改善了开放式数学推理，但在多项选择任务上收益有限或为负。智能体数量扩展是非单调的，且大多数改进出现在第一轮细化中。这些结果表明，任务特征决定了技能级集成是否有帮助，并为可扩展智能体设计提供了实用指导。

Original abstract: AI agents increasingly rely on large skill libraries, but selecting, combining, and maintaining skills remains difficult. We propose SKIMIX, a multi-agent framework in which agents with different skill portfolios collaborate through iterative refinement. SKIMIX combines embedding-based skill retrieval, submodular anti-dilution routing, and adaptive skill evolution. Across six reasoning benchmarks, multi-agent collaboration substantially improves open-ended mathematical reasoning but offers limited or negative gains on multiple-choice tasks. Agent-count scaling is non-monotonic, and most improvements arise during the first refinement round. These results show that task characteristics determine whether skill-level ensembles help and provide practical guidance for scalable agent design.

</details>


### [32] [DataClawEval: A Benchmark for Data Engineering Agents in Real Industrial Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28033)
*Debin Meng,Jiaming Yang,Zefang Zong,Tengyue Xu,Haining Xie,Yang Li,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 面向数据工程agent的端到端benchmark，含确定性沙箱评估和16个前沿agent对比，评测方法论可直接迁移。

TL;DR: DataClawEval是首个面向端到端数据工程的综合基准，基于生产级代码构建100个任务、覆盖5种引擎，采用确定性脚本评估16个前沿智能体，最高得分仅74.9，且无模型全能领先，揭示严格领域专业化，表明自主数据工程仍是未解难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要聚焦于简化的Text-to-SQL转换或数据分析，而端到端数据工程这一关键且复杂的领域在很大程度上未被探索，亟需一个专门评估自主智能体在真实世界数据工程场景中端到端任务完成能力的基准。

Method: 提出DataClawEval基准，基于专业企业数据工程师编写的生产级代码，包含100个端到端任务，覆盖PySpark、MySQL、HiveSQL、PrestoSQL/Trino和FlinkSQL五种执行引擎；每个任务在隔离沙箱中执行，并使用确定性的基于规则的脚本进行评分，而非LLM-as-a-judge。

Result: 对16个前沿智能体的评估显示，最强模型总体得分仅为74.9，且没有任何单一模型占据主导地位；每个模型在不同引擎上各有所长，反映出严格的领域专业化而非全能熟练度。

Conclusion: 自主数据工程仍然是一个艰巨且尚未解决的挑战。通过发布数据集、容器化环境和确定性评估脚本，DataClawEval为该领域的进一步研究和进步提供了基础。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLMs）和基于LLM的智能体正越来越多地被部署到自动化复杂工作流中，有望彻底改变数据管理和处理。然而，现有基准主要侧重于简化的Text-to-SQL转换或数据分析，留下端到端数据工程这一关键且复杂的领域在很大程度上未被探索。为了弥合这一差距，我们引入了DataClawEval，这是第一个专门用于评估自主智能体在真实世界数据工程场景中端到端任务完成能力的综合基准。它基于专业企业数据工程师编写的生产级代码，包含100个严谨的端到端任务，涵盖五种执行引擎：PySpark、MySQL、HiveSQL、PrestoSQL/Trino和FlinkSQL。与不确定性的LLM-as-a-judge评分不同，每个任务都在特定于案例的隔离沙箱中执行，并通过确定性的、基于规则的脚本进行评分。评估16个前沿智能体暴露了关键局限性：最强的模型总体得分仅为74.9，且没有单一模型占主导地位，因为每个模型在不同的引擎上表现出色，揭示了严格的领域专业化而非全能熟练度。因此，自主数据工程仍然是一个艰巨且未解决的挑战。我们已在https://github.com/Dicemy/DataClawEval/tree/master发布我们的数据集、容器化环境和确定性评估脚本。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and LLM-based agents are increasingly being deployed to automate complex workflows, promising to revolutionize data management and processing. However, existing benchmarks predominantly focus on simplified Text-to-SQL translation or data analysis, leaving the critical and complex domain of end-to-end data engineering largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce DataClawEval, the first comprehensive benchmark designed specifically to evaluate the end-to-end task completion capabilities of autonomous agents in real-world data engineering scenarios. Built upon production-grade code authored by professional enterprise data engineers, it comprises 100 rigorous, end-to-end tasks spanning five execution engines: PySpark, MySQL, HiveSQL, PrestoSQL/Trino, and FlinkSQL. Rather than non-deterministic LLM-as-a-judge scoring, each task is executed within a case-specific, isolated sandbox and graded by deterministic, rule-based scripts. Evaluating 16 frontier agents exposes critical limitations: The strongest model attains only 74.9 overall, and no single model dominates, as each excels on a different engine, revealing strict domain specialization rather than omnipotent proficiency. Thus, autonomous data engineering remains a formidable, unresolved challenge. We release our dataset, containerized environments, and deterministic evaluation scripts at https://github.com/Dicemy/DataClawEval/tree/master

</details>


### [33] [SKILL-KD: Contrastive Skill Distillation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28048)
*Qiming Shi,Yibo Dou,Jiawen Zhu,Yulong Tao,Linbo Jin,Zhaolu Kang,Yunfan Zhou,Di Weng*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 对比式技能蒸馏框架，将教师轨迹差异提炼为可更新的技能补丁，适用于agent技能获取与持续改进。

TL;DR: 提出SKILL-KD，通过对比性技能蒸馏将教师轨迹与学生失败轨迹的差异显式转化为技能补丁，并迭代优化，防止技能漂移，持续提升冻结学生智能体在多个基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能获取方法将技能视为经验总结或成功示范摘要，导致学生失败时无法从失败轨迹中推断缺失行为，而教师轨迹又过于隐晦，难以内化为可复用指导，造成弱学生智能体与技能知识之间的不匹配。

Method: SKILL-KD包括：对比性技能蒸馏，基于学生失败与教师轨迹生成技能补丁；通过重跑学生评估补丁并迭代细化；利用轨迹链接编辑历史和漂移感知技能整合，决定补丁是新增规则、删除/修改现有规则还是跳过，以防止技能漂移。

Result: 在五个智能体基准和两种学生设置下，SKILL-KD持续优于固定模型自适应基线，提升冻结学生智能体的性能。

Conclusion: SKILL-KD作为一种显式技能蒸馏机制，有效缩小了不同能力智能体之间的差距，提供了一种稳健的无需更新模型参数的智能体增强方法，且能防止技能漂移。

中文摘要：基于技能的提示已成为提升大型语言模型（LLM）智能体的一种实用机制，然而现有的技能获取方法通常将技能视为经验总结、记忆条目或成功示范的直接摘要。这导致较弱的学生智能体面临不匹配问题：当学生因缺乏任务知识或操作策略而失败时，其失败轨迹可能不足以推断缺失行为，而教师轨迹可能过于隐晦而无法被内化为可复用的指导。我们提出SKILL-KD，一种对比性技能蒸馏框架，将技能视为不同能力智能体之间的显式蒸馏媒介。给定同一任务上学生的失败轨迹和教师轨迹，SKILL-KD将它们的可操作差异蒸馏为文本技能补丁，通过重新运行学生来评估该补丁，并在学生仍然失败时迭代细化补丁。为防止重复的局部更新导致技能漂移，SKILL-KD进一步维护轨迹链接的编辑历史，并执行漂移感知技能整合，决定每个补丁应添加新规则、删除或修改现有规则，还是跳过。在五个智能体基准和两种学生设置下，SKILL-KD在固定模型自适应基线上持续改进了冻结的学生智能体。

Original abstract: Skill-based prompting has become a practical mechanism for improving large language model (LLM) agents, yet existing skill acquisition methods often treat skills as experience summaries, memory entries, or direct summaries of successful demonstrations. This creates a mismatch for weaker student agents: when a student fails because it lacks task knowledge or operational strategy, its failed trajectory may not contain enough evidence to infer the missing behavior, while the teacher trajectory may be too implicit to be internalized as reusable guidance. We propose SKILL-KD, a contrastive skill distillation framework that treats skills as an explicit distillation medium between agents of different capabilities. Given a student failure and the teacher trajectory on the same task, SKILL-KD distills their actionable discrepancy into a textual skill patch, evaluates the patch by re-running the student, and iteratively refines the patch when the student still fails. To prevent repeated local updates from causing skill drift, SKILL-KD further maintains trace-linked edit histories and performs Drift-Aware Skill Consolidation, deciding whether each patch should add a new rule, delete or modify an existing rule, or be skipped. Across five agent benchmarks and two student settings, SKILL-KD consistently improves frozen student agents over fixed-model adaptation baselines.

</details>


### [34] [Echoverse: Deep, Evolving Environments for Training Computer-Use Agents at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28074)
*Yash Pandya,Sahil Gupta,Sarthak Harne,Archana Yadav,Kavyansh Chourasia,Hussein Mozannar,Vibhav Vineet,Sara Abdali,Corby Rosset,Yash Lara,Ahmed Awadallah,Ece Kamar,Akshay Nambi*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 为computer-use agent构建深度演化环境并用验证器驱动训练，其环境生成与协同进化方法论可迁移到coding agent训练。

TL;DR: Echoverse通过编译规格生成有状态的合成环境，并利用协同进化循环修复环境与训练信号，使9B模型在计算机使用任务上达到接近前沿模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实应用多需登录且具状态，难以用于训练，现有合成环境生成管线虽可批量产生环境，但环境内部质量成为瓶颈。本文识别三个关键特性：行为深度、针对失败交互、与模型共同进化，并据此构建系统。

Method: Echoverse将规格编译为有状态应用，任务由应用数据库评分；协同进化循环每次读取评分的轨迹，既修复环境、任务和验证器，又为模型提供训练信号。同时支持强化学习，使用结合接地验证器和逐步骤评判器的奖励。

Result: 在12个环境中训练后，9B模型在14个评估分割上从36.5%升至67.1%，与前沿大模型差距在14点内。深层环境提升真实站点准确率，而浅层降低；修复单个环境提升模型16.2%至38.5%；强化学习奖励使留出得分从58.8%升至68.0%。发布4个基准环境。

Conclusion: 环境的行为深度、针对性及协同进化是提升计算机使用智能体的关键；Echoverse验证了这些特性可以显著提升小模型性能，并缩小与大模型的差距。代码已开源。

中文摘要：计算机使用智能体通过其动作所产生的变化进行学习，因此训练此类智能体需要可供其操作、破坏和重置的应用程序。最重要的应用程序通常需要登录且具有状态，因此合成环境可替代它们。近期的流程能够批量生成此类环境，这将瓶颈从环境数量转移到每个环境内部的内容质量。我们发现，收益来自三个特性：环境所承载的行为深度、是否针对智能体实际失败的交互进行设计，以及该环境是否随模型一同改进。我们提出了 Echoverse，它可将规格说明编译为有状态的应用程序，其任务根据应用程序自身的数据库进行评分；同时提出一个协同进化循环，它对每一次评分轨迹进行两次读取：一次用于对环境、任务及其验证器进行修复，另一次用作模型的训练信号。在十二个此类环境上训练后，一个 9B 模型在十四个评估子集上的表现从 36.5% 提升到 67.1%，与教授它的规模大得多的前沿模型仅相差十四个百分点。我们逐一考察每个特性。在相同领域上，浅层环境将真实站点准确率降至基线模型之下（80.0 → 75.0），而深层环境则将其提升（80.0 → 85.0 和 48.0 → 65.0）；在多种渲染中反复训练一个界面控件，该能力可迁移到留出的控件族以及开放网络上；并且修复单个环境可将训练于其上的模型从 16.2% 提升到 38.5%。这些环境同样可用于强化学习场景，其中结合了基于事实的验证器与密集逐步骤评判器的奖励函数，将留出得分从 58.8% 提升至 68.0%。我们发布了四个环境作为基准测试集，包含其应用程序、种子数据和基于事实的评分器。代码：https://aka.ms/echoverse

Original abstract: Computer-use agents learn from what their actions change, so training one needs applications it can act on, break and reset. The applications that matter most are login-gated and stateful, so synthetic environments stand in for them. Recent pipelines generate such environments in bulk, which moves the bottleneck from how many exist to what is inside each one. The returns, we find, come from three properties: how much behavioural depth an environment carries, whether it targets the interaction an agent actually fails, and whether it improves alongside the model. We present Echoverse, which compiles specifications into stateful applications whose tasks are graded against the application's own database, and a co-evolution loop that reads every graded rollout twice: as repairs to the environment, its tasks and its verifier, and as training signal for the model. Trained on twelve such environments, a 9B model improves from $36.5\%$ to $67.1\%$ across fourteen evaluation splits, within fourteen points of the much larger frontier model that taught it. We examine each property in turn. On the same domains, shallow environments push live-site accuracy below the base model ($80.0 \to 75.0$) while deep ones raise it ($80.0 \to 85.0$ and $48.0 \to 65.0$); drilling one interface control across many renderings transfers to held-out widget families and to the open web; and repairing a single environment lifts the model trained on it from $16.2\%$ to $38.5\%$. The same worlds serve as reinforcement-learning environments, where a reward combining the grounded verifier with a dense per-step judge raises held-out score from $58.8\%$ to $68.0\%$. We release four environments as a benchmark, with their applications, seed data and grounded graders. Code: https://aka.ms/echoverse

</details>


### [35] [Group-Reflective Self-Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28076)
*Binbin Zheng,Zijun Xie,Guanqun Zhao,Enlei Gong,Xing Ma,Xiaoliang Fu,Zeyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 基于组内反思的自我蒸馏改进agentic强化学习的稀疏奖励，让策略从自身轨迹提炼可执行指导，具有通用性。

TL;DR: 提出组反射自蒸馏（GRSD），利用策略自身验证回滚的反思对比构建组级特权指导，改进RLVR的稀疏奖励信用分配，实验证明其优于基线并提升泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR仅使用终端奖励，难以区分轨迹中的成功行为、错误和偶然选择；而现有自蒸馏方法依赖外部或更强模型提取的技能，可能不匹配策略能力或过于路径特定。

Method: GRSD对每个提示在同策略组中让策略反思每条验证轨迹，用停止梯度快照对比成功与失败回滚的反思生成组级特权指导，并由自我教师据此调制基于结果的优势进行回合级信用分配。

Result: 在多个智能体环境和模型规模下，GRSD持续优于竞争性基线，并在未见任务上泛化更有效。

Conclusion: GRSD通过从策略自身验证回滚中提取能力对齐且结果区分度的指导，有效增强RLVR的稀疏监督，提升了智能体训练的性能与泛化能力。

中文摘要：具有可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在训练大型语言模型智能体方面是有效的。然而，终端奖励仅提供粗略的轨迹级监督，使成功行为、重复出现的错误和偶然选择纠缠在相同的结果信号中。现有的智能体自蒸馏方法通过自然语言技能来丰富稀疏监督，但外部检索或由更强模型从单条轨迹中提取的技能可能与当前经验不匹配、超出策略能力或仍局限于特定路径。我们提出组反射自蒸馏（GRSD），该方法从策略自身的验证回滚中推导出与能力对齐且具有结果区分度的指导。对于每个提示，策略在同策略组中对每条验证轨迹进行反思，而一个停止梯度快照对比成功与失败回滚所产生的反思，以构建组级特权指导。在此指导下，自我教师通过调制基于结果的优势来细化回合级信用分配，同时保持验证器确定的学习方向。跨多个智能体环境和模型规模的实验表明，GRSD持续优于竞争性基线，并在未见任务上更有效地泛化。

Original abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is effective for training large language model agents. However, terminal rewards provide only coarse trajectory-level supervision, leaving successful behaviors, recurring mistakes, and incidental choices entangled in the same outcome signal. Existing agentic self-distillation methods enrich sparse supervision with natural-language skills, but skills retrieved externally or extracted from a single trajectory by stronger models may mismatch current experience, exceed the policy's capability, or remain path-specific. We propose Group-Reflective Self-Distillation (GRSD), which derives capability-aligned and outcome-discriminative guidance from the policy's own verified rollouts. For each prompt, the policy reflects on each verified trajectory in an on-policy group, and a stop-gradient snapshot contrasts the resulting reflections from successful and failed rollouts to construct group-level privileged guidance. Conditioned on this guidance, a self-teacher refines turn-level credit assignment by modulating outcome-based advantages while preserving the verifier-determined learning direction. Experiments across multiple agentic environments and model scales demonstrate that GRSD consistently outperforms competitive baselines and generalizes more effectively to unseen tasks.

</details>


### [36] [Distilling Answer Set Programming Theories from Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28086)
*Nelson Higuera Ruiz,Markus Hofmarcher,Claudiu Leoveanu-Condrei*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 研究固定agent harness下LLM+求解器在环蒸馏ASP理论的能力，对神经符号工具使用与agent验证有启发。

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号方法，让模型在1小时内从空文件开始提炼完整的ASP理论，用于VQA任务。评估了九个模型，结果显示前沿模型大多能成功，但GPT-5表现异常，且参考理论对其有负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从头编写ASP理论困难且耗时，作者希望研究模型能否自动提炼完整正确的理论。

Method: 采用神经符号方法，固定代理框架与求解器在环，以单一提示和空文件为起点，限时1小时，数据集无关协议。在CLEVR、GQA、CLEVRER上进行VQA任务评估，并比较多种规模模型。

Result: 三个前沿模型在CLEVR上达100%，GQA上92.8%-98.8%，CLEVRER上92.7%-95.3%；GPT-5在CLEVR上98.7%，但GQA降至41.8%，CLEVRER降至86.7%。添加参考理论对其他模型影响±3.4pp以内，但使GPT-5下降3-19pp。

Conclusion: 模型规模影响显著，前沿模型多数能蒸馏出完整理论，但GPT-5的泛化能力和对参考理论的鲁棒性较差。开源代码、提示词和理论。

中文摘要：从头编写答案集编程（ASP）理论是一项困难且耗时的任务。我们采用神经符号方法研究模型是否能在固定代理框架（带求解器在环）下提炼出完整且正确的理论。该协议与数据集无关：以单一提示词和空文件为起点，模型有1小时的时间限制来推导出完整理论。我们选择VQA作为应用领域，以及三个基准（CLEVR、GQA、CLEVRER），因为这些基准是公开可用的且具有非平凡难度。为了研究解决此任务所需的模型规模，我们评估了九个不同的模型：四个前沿模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6、Claude Opus 4.7、GPT-5、DeepSeek V4 Pro），两个中档模型（DeepSeek V4 Flash、gpt-oss-120b），以及三个开放权重模型（qwen3.6-27b、gpt-oss-20b、qwen3.5-9b）。四个前沿模型中有三个在CLEVR上达到100%，在GQA上达到92.8%-98.8%；在CLEVRER上，Sonnet、Opus、DeepSeek V4 Pro的得分在92.7%-95.3%之间。GPT-5在CLEVR上达到98.7%，但在GQA上降至41.8%，在CLEVRER上降至86.7%。添加来自其他数据集的手写参考理论对其他三个前沿模型的影响至多为±3.4个百分点，但使GPT-5的准确率降低了3-19个百分点。我们发布了代码、提示词和提炼出的理论。

Original abstract: Writing Answer Set Programming (ASP) theories from scratch is a difficult and time-consuming task. We take a neurosymbolic approach to study whether a model can distill complete and correct theories, given a fixed agent harness with the solver in the loop. The protocol is dataset-agnostic: with a single prompt and an empty file as the starting point the model is given a 1-hour time limit to derive a complete theory. We chose VQA as the application domain, three benchmarks (CLEVR, GQA, CLEVRER), as these are publicly available and non-trivial. In order to study the model scale required for solving this task we nine different models: four frontier (Claude Sonnet 4.6, Claude Opus 4.7, GPT-5, DeepSeek V4 Pro), two mid-tier (DeepSeek V4 Flash, gpt-oss-120b), and three open-weights (qwen3.6-27b, gpt-oss-20b, qwen3.5-9b). Three of four frontier models reach 100% on CLEVR and 92.8%-98.8% on GQA; on CLEVRER, Sonnet, Opus, DeepSeek V4 Pro score 92.7%-95.3%. GPT-5 reaches 98.7% on CLEVR but drops to 41.8% on GQA and to 86.7% on CLEVRER. Adding handwritten reference theories from other datasets moves the other three frontier models by at most +/-3.4 pp but reduces GPT-5's accuracy by 3-19 pp. We release the code, prompts, and theories distilled.

</details>


### [37] [SciDataSailor: Deep Scientific Data Exploring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28098)
*Jiyong Rao,Yicheng Qiu,Chi Zhang,Chunfeng Song,Runkai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 构建科学数据探索agent并用MCTS合成可执行轨迹，其探索-利用平衡与轨迹合成流程可迁移到coding agent训练。

TL;DR: 提出深度科学数据探索范式及 SciDataSailor 框架，通过 MCTS 合成工具交互轨迹，构建数据集和基准以评估 LLM 智能体的科学数据探索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体研究忽视了对真实科学数据资产的可执行环境交互，而科学数据集的分层异构特性使检查、集成和分析困难重重，需要自动化探索方法减少人工依赖。

Method: 提出 SciDataSailor 框架，将轨迹合成建模为 MCTS，并结合难度分层探索种子、双反馈首步紧迫性、层次化策略到工具动作生成、熵引导分支四种任务特定机制；进而构建 SFT 数据集和评测基准。

Result: 构建了 SciDataSailor-SFT-2K（2K 条监督微调轨迹）与 SciDataSailor-Bench（包含 627 个元信息摘要任务和 586 个科学问答任务），覆盖生命、地球和物理科学的 27 个数据集。

Conclusion: 为 LLM 智能体在真实科学数据环境中的交互式探索提供了可行范式与评测基准，有望显著降低科学数据检查与集成的人工成本。

中文摘要：科学数据集通常组织为包含异构且相互依赖文件的分层仓库，使得其检查、集成和分析劳动密集且依赖领域专业知识。尽管大语言模型（LLM）智能体在规划、推理和工具使用方面取得了显著进步，但现有研究大多忽视了它们通过可执行环境与真实科学数据资产交互的能力。我们引入了深度科学数据探索（Deep Scientific Data Exploration），一种智能体任务范式，其中智能体导航仓库、解释异构文件和模式、执行分析、集成跨文件证据，并基于已执行的观察得出结论。为使这一范式可操作化，我们提出了 SciDataSailor，一个通过平衡广泛探索与定向利用来合成工具交互轨迹的框架。SciDataSailor 将轨迹合成实例化为蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS），并具有四个任务特定机制：难度分层探索种子、双反馈首步紧迫性、层次化策略到工具动作生成以及熵引导分支。利用该框架，我们构建了用于监督微调的 SciDataSailor-SFT-2K 和用于评估的 SciDataSailor-Bench，后者包含 627 个元信息摘要任务和 586 个科学问答任务，涵盖生命、地球和物理科学的 27 个数据集。

Original abstract: Scientific datasets are commonly organized as hierarchical repositories containing heterogeneous and interdependent files, making their inspection, integration, and analysis labor-intensive and reliant on domain expertise. Although large language model (LLM) agents have advanced substantially in planning, reasoning, and tool use, existing research has largely overlooked their ability to interact with real scientific data assets through executable environments. We introduce Deep Scientific Data Exploration, an agentic task paradigm in which agents navigate repositories, interpret heterogeneous files and schemas, execute analyses, integrate cross-file evidence, and produce conclusions grounded in executed observations. To operationalize this paradigm, we present SciDataSailor, a framework for synthesizing tool-interactive trajectories by balancing broad exploration with targeted exploitation. SciDataSailor instantiates trajectory synthesis as Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) with four task-specific mechanisms: difficulty-stratified exploration seeds, dual-feedback first-play urgency, hierarchical strategy-to-tool action generation, and entropy-guided branching. Using this framework, we construct SciDataSailor-SFT-2K for supervised fine-tuning and SciDataSailor-Bench for evaluation, with the latter comprising 627 meta-information summarization tasks and 586 scientific question-answering tasks across 27 datasets spanning the life, earth, and physical sciences.

</details>


### [38] [MIND: Lightweight and Effective Memory Injection Defense for LLM Agents via Intent-Aware Information Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28103)
*Dongyi Liu,Haixing He,Xiaobao Wu,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 针对记忆增强LLM agent的注入攻击提出轻量防御，涉及意图-行为互信息与检测器，对agent安全控制有直接借鉴。

TL;DR: 提出轻量级防御框架MIND，利用意图感知信息瓶颈提取紧凑表示并检测恶意记忆，从而在多轮上下文中降低记忆注入攻击成功率，同时保持任务准确性和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御手段成本高或存在多轮信息冗余，而记忆注入攻击会使智能体检索到中毒记忆并偏离用户意图，因此需要轻量且能处理多轮上下文的防御方法。

Method: MIND基于良性与中毒轨迹在初始意图和后续行为关系上的差异，用意图感知信息瓶颈提取紧凑的意图-行为表示，过滤冗余信息，再由轻量检测器识别恶意记忆。

Result: 实验表明MIND降低攻击成功率同时保持精度和效率；在ReAct-StrategyQA上平均ASR-r和ASR-a分别降低55.4%和55.3%，平均准确率和延迟与未防御相当。

Conclusion: MIND为记忆注入攻击提供了一种轻量有效的防御方案，兼顾安全性与任务性能，适合多轮对话场景。

中文摘要：基于记忆增强的LLM智能体容易受到记忆注入攻击：智能体可能检索到来自攻击者的中毒记忆，从而使其行为偏离初始用户意图，最终导致任务失败。然而，现有防御机制要么产生高计算成本，要么在多轮上下文中面临信息冗余问题。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了记忆意图感知神经去噪（MIND），一种针对记忆注入攻击的轻量级防御框架。我们的初步分析表明，良性轨迹和中毒轨迹在初始用户意图与后续行为之间表现出可区分的关联。基于这一观察，MIND采用意图感知信息瓶颈（IB）从初始意图和回合级行为中提取紧凑的意图-行为表示。IB保留与意图相关的跨回合攻击信号，同时过滤与任务无关和重复的信息，并且一个轻量级检测器从所得表示中识别恶意记忆。由此，MIND减轻了多轮上下文中的信息冗余，同时避免了重复LLM审计的开销。大量实验表明，MIND在保持任务准确性和推理效率的同时降低了攻击成功率。值得注意的是，在ReAct-StrategyQA上，MIND将平均ASR-r和ASR-a分别降低了55.4%和55.3%，同时在平均准确率和延迟上与未防御的智能体相当。

Original abstract: Memory-augmented LLM-based agents are vulnerable to memory injection attacks: Agents may retrieve poisoned memory from attackers, which diverts their behavior from initial user intent and finally causes task failure. However, existing defense mechanisms either incur high computational cost or suffer from information redundancy in multi-turn contexts. To address these challenges, we propose Memory Intent-Aware Neural Denoising(MIND), a lightweight defense framework for memory injection attack. Our preliminary analysis reveals that benign and poisoned trajectories exhibit distinguishable relationships between the initial user intent and subsequent behavior. Building on this observation, MIND employs an intent-aware Information Bottleneck(IB) to extract compact intent--behavior representations from the initial intent and turn-level behavior. The IB preserves intent-relevant cross-turn attack signals while filtering task-irrelevant and repetitive information, and a lightweight detector identifies malicious memories from the resulting representations. As such, MIND mitigates information redundancy in multi-turn contexts while avoiding the overhead of repeated LLM auditing. Extensive experiments show that MIND reduces attack success rates while preserving task accuracy and inference efficiency. Notably, on ReAct-StrategyQA, MIND reduces mean ASR-r and ASR-a by 55.4% and 55.3%, respectively, while matching the undefended agent in average accuracy and latency.

</details>


### [39] [BlueprintRepair: Typed Local Edits for Failed Lean Proof Blueprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28110)
*Ruslan Khrulev*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 为LLM证明agent提供类型化本地编辑的蓝图修复接口和失败轨迹benchmark，可迁移到coding agent的验证与修复设计。

TL;DR: 提出BlueprintRepair，一种通过十种模式检查的局部操作来修改证明蓝图依赖图的修复接口，并构建了BlueprintTrace基准，证明类型化修复在成本效益上优于源码补丁和模块重写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的不变式证明系统常将证明组织为蓝图，但对蓝图的修改缺乏结构化约束，导致目标定理被意外改变或修复效率低下。需要一种既能自由修改依赖图又能保证目标不变、且成本可控的修复机制。

Method: 定义BlueprintRepair，提供十种模式检查的局部操作，每个操作指定编辑节点并锁定目标定理；利用Lean的类型检查确保每一步修改合法性，且接受的修复必须声明所有使用的蓝图引理。构建BlueprintTrace基准，包含142个受控失败及其完整成功/失败修复轨迹，在相同源码、反馈、模型和预算下对比类型化编辑、精确源码补丁和完整模块重写。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上，三种接口解决的局部失败数几乎相同，但类型化修复每个已解决状态最便宜（补丁1.30倍、重写2.06倍），并在10,000令牌内达到几乎全部覆盖率；Qwen3.6-Flash解决状态较少，但类型化修复仍最便宜，且在证明编写状态上领先。

Conclusion: BlueprintRepair的类型化修复接口在效率上优于自由形式补丁和重写，能以更低成本达到相同覆盖率，是一种有效的证明蓝图修复方式。

中文摘要：基于LLM的Lean证明系统越来越多地将证明组织为蓝图：一个形式化语句的依赖图。我们引入了BlueprintRepair，一个修复接口，让模型通过十种经过模式检查的局部操作来改变这个图。每个操作都指定其编辑的节点，因此目标定理不能被更改。Lean检查每一个应用的更改，并且一个被接受的修复必须声明其证明使用的每一个蓝图引理。我们还构建了BlueprintTrace，一个包含142个受控失败及其完整接受和拒绝修复轨迹的基准。我们在匹配的源码、反馈、模型和预算下，比较了类型化编辑、精确源码补丁和完整模块重写，每个状态和接口一个回合。使用DeepSeek-V4-Flash，三种接口解决的基准局部失败数量几乎相同。类型化修复是每个已解决状态中最便宜的（补丁成本是其1.30倍，重写成本是其2.06倍），并且在每个任务10,000个完成令牌内，它几乎达到了其最终覆盖率的全部，而两种自由形式接口则远远落后。第二个模型Qwen3.6-Flash解决了更少的状态，但保持了类型化修复的最便宜性，使其在证明编写状态上领先，并重复了局部模式。

Original abstract: LLM-based Lean proving systems increasingly organize a proof as a blueprint: a dependency graph of formal statements. We introduce BlueprintRepair, a repair interface that lets a model change this graph through ten schema-checked local operations. An operation names the node it edits, so the target theorem cannot be changed. Lean checks every applied change, and an accepted repair must declare every blueprint lemma its proof uses. We also construct BlueprintTrace, a benchmark of 142 controlled failures with complete accepted and rejected repair trajectories. We compare typed edits, exact source patches, and complete module rewrites under matched source, feedback, model, and budget, one episode per state and interface. With DeepSeek-V4-Flash, the three interfaces solve almost the same number of the benchmark's localized failures. Typed repair is the cheapest per solved state (patching is 1.30x as expensive, rewriting 2.06x), and within 10,000 completion tokens per task it reaches almost all of its final coverage, while both free-form interfaces are well behind. A second model, Qwen3.6-Flash, solves fewer states but keeps typed repair cheapest, puts it ahead on the proof-authoring states, and repeats the localized pattern.

</details>


### [40] [ConMem: Contribution-Aware Memory for Long-Horizon Manufacturing Inspection Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28126)
*Bingchen Liu,Yuanyuan Fang,Lei Liu,Guangyuan Dong,Xing Fu,Yuanyuan Gao,Shuyue Wei,Xin Li,Xiangtian Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出贡献感知记忆框架，用Shapley估算记忆单元价值并在预算下保留高价值证据，可迁移到长程agent的记忆管理。

TL;DR: 提出ConMem，通过贡献感知的记忆管理实现长周期钢铁设备检查中的早期风险筛查，在提升QA准确率的同时大幅降低输入令牌数和响应时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强生成系统将历史日志视为静态语料，未评估记录的诊断价值，导致无法报告早期风险，因此需要一种能够估计记忆贡献并保留高价值证据的框架来支持长周期检查中的早期风险筛查。

Method: ConMem首先将检查日志分割为功能证据单元，然后通过Shapley式估计评估每个记忆单元对下游诊断的贡献，最后在受限内存预算下保留高价值证据，并支持人在回路的早期风险筛查系统。

Result: 在真实数据集上达到76.0%的QA准确率，超过最强直接可比基线；相对朴素8K上下文LLM基线，平均输入令牌数减少88.2%，响应时间减少86.6%；消融实验证明功能角色感知分割和贡献评估有助于优先处理弱退化信号；实际部署中在三个检查周期内保留弱早期信号，提供早期密封磨损警报。

Conclusion: ConMem通过贡献感知的记忆管理显著提升了长周期设备检查中的早期风险检测能力，验证了该方法在工业检查场景中的实用价值，为LLM辅助诊断系统提供了新的设计思路。

中文摘要：长周期钢铁设备检查需要在重复检查周期中积累的异构记录上进行推理。现有的检索增强生成系统将历史日志视为静态语料库，保留记录而不评估其诊断价值，无法报告早期风险。为此，我们提出了ConMem，一种用于LLM辅助设备检查的贡献感知记忆框架，支持人在回路中的早期风险筛查系统。具体而言，我们的ConMem首先将检查日志分割为功能证据单元，然后通过Shapley式估计来估计每个记忆单元对下游诊断的贡献，最后在受限的记忆预算下保留高价值证据。在实验中，我们在真实世界数据集上评估了ConMem，ConMem达到了76.0%的QA准确率，超过了最强的直接可比基线。相对于朴素的8K上下文LLM基线，它将平均输入令牌数量减少了88.2%，响应时间减少了86.6%。消融研究还表明，功能角色感知的分割和基于贡献的评估有助于优先处理弱退化信号，以实现有针对性的现场检查。实际部署进一步证实，ConMem在三个检查周期中保留了微弱的早期信号，为现场检查人员提供了早期密封磨损警报。

Original abstract: Long-horizon steel-equipment inspection requires reasoning over heterogeneous records accumulated across repeated inspection cycles. Existing retrieval-augmented generation systems treat historical logs as a static corpus and retain records without estimating their diagnostic value, failing to report early risk. To this end, we propose ConMem, a contribution-aware memory framework for LLM-assisted equipment inspection, supporting a human-in-the-loop early-risk screening system. Specifically, our ConMem first segments inspection logs into functional evidence units, then estimates each memory unit's contribution to downstream diagnosis through a Shapley-style estimation, and finally retains high-value evidence under a constrained memory budget. In experiments, we evaluate ConMem on real-world dataset and ConMem achieves 76.0% QA accuracy, exceeding the strongest directly comparable baseline. Relative to the naive 8K-context LLM baselines, it reduces the average number of input tokens by 88.2% and response time by 86.6%. Ablation studies also show that the functional-role-aware segmentation and contribution-based valuation are helping prioritize weak degradation signals for targeted field inspection. Practical deployments further confirm that ConMem retains the weak early signal across three inspection cycles, providing an early-stage seal-wear alert targeted for on-site inspectors.

</details>


### [41] [AgenticASR: Refining Speech Recognition in Real-World Scenarios via an Agentic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28175)
*Zixuan Jiang,Binghao Qiang,Jiaying Chi,Yanqiao Zhu,Kai Yu,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出AgenticSR的ASR-Refiner流式持续修订架构，虽是语音领域，但其迭代修订与span替换机制对agent反馈循环有启发。

TL;DR: 提出AgenticASR，一种通过ASR-Refiner架构实现在线语音流中持续修订的意图保持干净转录方法，并引入双语基准AASR-Bench，在多个ASR前端上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐字转录包含填充词、重复和自纠等不流畅内容，增加阅读负担且可能传播错误意图；现有口语转书面方法只能处理完整音频或转录，无法在流式语音到达时根据后续信息修订已输出文本。

Method: 构建AgenticSR任务并设计AgenticASR系统，采用ASR-Refiner架构，对有限活动上下文进行迭代变换并替换对应输出片段，支持任意时长音频流的持续发射与修订；同时提出带细粒度原子评分标准的双语基准AASR-Bench。

Result: 在多个ASR前端上，AgenticASR取得最高AASR-Bench分数；人-AI一致性研究显示评分标准判断与专家评估一致；消融实验揭示了Refiner容量、上下文长度及在线/离线推理的质量-延迟权衡。

Conclusion: AgenticASR为持续语音中保留意图的干净转录提供了实用框架，验证了流式修订的有效性，并发布了代码、基准和演示以推动该方向研究。

中文摘要：自动语音识别（ASR）在转录准确性方面取得了显著进展，但逐字转录并不一定能产生易于使用的文本。它保留了填充词、重复、错误开头和自我修正，这些会增加阅读负担，模糊说话者的最终意图，并将未解决或放弃的内容传播到下游任务。现有的口语到书面语方法处理完整的音频或转录文本，但当后续语音改变了对先前内容的解释方式时，无法修改已输出的文本。因此，我们提出了智能体语音识别（AgenticSR），一项将音频转换为干净文本的任务，它消除不流畅、解决自我修正并规范化书面形式，同时保留说话者的最终意图。AgenticASR 通过 ASR-Refiner 架构实现该任务，该架构在音频到达时反复转换一个有界的活动上下文，并替换其对应的输出片段。这使得在任意时长的流式音频上能够持续输出和修订。我们还引入了 AASR-Bench，一个带有细粒度原子评分标准的双语基准。在多个 ASR 前端上，AgenticASR 在评估系统中取得了最高的 AASR-Bench 分数。人-AI 一致性研究表明，基于评分标准的判断与独立专家评估一致。消融实验表征了 Refiner 容量、上下文长度以及在线和离线推理之间的质量-延迟权衡。总之，这些结果确立了 AgenticASR 作为在持续语音中进行意图保持的干净转录的实用框架。代码、AASR-Bench 和演示将在 https://github.com/AnXMuy/AgenticASR 发布。

Original abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) has achieved substantial gains in transcription accuracy, yet verbatim transcription does not necessarily produce readily usable text. It retains fillers, repetitions, false starts, and self-corrections that increase reading effort, obscure the speaker's final intent, and propagate unresolved or abandoned content to downstream tasks. Existing spoken-to-written methods process completed audio or transcripts but cannot revise emitted text when later speech changes how preceding content should be interpreted. We therefore formulate Agentic Speech Recognition (AgenticSR), an audio-to-clean-text task that removes disfluencies, resolves self-corrections, and normalizes written form while preserving the speaker's final intent. AgenticASR implements this task through an ASR--Refiner architecture that repeatedly transforms a bounded active context and replaces its corresponding output span as audio arrives. This enables continual emission and revision over streams of arbitrary duration. We also introduce AASR-Bench, a bilingual benchmark with fine-grained atomic rubrics. Across multiple ASR front ends, AgenticASR attains the highest AASR-Bench scores among evaluated systems. A human--AI agreement study shows that rubric-based judgments align with independent expert assessments. Ablations characterize Refiner capacity, context length, and the quality--latency trade-off between online and offline inference. Together, these results establish AgenticASR as a practical framework for intent-preserving clean transcription during ongoing speech. Code, AASR-Bench, and a demo will be released at https://github.com/AnXMuy/AgenticASR.

</details>


### [42] [Qwen-UI-Agent Technical Report: Toward Next-Generation Real-World Centric Foundation GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28227)
*Hanzhang Zhou,Panrong Tong,Xu Zhang,Quyu Kong,Chenglin Cai,Tianyu Xia,Gongjie Zhang,Jianan Zhang,Long Li,Long Chen,Lei Wang,Gaole Dai,Pengxiang Li,Liangyu Chen,Yue Wang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 真实世界GUI agent，统一动作空间、长程训练、AutoResearch数据飞轮和在线RL，对软件工程agent的harness与执行有直接参考价值。

TL;DR: Qwen-UI-Agent是一个以真实世界为中心的基础GUI智能体，覆盖移动、电脑、网页和DeepSearch环境，通过统一动作空间、数据飞轮和在线强化学习，在移动使用基准上取得最先进性能，并在电脑和浏览器任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了推动GUI智能体走向实际应用，需要能够在真实设备上可靠运行、跨平台执行工作流、结合GUI和CLI操作、完成长时程任务并自主提升能力的智能体。

Method: 提出了Qwen-UI-Agent，结合多种沙盒环境和真实设备运行时；设计统一动作空间，支持GUI与CLI交错执行和批量动作；采用AutoResearch风格数据飞轮构建任务和环境；使用在线强化学习，支持超过100轮轨迹训练，并利用10,000+并发环境加速；通过轻量级封装层支持主动服务发起和有状态工作流。

Result: 在移动基准上，MobileWorld 82.1%，MobileWorld-Real 92.2%，AndroidDaily 97.5%；电脑使用：OSWorld-Verified 79.5%，OSWorld-v2部分进步得分40.0%；浏览器使用WebArena 73.6%，GUI grounding ScreenSpot-Pro 81.5%。在电脑和浏览器任务上对阵Opus 4.8、Gemini 3.1 Pro和GPT-5.6 Sol取得了有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: Qwen-UI-Agent在移动使用基准上达到最先进水平，同时在电脑和浏览器使用任务上展现出竞争力，证明了其作为现实世界通用GUI智能体的潜力。

中文摘要：GUI智能体有潜力成为现有数字设备上的通用执行器。为了推动它们走向真实世界应用，我们设想智能体能够在真实设备上可靠运行、跨平台执行工作流、将GUI交互与命令行执行相结合、完成长时程任务、主动发起有用的服务，并以最少的人力投入自主提升其能力。在这一愿景的指导下，我们提出了Qwen-UI-Agent，一个以真实世界为中心的基础GUI智能体，覆盖移动、电脑使用、网页和DeepSearch环境。Qwen-UI-Agent将多样化的沙盒环境与大规模真实设备移动运行时相结合。其统一动作空间将GUI操作与命令行执行交错，并在单次模型轮次中生成批量动作。一个AutoResearch风格的数据飞轮使用智能体来构建任务和环境、诊断失败并规划后续迭代。在线强化学习支持对超过100轮次轨迹的训练，超过10,000个并发环境加速数据收集。一个轻量级封装层支持跨移动和电脑的主动服务发起和有状态工作流。在广泛的评估中，Qwen-UI-Agent在移动使用基准上取得了最先进的性能，同时在电脑和浏览器使用任务上对领先模型（包括Opus 4.8、Gemini 3.1 Pro和GPT-5.6 Sol）展现出具有竞争力的性能。在移动使用方面，它在MobileWorld上达到82.1%，在MobileWorld-Real上达到92.2%，在AndroidDaily上达到97.5%。在电脑使用方面，它在OSWorld-Verified上达到79.5%，在OSWorld-v2上获得40.0%的部分进步得分。在浏览器使用和GUI接地方面，它分别达到WebArena 73.6%和ScreenSpot-Pro 81.5%。

Original abstract: GUI agents have the potential to become a general purpose executor over existing digital devices. To advance them toward real-world use, we envision agents that operate reliably on real devices, execute workflows across platforms, combine GUI interaction with CLI execution, complete long-horizon tasks, proactively initiate useful services, and autonomously improve their capabilities with minimal human effort. Guided by this vision, we present Qwen-UI-Agent, a real-world centric foundation GUI agent spanning mobile, computer-use, web, and DeepSearch environments. Qwen-UI-Agent combines diverse sandbox environments with a large-scale real-device mobile runtime. Its unified action space interleaves GUI operations with CLI execution and generates batched actions in a single model turn. An AutoResearch-style data flywheel uses agents to construct tasks and environments, diagnose failures, and plan subsequent iterations. Online RL supports training on trajectories exceeding 100 turns, with over 10,000 concurrent environments accelerating rollout. A lightweight harness layer supports proactive service initiation and stateful workflows across mobile and computer.
  Across a broad suite of evaluations, Qwen-UI-Agent sets state-of-the-art performance on mobile-use benchmarks while delivering competitive performance on computer- and browser-use tasks against frontier models, including Opus 4.8, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and GPT-5.6 Sol. On mobile use, it achieves 82.1% on MobileWorld, 92.2% on MobileWorld-Real, and 97.5% on AndroidDaily. On computer use, it achieves 79.5% on OSWorld-Verified and a 40.0% partial-progress score on OSWorld-v2. On browser use and GUI grounding, it achieves 73.6% on WebArena and 81.5% on ScreenSpot-Pro, respectively.

</details>


### [43] [LLM-Guided Evolutionary Search for Constraint Model Reformulation to Improve Solver Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28268)
*Kostis Michailidis,Dimos Tsouros,Nguyen Dang,Tias Guns*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 用LLM进化搜索自动重表述约束模型以提升求解器效率，是直接针对性能优化的agent式方法，且含多样性保留策略。

TL;DR: 提出用LLM在进化框架中自动重构约束模型以提升性能，并引入PDR策略保留行为多样化的尝试，在CSPLib上验证了显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合问题求解性能对建模选择敏感，即使正确的模型也可能求解昂贵，因此需要自动化性能导向的模型重构。

Method: 受AHD启发，采用进化框架，LLM提出候选重构，验证和基准测试；比较多种上下文保留策略，提出基于MMR的PDR策略以多样化保留尝试；使用验证集选择最终模型。

Result: 迭代重构带来显著留出加速；多样化保留策略优于仅保留最近或最快尝试；验证选择进一步提升所有策略的留出加速。

Conclusion: LLM能有效自动化约束模型的性能导向重构，且上下文多样性对搜索成功至关重要，验证选择是可靠的方法。

中文摘要：组合问题出现在众多工业应用中。一种常见的方法是将这些问题表述为声明式约束模型，随后可编译并由一系列后端求解器求解。近期工作表明，大型语言模型（LLMs）可以从自然语言生成正确的模型，但即使模型正确，求解成本也可能很高，因为性能对建模选择仍然敏感。在这项工作中，我们研究LLMs能否自动化面向性能的模型重构。受自动启发式设计（AHD）启发，我们使用一个进化框架，其中LLM提出候选重构方案，并针对用户定义的基线模型进行验证和基准测试。我们比较了适应AHD的搜索策略，这些策略控制哪些先前的尝试、指令和测量反馈进入每个提示。现有保留策略优先考虑最近性或性能，但并未显式多样化上下文。为弥补这一空白，我们引入了Profile-Diverse Retention（PDR），它将最大边际相关性（MMR）应用于实例级运行时向量，以保留行为多样化的尝试。我们使用基于验证的最终模型选择，系统评估了八个月CSPLib问题上的策略。结果表明：（i）迭代重构可以产生显著的留出集加速；（ii）保持保留上下文多样化的策略优于仅保留最近或最快尝试的策略；（iii）基于验证的选择提高了每种策略的留出集加速。

Original abstract: Combinatorial problems appear in numerous industrial applications. A common approach is to formulate these problems as declarative constraint models that can subsequently be compiled to and solved by a range of back-end solvers. Recent work shows that Large Language Models (LLMs) can produce correct models from natural language, but even a correct model can be expensive to solve because performance remains sensitive to modelling choices. In this work, we investigate whether LLMs can automate performance-oriented model reformulation. Inspired by Automatic Heuristic Design (AHD), we use an evolutionary framework in which an LLM proposes candidate reformulations that are verified and benchmarked against the user-defined baseline model. We compare AHD-adapted search strategies that control which prior attempts, instructions, and measured feedback enter each prompt. Existing retention strategies prioritize recency or performance, but do not explicitly diversify the context. To cover this gap, we introduce Profile-Diverse Retention (PDR), which applies Maximal Marginal Relevance (MMR) to instance-level runtime vectors to retain behaviourally diverse attempts. We systematically evaluate the strategies on eight CSPLib problems using validation-based final model selection. The results show that: (i) iterative reformulation can produce substantial held-out speedups; (ii) strategies that keep the retained context diverse outperform those that retain only recent or the fastest attempts; and (iii) validation-based selection improves the held-out speedup of every strategy.

</details>


### [44] [MemHarness: Memory Is Reconstructed, Not Replayed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28272)
*Rong Wu,Daocheng Fu,Licheng Wen,Xuemeng Yang,Shu Zou,Jianbiao Mei,Yuxin Wang,Hairong Zhang,Yu Yang,Tao Hu,Cong Zhang,Botian Shi,Pinlong Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出记忆重建而非重放的通用agent记忆框架，通过GRPO端到端训练，显著改进了ALFWorld等agent任务，可迁移到coding agent。

TL;DR: MemHarness是一个让LLM代理根据当前情境主动重建而非简单重放过去经验的框架，通过GRPO端到端训练实现，在ALFWorld和WebShop上显著优于基线，并提升OOD鲁棒性和内在推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强代理将检索到的经验作为静态记录直接重放，忽略了存储经验与当前具体状态之间的差距，导致负迁移。人类则倾向于根据当前情境重新组织记忆，受此启发提出MemHarness。

Method: MemHarness框架在每个决策步骤使用统一的策略模型，以当前状态为条件对检索到的经验进行批判和重建，生成上下文相关的指导；重建能力通过端到端的GRPO（组相对策略优化）训练自然涌现。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上，MemHarness显著优于纯强化学习和静态记忆增强基线，在分布外（OOD）场景中表现出强鲁棒性；分析还表明重建目标能防止负迁移并作为潜在训练指导。

Conclusion: MemHarness证明了对记忆的重建而非简单重放能有效提升LLM代理的性能和泛化能力，同时改善内在推理能力，为记忆增强代理提供了新范式。

中文摘要：检索过去的经验已成为增强大语言模型代理的常见策略。然而，大多数现有的记忆增强代理将检索到的经验视为静态记录并逐字重放，无论这些经验是否与代理当前的情况一致，都将其注入上下文中。这种“重放”范式忽略了存储经验的抽象、一般性质与决策时具体、不断变化的状态之间的差距，常常导致负迁移。相比之下，人类很少逐字回忆过去的经验；相反，他们会根据当前情境重新组织和调整检索到的记忆。受此启发，我们提出了MemHarness，一个使LLM代理能够基于当前情境主动利用和重建过去经验的框架。在每个决策步骤，一个统一的策略模型以当前状态为条件，对检索到的经验进行批判和重建，在行动前生成基于上下文的指导。这种重建能力通过端到端的GRPO训练自然涌现。在ALFWorld和WebShop上的实验表明，MemHarness显著优于纯强化学习和静态记忆增强基线，在分布外（OOD）场景中表现出强大的鲁棒性。此外，我们的分析揭示，这种重建目标不仅防止负迁移，还在训练期间作为潜在指导，从根本上提高代理的内在推理能力。

Original abstract: Retrieving past experiences has become a common strategy to enhance large language model agents. However, most existing memory-augmented agents treat retrieved experiences as static records to be replayed verbatim, injecting them into the context regardless of whether they align with the agent's current situation. This ``replay'' paradigm ignores the gap between the abstract, general nature of stored experience and the concrete, ever-changing states encountered at decision time, frequently causing negative transfer. In contrast, humans rarely recall past experiences verbatim; instead, they reorganize and adapt retrieved memories to fit the present context. Inspired by this, we propose MemHarness, a framework that equips LLM agents to actively harness and reconstruct past experiences based on the present context. At each decision step, a unified policy model critiques and reconstructs the retrieved experience conditioned on the current state, producing context-grounded guidance before acting. This reconstructive ability emerges naturally through end-to-end training with GRPO. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show that MemHarness substantially outperforms pure RL and static memory-augmented baselines, demonstrating strong robustness in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. Furthermore, our analyses reveal that this reconstruction objective not only prevents negative transfer but also serves as latent guidance during training, fundamentally improving the agent's intrinsic reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [45] [Tycho: Active Abstraction with Programmatic World Models for ARC-AGI-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28287)
*Jens Lehmann,Andrei Aioanei,Sahar Vahdati*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 编码agent系统在ARC-AGI-3中主动构建、测试、修复程序化世界模型，涉及模型使用成本权衡，对agent长程推理与验证有启发。

TL;DR: 提出 Tycho 系统，将游戏环境形式化为 Moore 机，通过主动抽象策略在 ARC-AGI-3 上达到 100.00 RHAE 并完成全部 183 个关卡，且 Opus 5 比官方人类基线少用 61% 计分动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 ARC-AGI-3 中，抽象是交互式技能获取问题，玩家需在保持动作效率的同时推断规则、隐藏状态和目标；关键挑战是从昂贵交互中生成可测试模型，并决定获取或使用它是否值得其成本，即主动抽象问题。

Method: 将环境形式化为参数化渲染的确定性 Moore 机；Tycho 分离可操作观察与动画/关卡完成/游戏结束帧，使代理能建模、测试、规划、修复或绕过可执行假设；在匹配推理预算下比较四种编排策略。

Result: 在所有 25 个公共游戏上，actor-requested delegation 策略取得最高平均 RHAE 88.49；选定该策略后，GPT-5.6 Sol 和 Opus 5 均达到 100.00 RHAE 并完成全部 183 关，Opus 5 比官方人类基线少用 61% 计分动作；自动修复生成的模型更准确但 RHAE 仅 83.07。

Conclusion: 主动抽象是联合问题：不仅要生成可测试模型，还要决定何时构建、修复、使用或绕过模型；转换匹配只说明模拟器再现了观测动态，并不等同于识别目标或改进动作，需要更全面的策略。

中文摘要：ARC-AGI-3 将抽象转化为一个交互式的技能获取问题。玩家必须推断不熟悉游戏的规则、隐藏状态和目标，同时保持动作效率，因为每一步都至关重要。我们将这些环境形式化为参数化渲染的确定性摩尔机，并引入 Tycho，一个在交互过程中构建和使用游戏特定模型的编码代理系统。Tycho 将可操作观察与中间动画、关卡完成和游戏结束帧分离。通过这种结构化的历史，代理可以对一个自由格式的可执行假设进行建模、测试、规划、修复或绕过。
在每种策略的一次匹配公共集运行中，我们在所有 25 个公共游戏上使用 Claude Opus 4.8 在匹配推理预算下比较了四种编排策略。由执行者请求委托给模型构建器获得了最高的观测平均相对人类动作效率（RHAE），为 88.49。使用该选定策略，GPT-5.6 Sol 和 Opus 5 均达到 100.00 RHAE，并完成所有 183 个关卡。它们的游戏平衡首次运行人类回放中位排名分别为 98.5 和 100.0。Opus 5 使用的计分动作比官方人类基线总计少 61%。
验证失败后的自动修复生成的模型能更准确地再现观察到的转换，但仅达到 83.07 RHAE。转换匹配表示模拟器是否再现了观察到的动态，而不表示它是否已识别目标或改进了下一个动作。强大的玩法还需要决定何时构建、修复、使用或绕过模型。我们将这一联合问题称为主动抽象：从昂贵的交互中生成可测试模型，并决定获取或使用它是否值得其成本。

Original abstract: ARC-AGI-3 turns abstraction into an interactive problem of skill acquisition. A player must infer an unfamiliar game's rules, hidden state, and goal while maintaining action efficiency because every move counts. We formalize these environments as parameterized rendered deterministic Moore machines and introduce Tycho, a coding-agent system that constructs and uses game-specific models during interaction. Tycho separates actionable observations from intermediate animation, level-completion, and game-over frames. From this structured history, an agent can model, test, plan with, repair, or bypass a free-form executable hypothesis.
  In one matched public-set run per policy, we compare four orchestration policies on all 25 public games using Claude Opus 4.8 under matched inference budgets. Actor-requested delegation to a model builder obtains the highest observed mean Relative Human Action Efficiency (RHAE), 88.49. With this selected policy, GPT-5.6 Sol and Opus 5 both reach 100.00 RHAE and complete all 183 levels. Their game-balanced first-run human-replay midranks are 98.5 and 100.0. Opus 5 uses 61% fewer scored actions than the aggregate official human baselines.
  Automatic repair after verification failures produces models that reproduce observed transitions much more accurately, yet reaches only 83.07 RHAE. Transition match indicates whether a simulator reproduces observed dynamics, not whether it has identified the objective or improves the next action. Strong play also requires deciding when to construct, repair, use, or bypass a model. We call this joint problem active abstraction: generating a testable model from costly interaction and deciding when acquiring or using it is worth its cost.

</details>


### [46] [One Human, $N$ Agents: Audit-Budget Allocation for LLM Agent Fleets under Miscalibrated, Correlated Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28317)
*Cesare Zavattari,Alessandro Tommasi,Giuseppe Prencipe*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 研究单人对多LLM agent的审计预算分配与置信度校准，是agent可靠性/安全控制的方法论，可迁移到agent输出审查。

TL;DR: 在预算有限的 LLM 审计中，置信度排序在错误校准超过阈值 δ* 时劣于随机；δ* 随预算减小而增大，且跨家族相关性高。开放权重模型置信度近乎恒定，专有模型则有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类需在有限预算下审计大量 LLM 智能体，但置信度可能被对抗性错误校准且错误相关，需要判断基于置信度的审计是否有效。

Method: 使用两水平高斯 copula 对预算受限的噪声检查建模，推导错误校准阈值 δ*，并通过实验评估不同 LLM 的置信度质量及策略重放。

Result: δ* 随预算减小而上升；跨家族相关性高。五个开放权重 LLM 的置信度操作上无用（近似恒定），点估计接近或超过翻转点但置信区间跨越；专有模型有信息性且低于翻转点。

Conclusion: 给出空洞监督的定量标准；基于置信度的审计仅在模型校准良好时有效，开放权重模型当前不适合此策略。

中文摘要：一个人类审计员必须在每轮预算 B << N 次审计的情况下审计 N 个 LLM 智能体，并依赖于自我报告的置信度（可能被对抗性地错误校准）以及相关错误。我们将此建模为在两水平高斯 copula 上的预算受限噪声检查，并找到了错误校准阈值 δ*，超过该阈值时，按置信度排序的审计比随机审计更差。两个先验预期被反转：δ* 随着预算缩小而上升，并且跨家族相关性并不低——共享难度主导了谱系。五个开放权重 LLM 显示出操作上无用的（近乎恒定的）置信度，点估计位于或超过翻转点，尽管置信区间跨越该点；一个专有模型具有信息性并落在该点之下。我们给出了空洞监督的定量标准，并在记录轨迹上重放策略证实了排序。

Original abstract: A single human must audit $N$ LLM agents under a budget of $B \ll N$ audits per round, guided by self-reported confidence that may be adversarially miscalibrated and by correlated errors. We model this as budgeted noisy inspection over a two-level Gaussian copula and locate the miscalibration threshold $δ^*$ past which confidence-ranked auditing is \emph{worse} than random. Two a-priori expectations reverse: $δ^*$ \emph{rises} as the budget shrinks, and cross-family correlation is not low---shared difficulty dominates lineage. Five open-weight LLMs show operationally useless (near-constant) confidence, point estimates at or beyond the flip though CIs straddle it; a proprietary model is informative and lands below it. We give a quantitative criterion for \emph{vacuous} oversight, and replaying policies on recorded traces confirms the ordering.

</details>


### [47] [Paying for Honesty Without Knowing the Truth: Reputation-Penalty Design for LLM Marketplace Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28330)
*Mingdai Yang,Shicheng Fan,Kejing Yu,Duohao Wang,Li Sun,Hao Peng,Philip S. Yu,Zhiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 设计声誉惩罚机制约束LLM商家agent的欺诈行为，属于自适应agent行为控制与激励设计，可启发可靠agent设计。

TL;DR: 提出CARP和SPARC机制，无需真实信息即可抑制LLM商户的捏造行为，提升消费者福利并实现行为约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM商户在竞争压力下经常捏造产品属性，即使被要求诚实也难以避免；平台无法验证每条声明，只能获得有噪声的投诉信号。

Method: 设计CARP声誉惩罚机制，包含死区（容忍投诉噪声）和状态依赖严重性（防止声誉驱动的检测侵蚀），并与SPARC（字节级代码门控反思机制）结合，使惩罚在行为上可感知。

Result: CARP能压低低评分说谎者的销量而不伤害诚实卖家，与SPARC配合时弥补了大部分消费者福利差距，且相比其他策略实现最佳福利；SPARC使LLM商户在捏造有代价时自我克制，这种绑定效应跨模型存在并有置信区间支持。

Conclusion: 通过惩罚门控自我纠正推理，LLM商户的自利反应而非合规性促使其减少捏造，CARP和SPARC为平台提供了无需真实信息的有效干预方案。

中文摘要：LLM代理日益成为自主商户，自行撰写产品列表，而在竞争压力下，它们会捏造属性以赢得销售。即使在要求诚实的指令下，各种模型在大多数列表中也都会捏造属性。平台显而易见的补救措施——对照事实核实每一条声明——并不可行，因为它只能观察到带有噪声和偏见的投诉信号，而永远无法获得真实情况。我们设计了CARP，一种声誉惩罚机制，其具有容忍投诉噪声的死区，以及对抗声誉驱动的检测侵蚀的状态依赖严重性。CARP不需要产品级真实情况，并且对策略性博弈具有鲁棒性。CARP通过压低低评分说谎者的销量来保护消费者，同时不伤害诚实卖家。与SPARC配合使用时，即使从不访问真实情况，它也能弥补相对于完美信息预言机的大部分消费者福利差距。在我们比较的策略中，它也实现了最佳福利。我们进一步证明，这种实际感受到的惩罚通过SPARC（一种字节级清洁、代码门控的反思机制）在行为上具有约束力：LLM商户在说谎没有代价时会捏造，但当捏造会损害其销量时会自我克制，这是一种自利反应而非合规行为。我们将这种区别追溯到惩罚门控的自我纠正推理，并观察到这种约束力跨模型存在，并附有支持性的置信区间。

Original abstract: LLM agents increasingly act as autonomous merchants that write their own product listings, and under competitive pressure, they fabricate attributes to win sales. Even under instructions to be honest, they fabricate attributes in a majority of listings across models. A platform's obvious remedy---verifying each claim against the truth---is unavailable, because it observes only a noisy, biased complaint signal, never the ground truth. We design CARP, a reputation-penalty mechanism with a deadband that forgives complaint noise and a state-dependent severity that counters reputation-driven detection erosion. CARP requires no product-level ground truth and is robust to strategic gaming. CARP protects consumers by suppressing the sales volume of low-rated liars while sparing honest sellers. Paired with SPARC, it closes most of the consumer-welfare gap relative to a perfect-information oracle, without ever accessing the truth. It also achieves the best welfare of the policies we compare. We further show that this felt penalty becomes behaviorally binding through SPARC, a byte-clean code-gated reflection mechanism: LLM merchants fabricate when lying is free but restrain themselves when fabrication costs them sales, a self-interested response rather than compliance. We trace this distinction to penalty-gated self-correction reasoning, and observe the binding across models, with supporting confidence intervals.

</details>


### [48] [How Benchmarks Mis-Score Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28367)
*Zihan Dong,Zhiyuan Ma,Zekun Wang,Yunqing Li,Zirou Liu,Ruixuan Deng,Qishi Zhan,Rui Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 系统审计计算机使用agent benchmark的评分可靠性，发现15.3%错误判定，对agent评估与验证设计有直接借鉴价值。

TL;DR: 通过审计150条失败轨迹，发现15.3%的FAIL判定有误，并提出CUA评估的可靠性框架与阶段特定设计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CUA基准分数由脆弱的脚本化预言机产生，评估流程存在任务过时、轨迹不完整、评估器误判等问题，导致分数不可靠，需要系统性分析。

Method: 提出可靠性框架，审计五个基准的150条失败轨迹，使用三层诊断分类法分析真实失败原因。

Result: 15.3%的FAIL判定错误，其中10.7%为评估者假阴性，4.7%为任务损坏；真实失败中验证/反馈和规划错误为主。

Conclusion: 连接长时程基准，提出阶段特定的CUA评估设计规则，以提升评估可靠性。

中文摘要：计算机使用代理（CUA）正被部署用于浏览网页和操作桌面软件，然而它们的基准测试分数仍然通常由脆弱的脚本化预言机产生。分数是一个流水线的输出，其中任务可能过时，轨迹可能遗漏决定性的视觉证据，评估者可能拒绝有效的替代方案，汇总报告可能掩盖失败的原因。我们将这些问题组织成一个可靠性框架，涵盖任务构建、轨迹观察、评分和报告。然后我们审计了来自五个网页、企业工作流和桌面控制基准测试的150条公开的失败评分轨迹，发现15.3%的FAIL判定是错误的：10.7%是评估者的假阴性，4.7%是损坏的任务。对于真正的失败，一个三层诊断分类法表明，验证/反馈和规划失败主导了执行/接地错误，而单一的标量成功率无法解释这一点。我们将这些发现与更新的长时程CUA基准测试联系起来，并为CUA评估推导出阶段特定的设计规则。

Original abstract: Computer-use agents (CUA) are being deployed to browse the web and operate desktop software, yet their benchmark scores are still commonly produced by brittle scripted oracles. A score is the output of a pipeline in which tasks can be stale, trajectories can omit decisive visual evidence, evaluators can reject valid alternatives, and aggregate reports can hide the cause of failure. We organize these problems into a reliability framework spanning task construction, trajectory observation, scoring, and reporting. We then audit 150 public failure-scored trajectories from five web, enterprise-workflow, and desktop-control benchmarks, find that 15.3\% of FAIL verdicts are wrong: 10.7\% are evaluator false negatives and 4.7\% are broken tasks. For genuine failures, a three-tier diagnostic taxonomy shows that verification/feedback and planning failures dominate execution/grounding errors, while a single scalar success rate can not explain. We connect these findings to newer long-horizon CUA benchmarks and derive stage-specific design rules for CUA evaluation.

</details>


### [49] [When Specifications Conflict: A Symmetry-Based Framework for Measuring LLM Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28384)
*Tairan Wang,Liang Zhou,Zikang Zhan,Pingchuan Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出测量LLM在冲突规范下偏好的可控框架，对称性设计减少混淆因素，适用于agent规范遵循与冲突消解研究。

TL;DR: 提出一个受控实验框架，用于系统分析LLMs在冲突规范下的偏好，发现形式语言偏好优于自然语言和示例，并扩展到多个领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏可控且可归因的方式分析LLMs如何解决冲突，需要统一的框架。

Method: 通过显式构造冲突规范并采用对称性设计，在数学基准和多个领域上比较不同表示类型下的模型选择。

Result: 模型表现出系统性偏好，排序为形式语言≈自然化形式语言>纯自然语言>输入-输出示例，且示例效应受模型能力和函数家族影响。

Conclusion: 该框架提供统一方法衡量LLMs解决冲突的偏好，适用于多种任务和规范形式。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLMs）日益需要整合可能不一致或相互冲突的多种信息源。然而，目前仍缺乏可控制且可归因的方法来分析模型如何解决竞争性规范之间的冲突。我们提出了一个受控实验框架，用于研究模型在冲突规范下的偏好。通过构建具有明确冲突的规范，该框架能够直接观察和分析模型在竞争性规范之间的选择。基于对称性的设计进一步减少了混杂因素，使得能够系统地比较不同表示类型之间的偏好。我们在一个可执行的数学基准上评估了该框架，该基准包含11个函数家族的550个冲突实例，比较了四种表示类型：纯自然语言、形式语言、自然化形式语言和输入-输出示例。结果显示系统性的偏好模式而非随机行为，并呈现一致的排序：形式语言≈自然化形式语言>纯自然语言>输入-输出示例。示例效应还取决于模型能力和函数家族。我们将框架扩展到布尔代数、代码生成和临床领域的异构规范冲突，展示了其在不同任务和规范形式中的适用性。该框架为衡量LLMs如何解决竞争性信息源之间的冲突提供了一种统一的方法。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly required to integrate multiple sources of information that may be inconsistent or conflicting. However, there is still a lack of controllable and attributable methods for analyzing how models resolve conflicts between competing specifications. We propose a controlled experimental framework for studying model preferences under conflicting specifications. By constructing specifications with explicit conflicts, the framework enables model choices between competing specifications to be directly observed and analyzed. A symmetry-based design further reduces confounding factors, allowing preferences across representation types to be compared systematically.
  We evaluate the framework on an executable mathematical benchmark with 550 conflict instances spanning 11 function families, comparing four representation types: pure natural language, formal language, naturalized formal language, and input--output examples. Results show systematic preference patterns rather than random behavior, with a consistent ordering: $ \text{Formal} \approx \text{Naturalized Formal} > \text{Pure Natural Language} > \text{Input--Output Examples} $. Example effects further depend on model capability and function family.
  We extend the framework to heterogeneous specification conflicts in Boolean algebra, code generation, and the clinical domain, demonstrating its applicability across diverse tasks and specification forms. The framework provides a unified approach for measuring how LLMs resolve conflicts between competing sources of information.

</details>


### [50] [GLM-RAG: Graph Language Models for Graph-Based Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28397)
*Maya Arseven,Anette Frank,Beni Egressy,Johann Higl,Moritz Plenz*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 图语言模型用于图RAG检索，分析GLM/GNN/向量检索的跨域泛化，可迁移到agent的检索增强组件设计。

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图语言模型的检索器，并系统比较了GLM、GNN和向量搜索检索器在单跳/多跳RAG中的表现，发现微调GLM检索器在域外泛化最佳，达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG检索器难以同时捕捉图结构和语义信息，需要探索更好的检索器范式。

Method: 引入基于GLM的检索器，并与GNN检索器和向量搜索检索器进行对比，评估在单跳和多跳RAG中的性能及跨域迁移性。

Result: 微调GLM检索器在两个多跳基准上取得SOTA，域外泛化好；GNN检索器图覆盖率高且训练高效；向量搜索在单跳数据集上最优。

Conclusion: GLM检索器在多跳RAG和域外泛化上具有优势，GNN和向量搜索各有适用场景，为RAG检索器选择提供了参考。

中文摘要：基于知识图谱的检索增强生成（RAG）需要能够有效捕获图结构和语义信息的检索器。近期方法探索了基于图神经网络（GNN）的检索器，以在多跳推理任务中对图拓扑进行建模。与此同时，图语言模型（GLM）作为一种有前景的范式出现，它整合了图推理和语言模型的语义能力。在这项工作中，我们引入了一种基于GLM的检索器，并研究了基于GLM、基于GNN和传统基于向量搜索的检索器在单跳和多跳RAG设置中的相对优势，特别关注对未见领域的可迁移性。我们的研究结果表明，微调后的GLM检索器在域外具有更好的泛化能力，在两个多跳基准上达到了最先进的性能。在域内多跳问答数据集上，它们与先前工作相当，并且随着参数和子图覆盖率的增加展现出有前景的扩展性。基于GNN的检索器通过高效的训练设置实现了更高的图覆盖率，而基于向量搜索的基线在单跳数据集上表现出色。

Original abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over knowledge graphs requires retrievers that can effectively capture both graph structure and semantic information. Recent approaches have explored graph neural network (GNN)-based retrievers to model graph topology in multi-hop reasoning tasks. In parallel, graph language models (GLMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm that integrates graph reasoning and the semantic capabilities of language models. In this work, we introduce a GLM-based retriever and investigate the comparative strengths of GLM-based, GNN-based, and traditional vector-search-based retrievers in single- and multi-hop RAG settings, and with a particular focus on transferability to unseen domains. Our findings suggest that finetuned GLM retrievers generalize better out of domain, achieving SOTA on two multi-hop benchmarks. On in-domain multi-hop QA datasets they remain comparable to prior work, with promising scaling as parameters and subgraph coverage increase. GNN-based retrievers achieve higher graph coverage with an efficient training setup, whereas the vector-search baseline excels at single-hop datasets.

</details>


### [51] [SVR: Self-Verifying Refinement via Joint Verdict-Confidence Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Test-Time Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28457)
*Hongyu Chen,Liang Lin,Guangrun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: SVR 用自验证与置信度作为自适应测试时计算控制策略，属于可迁移到 coding agent 的自我改进/可靠性一般方法。

TL;DR: SVR 是一个无外部反馈的多轮强化学习框架，通过自验证作为计算控制策略，在数学推理上以更少的推理轮次达到更高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时计算扩展方法要么使用统一预算浪费计算，要么依赖外部验证器反馈，缺乏内部可控的自适应机制。

Method: 提出 Self-Verifying Refinement (SVR)，每轮生成解决方案、正确性判定和置信度，仅在判定为正确且置信度超阈值时停止；使用 GRPO 在固定时域轨迹上训练，奖励设计包含正确性、校准感知的自验证和可停止正确状态，推理时启用自适应停止。

Result: 在七个数学基准上，SVR 达到 0.563 宏平均准确率，平均仅需 2.99 轮推理，优于标准 GRPO、强多轮基线及固定预算 oracle 引导方法，且所需轮次远少于固定十轮。

Conclusion: 学习到的自验证可作为有效的内部控制信号，实现答案保留和自适应测试时计算分配，无需外部反馈。

中文摘要：扩展测试时计算可以改善语言模型的推理能力，但统一的预算会在简单输入上浪费计算，而验证器引导的细化则依赖外部反馈。我们引入了自验证细化（SVR），一个无 oracle 的多轮强化学习框架，学习将自验证用作计算控制策略。在每一轮，模型生成一个解决方案，同时附带一个离散的正确性判定和一个置信度分数；仅当判定为“正确”且置信度超过阈值时，它才保留当前答案，否则利用自身的自验证继续进行细化。真实正确性仅用于构建训练奖励，从不通过细化提示暴露给策略，也不在推理时要求提供。SVR 在固定时域轨迹上使用 GRPO 进行训练，奖励促进解决方案正确性、校准感知的自验证以及可停止的正确状态；自适应停止仅在推理时激活。在七个数学推理基准上使用 Qwen3.5-2B，SVR 实现了 0.563 的宏平均准确率，平均仅需 2.99 个推理轮次。在评估的完整系统比较中，它超过了标准 GRPO、强大的多轮基线和固定预算的 oracle 引导分数反馈参考，同时所需轮次远少于固定的十轮推理。这些结果表明，学习到的自验证可以作为有效的内部控制信号，用于答案保留和自适应测试时计算分配。

Original abstract: Scaling test-time computation can improve language-model reasoning, but uniform budgets waste computation on easy inputs, while verifier-guided refinement relies on external feedback. We introduce Self-Verifying Refinement (SVR), an oracle-free multi-turn reinforcement learning framework that learns to use self-verification as a compute-control policy. At each turn, the model produces a solution together with a discrete correctness verdict and a confidence score; it retains the current answer only when the verdict is Correct and confidence exceeds a threshold, and otherwise continues refinement using its own self-verification. Ground-truth correctness is used only to construct training rewards and is never exposed to the policy through refinement prompts or required at inference. SVR is trained with GRPO on fixed-horizon trajectories using rewards that promote solution correctness, calibration-aware self-verification, and stop-ready correct states; adaptive stopping is activated only at inference. On seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks with Qwen3.5-2B, SVR achieves a macro-average accuracy of 0.563 with only 2.99 inference turns on average. In the evaluated complete-system comparison, it exceeds standard GRPO, strong multi-turn baselines, and a fixed-budget oracle-guided score-feedback reference while requiring substantially fewer turns than fixed ten-turn inference. These results demonstrate that learned self-verification can serve as an effective internal control signal for answer retention and adaptive test-time compute allocation.

</details>


### [52] [MANTA: Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation for Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28527)
*Mao-xun Huang,Jerry Wang,Yi-Cheng Lai,Zhengxin Zhang,Claire Cardie,Hen-Hsen Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: MANTA 在推理时自适应演化多 agent 通信拓扑，多 agent 协作架构可迁移到 coding agent 工作流。

TL;DR: MANTA提出在推理时动态调整多智能体系统的通信拓扑，通过监控协作痕迹进行有界结构更新，在五个基准上取得最高平均分74.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统将通信拓扑视为固定设计或离线优化目标，缺乏在推理时根据任务动态调整组织结构的能力。

Method: 初始化任务条件化拓扑，部署时监控协作痕迹，并在当前组织不足时进行有界结构更新，可修改角色、链接、顺序、可见性和验证路径。

Result: 在五个基准上最高平均分74.0，比最强基线高5.8个百分点，PlanCraft最佳。

Conclusion: 推理时自我改进可扩展至协作本身的结构，表明多智能体系统的架构也能在部署中自适应演化。

中文摘要：基于大语言模型的多智能体系统通过任务分解、智能体专业化、信息交换和中间验证来改善复杂问题求解。然而，现有系统通常将通信拓扑视为固定的设计选择或离线优化目标。我们提出MANTA，一个用于多智能体网络拓扑自适应（Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation）的框架，使通信结构能够在推理时自我演化。在执行之前，MANTA从先前的结构经验中初始化一个任务条件化拓扑。在部署期间，它监控协作轨迹，并在当前组织变得不足时应用有界的结构更新。这些更新可以修改智能体角色、通信链接、执行顺序、信息可见性和验证路径，同时保留任务接口和智能体预算。我们在五个涵盖信息寻求、工具使用、规划、工作流执行和数学推理的基准上，将MANTA与具有代表性的单智能体和多智能体基线进行了评估。MANTA取得了74.0的最高平均分，比最强基线高出5.8个百分点，并在PlanCraft上取得了最佳结果。这些结果表明，推理时的自我改进可以扩展到协作本身的结构。

Original abstract: Large language model-based multi-agent systems improve complex problem solving through task decomposition, agent specialization, information exchange, and intermediate validation. However, existing systems typically treat communication topology as a fixed design choice or an offline optimization target. We introduce MANTA, a framework for Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation that enables communication structures to self-evolve at inference time. Before execution, MANTA initializes a task-conditioned topology from prior structural experience. During deployment, it monitors collaboration traces and applies bounded structural updates when the current organization becomes insufficient. These updates can modify agent roles, communication links, execution order, information visibility, and validation pathways while preserving the task interface and agent budget. We evaluate MANTA against representative single-agent and multi-agent baselines on five benchmarks spanning information seeking, tool use, planning, workflow execution, and mathematical reasoning. MANTA achieves the highest average score of 74.0, outperforming the strongest baseline by 5.8 percentage points and obtaining the best result on PlanCraft. These results show that inference-time self-improvement can extend to the architecture of collaboration itself.

</details>


### [53] [Rethinking Inference-Time Scaling in Local Computer-Use Agents: Failure Modes and Compute Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28573)
*Woongkyu Lee,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 本地计算机使用 agent 的推理时扩展系统实证，揭示了失败模式与计算分配权衡，对 coding agent 设计有直接启发。

TL;DR: 系统研究了本地计算机使用智能体在推理时扩展的四种维度，发现额外计算收益有限且失败模式改变，需选择性计算分配和感知失败控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地部署自主计算机使用智能体对隐私、成本效率和实用性日益重要，但在严格硬件约束下提升其性能仍具挑战。现有推理时扩展研究主要针对前沿模型，对资源受限的本地模型效果尚不明确。

Method: 在OSWorld基准上，对Qwen3-VL-8B/30B-A3B、UI-TARS-1.5-7B和OpenCUA-7B进行系统的实证研究，从上下文、时间、结构和并行四个维度分析推理时扩展的影响。

Result: 额外计算常导致收益递减并改变失败模式；上下文扩展可提升轨迹稳定性和任务准确性但收益饱和，时间扩展减少停滞但不提升成功率，结构分解引入额外开销，并行扩展部分缓解失败但计算成本高。

Conclusion: 高效的本地CUA需要选择性计算分配、感知失败的控制机制以及适配本地模型能力与局限的智能体框架。

中文摘要：在本地部署自主计算机使用智能体（CUAs）对于隐私、成本效益和实际可用性日益重要，但如何在严格的硬件约束下提高其性能仍然具有挑战性。尽管近期研究表明，推理时扩展可以通过执行期间的额外计算来改进前沿计算机使用智能体，但其对资源受限的本地模型的有效性仍知之甚少。我们对本地CUA在上下文、时间、结构和并行维度上的推理时扩展进行了系统的实证研究。我们在OSWorld基准上评估了Qwen3-VL-8B/30B-A3B、UI-TARS-1.5-7B和OpenCUA-7B。结果表明，额外的计算往往产生递减的回报，同时改变失败模式。上下文扩展提供历史基础，改善轨迹稳定性和任务准确性，但其收益随着token成本增加而饱和，失败模式从重复或停滞轨迹转向过早的虚假成功。时间扩展同样减少了最大步数停滞，但并未显著提高任务成功率，表明更长的视野往往延长错误轨迹而非纠正它们。我们进一步发现，结构分解可能给本地两阶段智能体带来规划和格式化开销，而并行扩展以大量计算成本部分缓解了这些失败。总体而言，我们的发现表明，高效的本地CUA需要选择性计算分配、感知失败的控制机制，以及围绕本地模型的能力和局限设计的智能体框架。

Original abstract: Deploying autonomous computer-use agents (CUAs) locally is increasingly important for privacy, cost efficiency, and practical usability, yet improving their performance under strict hardware constraints remains challenging. While recent studies show that inference-time scaling can improve frontier computer-use agents through additional computation during execution, its effectiveness for resource-constrained local models remains poorly understood. We present a systematic empirical study of inference-time scaling in local CUAs across contextual, temporal, structural, and parallel dimensions. We evaluate Qwen3-VL-8B/30B-A3B, UI-TARS-1.5-7B, and OpenCUA-7B on the OSWorld benchmark. Our results show that additional computation often yields diminishing returns while changing failure modes. Contextual scaling provides historical grounding that improves trajectory stability and task accuracy, but its gains saturate as token cost increases and failures shift from repetitive or stalled trajectories toward premature false successes. Temporal scaling similarly reduces max-step stalls, yet does not substantially improve task success, indicating that longer horizons often extend erroneous trajectories rather than correct them. We further find that structural decomposition can introduce planning and formatting overhead in local two-stage agents, while parallel scaling partially mitigates these failures at a substantial computational cost. Overall, our findings suggest that efficient local CUAs require selective compute allocation, failure-aware control mechanisms, and agentic frameworks designed around the capabilities and limitations of local models.

</details>


### [54] [DualG-MRAG: Decoupling Macro-Reasoning and Micro-Matching for Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28580)
*Jiacheng Tao,Qingyun Sun,Haonan Yuan,Ziwei Zhang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: DualG-MRAG 将检索中的宏观推理与微观匹配解耦，作为 RAG/检索方法对检索增强 coding agent 有潜在迁移价值。

TL;DR: DualG-MRAG提出双层次解耦图框架，分别进行宏观推理与微观匹配，通过GNN检索器和动态规划解码提升多模态RAG的多跳推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MM-RAG方法在复杂多跳推理中表现不佳，主要因为独立实例匹配缺乏跨模态关系，而图增强方法面临细粒度视觉特征导致图爆炸和噪声，粗粒度表示丢失局部证据的两难困境。

Method: 构建宏观图进行全局拓扑路由和微观图进行局部精确验证，通过查询驱动的GNN消息传递实现动态相关性传播，并利用动态规划解码从GNN前向传播中提取显式推理路径作为生成输入。

Result: 在证据召回率和复杂问答准确率上均超越基线方法。

Conclusion: DualG-MRAG通过解耦宏观与微观图，有效平衡了结构推理与证据细节，为多模态RAG提供了一种可行的增强方案。

中文摘要：尽管多模态检索增强生成（MM-RAG）已展现出令人鼓舞的结果，但它在处理复杂的多跳推理任务时仍然面临困难。现有方法主要关注独立的实例级匹配，往往无法捕获跨模态和跨文档的显式关系。尽管图增强方法引入了结构建模，但它们在多模态场景中面临一个根本性挑战：融入细粒度视觉特征会导致图快速膨胀和检索噪声，而粗粒度表示则会导致关键局部证据被丢弃。为解决这一困境，我们提出了DualG-MRAG，一个双层次框架，为多模态RAG引入了解耦架构，包含宏观推理图和微观匹配图。具体而言，为了通过将全局结构推理与细粒度证据匹配相隔离来抑制检索噪声，我们构建了用于全局拓扑路由的宏观图和用于精确局部验证的微观图。随后，为了实现跨异构证据源的动态相关性传播，我们通过GNN检索器将检索表述为查询驱动的消息传递过程。此外，为了给生成模型提供连贯的结构指导，我们引入了一种动态规划解码机制，直接从GNN的前向传播中提取显式推理路径，取代了孤立文档块的标准输入。大量实验表明，DualG-MRAG在证据召回率和复杂问答准确性方面均优于基线方法。

Original abstract: While Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MM-RAG) has shown promising results, it still struggles with complex multi-hop reasoning tasks. Existing methods primarily focus on independent instance-level matching, which often fails to capture explicit relationships across modalities and documents. Although Graph-enhanced methods introduce structural modeling, they face a fundamental challenge in multimodal scenarios: incorporating fine-grained visual features leads to rapid graph expansion and retrieval noise, whereas coarse-grained representations cause the discarding of critical local evidence. To address this dilemma, we propose DualG-MRAG, a Dual-tier framework that introduces a decoupled architecture comprising Macro-reasoning and Micro-matching Graphs for Multimodal RAG. Specifically, to suppress retrieval noise by isolating global structural reasoning from fine-grained evidence matching, we construct a Macro Graph for global topological routing and a Micro Graph for precise local verification. Subsequently, to enable dynamic relevance propagation across heterogeneous evidence sources, we formulate retrieval as a query-driven message passing process via a GNN Retriever. Furthermore, to provide the generative model with coherent structural guidance, we introduce a dynamic programming decoding mechanism that extracts explicit reasoning paths directly from the GNN's forward pass, replacing the standard input of isolated document chunks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DualG-MRAG outperforms baselines in both evidence recall and complex QA accuracy.

</details>


### [55] [OSReward: Instituting Standardized Evaluation for Cross-Platform Computer-Use Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28609)
*Qiushi Sun,Kanzhi Cheng,Yian Wang,Bowen Yang,Hang Yan,Liheng Chen,Fangzhi Xu,Zichen Ding,Nuo Chen,Jialin Cao,Xingdong Gong,Zehao Li,Kaiming Jin,Xinfeng Yuan,Zhoumianze Liu,Jingyang Gong,Zhangyue Yin,Jiahui Gao,Zhiyong Wu,Tianbao Xie,Jianbing Zhang,Ben Kao,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: OSReward 构建 CUA 轨迹奖励模型评测基准并训练开源判分模型，对 agent 评测与可靠奖励信号设计有显著参考价值。

TL;DR: 提出了OSReward基准来评估VLM评判者对CUA轨迹的可靠性，发现其存在系统性宽松偏差，并训练了低成本可靠的OS-Shepherd奖励模型以匹配商业评判者性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CUA轨迹验证是评估、数据整理和强化学习的关键，但人工验证无法规模化，而VLM评判者的可靠性尚未被系统检验。

Method: 构建了包含多平台真实轨迹和人工标注的OSReward基准，派生困难子集OSReward-Hard和多维度评分子集OSReward-Multi；在此基础上构建OS-Shepherd-100K语料库，训练开放奖励模型OS-Shepherd（9B和35B）。

Result: 最先进的VLM评判者也存在宽松偏差，将失败误判为成功；可靠模型成本高昂，开放模型性能落后，而OS-Shepherd以比前沿模型低30-60%的成本达到商业评判者水平。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了VLM评判者的可靠性缺陷，并证明开放奖励模型能以低成本提供可靠的CUA轨迹奖励信号，为大规模CUA评估提供了实用方案和设计洞见。

中文摘要：计算机使用智能体（CUA）正在数字世界中快速发展。CUA轨迹记录了智能体的动作、状态和推理过程。验证其是否完成了任务指令是CUA评估、数据整理和强化学习的核心。无论是人工编写的验证器还是人工标注者，都无法大规模提供此类验证，因此该领域越来越倾向于使用视觉语言模型（VLM）作为CUA轨迹的评判者。但一个基本问题长期以来未被审视：这些VLM评判者是否足够可靠？为了系统地研究这一问题，我们引入了OSReward，一个真实、高质量的基准，用于评估VLM评判者对CUA轨迹的判断。这些轨迹来自多种智能体骨干在不同平台上执行经过人工验证的指令，然后通过多阶段人工标注严格标注出真实结论。在此基础上，我们构建了OSReward-Hard，一个集中真正困难案例的挑战集，以及OSReward-Multi，用于细粒度的效率和一致性评分。迄今为止最全面的VLM评判者评估发现，即使是最先进的模型也达不到理想评判者的水平，它们普遍存在宽松偏差，将失败运行错误标记为成功。少数可靠到值得信任的模型在大规模运行时成本过高，而价格可承受的开放模型则远远落后。为了弥补这一差距，我们构建并发布了OS-Shepherd-100K，一个面向CUA社区、带有推理标注的轨迹判断开放语料库。在此基础上，我们训练了OS-Shepherd（9B和35B），这些开放奖励模型提供低成本、稳定且可靠的奖励信号，以比前沿模型低30-60%的成本达到商业评判者的水平。广泛的分析进一步为大规模可靠CUA奖励的设计提供了信息。我们的代码、基准、数据集和模型检查点可在 https://os-copilot.github.io/OSReward-Home/ 获取。

Original abstract: Computer-using agents (CUAs) are advancing rapidly across the digital world. A CUA trajectory records the agent's actions, states, and reasoning. Verifying whether it fulfilled the task instruction is central to CUA evaluation, data curation, and reinforcement learning. Neither human-written verifiers nor human annotators can provide such verification at scale, so the field increasingly turns to vision-language models (VLMs) as judges of CUA trajectories. But a fundamental question has long gone unexamined: are these VLM judges reliable enough? To study it systematically, we introduce OSReward, a realistic, high-quality benchmark that evaluates VLM judges on CUA trajectories. The trajectories come from diverse agent backbones executing human-verified instructions across platforms, then rigorously labeled with ground-truth verdicts through multi-stage human annotation. Building on it, we derive OSReward-Hard, a challenge set concentrating genuinely hard cases, and OSReward-Multi for fine-grained efficiency and alignment scoring. The most comprehensive evaluation of VLM judges to date finds even state-of-the-art models fall short of an ideal judge, sharing a systematic leniency bias that mislabels failed runs as successes. The few reliable enough to trust are too expensive to run at scale, while affordable open models trail far behind. To close this gap, we construct and release OS-Shepherd-100K, an open corpus of reasoning-annotated trajectory judgments for the CUA community. On it, we train OS-Shepherd (9B and 35B), open reward models that supply low-cost, stable, and reliable reward signals, matching commercial judges at 30-60% lower cost than the frontier. Extensive analyses further inform the design of reliable CUA reward at scale. Our code, benchmark, dataset, and model checkpoints are available at https://os-copilot.github.io/OSReward-Home/.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [56] [OwlPath: Lossless Knowledge Compression for LLM Bug Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27249)
*Bo Zhang,Ren Pan,Huan Chen,Xiang Song*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: OwlPath通过OWL2本体和SPARQL推理，将代码结构压缩为知识图谱，显著提升LLM智能体的多跳代码检索效率，在减少token和运行时间的同时提高召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码智能体受限于上下文窗口，标准检索将代码视为纯文本，难以高效处理多跳依赖（如子类链、传递调用者、接口实现），导致缓慢的试错和低效的检索。

Method: OwlPath基于CodeGraph构建OWL2推理层，利用tree-sitter解析多语言代码并编码为统一OWL2本体；采用两个模块：传递闭包引擎通过SPARQL属性路径查询直接获取所有结构关联符号，OWL-SKM预计算紧凑的3KB摘要以快速定位目标模块。

Result: 在18个SWE-bench Pro实例上，严格应用率68.4% vs 66.7%，token减少28.8%，运行时间减少39.5%；在67个离线检索实例上召回率0.464 vs 0.226（2.06倍），命中率88.1% vs 59.7%；在37题基准上召回率从4.4%提升至28.8%，传递调用者和接口任务准确率69%-80%。

Conclusion: OwlPath通过无损知识压缩和OWL2推理，有效解决了LLM智能体的结构检索瓶颈，显著提升多跳依赖解析的准确性和效率，为代码智能平台提供了可扩展的解决方案。

中文摘要：基于大语言模型的软件工程智能体受限于有限的上下文窗口：大约10万token必须存储结构相关的代码子集以解决缺陷。标准检索模型将代码视为纯文本，迫使智能体通过缓慢的试错来解析多跳依赖，包括子类链、传递调用者和接口实现。我们通过无损知识压缩来应对这一限制，将源代码编码为OWL2本体，以使用最小的相关代码片段回答结构查询。我们提出了OwlPath，一个构建在CodeGraph之上的OWL2推理层，CodeGraph是一个广泛使用的代码智能平台，拥有超过50万GitHub星标，提供统一的结构化代码检索命令行界面。凭借tree-sitter解析，OwlPath支持多语言仓库（Python、JavaScript、TypeScript、Go等），并将语言特定语义编码为统一的OWL2本体。它采用两个互补模块。首先，传递闭包引擎通过单个SPARQL属性路径查询获取所有结构关联符号，捕获字符串匹配遗漏的多跳关系。其次，OWL软件知识图谱（OWL-SKM）预计算一个紧凑的3KB摘要，包含模块树、核心API和与问题相关的符号，指导智能体在首次查询中定位目标模块。在18个SWE-bench Pro实例上的评估中，OwlPath的严格应用率为68.4%，而CodeGraph基线为66.7%，同时将token使用量减少28.8%，运行时间减少39.5%。在67个实例的离线检索测试中，OwlPath将召回率提高了2.06倍（0.464对0.226），命中率达到88.1%，而CodeGraph为59.7%。在一个37道题的结构化检索基准测试中，召回率从4.4%提升至28.8%，在传递调用者和接口任务上的准确率为69%-80%。

Original abstract: LLM-based software engineering agents are constrained by limited context windows: roughly 100K tokens must store structurally relevant code  subsets to resolve bugs. Standard retrieval models treat code as plain text, forcing agents to resolve multi-hop dependencies including subclass  chains, transitive callers and interface implementations through slow trial and error. We tackle this limitation with lossless knowledge  compression, encoding source code into an OWL2 ontology to answer structural queries using minimal relevant code fragments.
  We present OwlPath, an OWL2 reasoning layer atop CodeGraph, a widely used code intelligence platform with 500K+ GitHub stars, offering a unified  CLI for structural code retrieval. Powered by tree-sitter parsing, OwlPath supports multi-language repositories (Python, JavaScript,  TypeScript, Go, etc.) and encodes language-specific semantics into a unified OWL2 ontology. It adopts two complementary modules. First, a  transitive-closure engine fetches all structurally linked symbols via single SPARQL property-path queries, capturing multi-hop relations missed  by string matching. Second, the OWL Software Knowledge Map (OWL-SKM) precomputes a compact 3KB summary with module trees, core APIs and  issue-related symbols, directing agents to target modules in the first query.
  Evaluated on 18 SWE-bench Pro instances, OwlPath obtains a 68.4% strict-apply rate versus 66.7% for the CodeGraph baseline, cutting token usage  by 28.8% and runtime by 39.5%. In offline retrieval tests over 67 instances, OwlPath improves recall 2.06 times (0.464 vs 0.226) and reaches  88.1% hit rate compared to CodeGraph's 59.7%. On a 37-question structural retrieval benchmark, recall rises from 4.4% to 28.8%, with 69-80%  accuracy on transitive caller and interface tasks.

</details>


### [57] [Do Context Files Help Coding Agents? A Two-Agent Ablation Study on Real Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27250)
*Prakhar Khatri*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 持久化上下文文件不会显著提升AI编码代理的正确性；失败主因是实现技能而非知识缺失，且任务难度因代理而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGENTS.md等上下文文件被广泛使用，但关于其有效性的证据相互矛盾，缺乏严格的受控消融研究，因此本文设计了严格控制实验来检验其真实影响。

Method: 对Claude Code和Codex两个代理进行上下文注入策略的受控消融；使用3个仓库的17个真实任务（15共享+2仅Codex）和288次评估运行，采用黄金测试评估，并结合等价性检验、失败模式分诊及操作探针。

Result: 上下文策略在任一代理上均未使正确性产生可测量变化（等价性上界≤10-15pp）；失败模式为实现技能（功能设计、模式选择、连接），而非知识缺失；真实的AGENTS.md不能将接近失败转为通过；临界难度与代理相关（Spearman rho=0.75）。

Conclusion: 先前矛盾可能源于单代理研究使用了不同代理的信息带；本文公开代码、数据和全部分析，呼吁更严谨的上下文策略评估。

中文摘要：持久化上下文文件（AGENTS.md、CLAUDE.md）是指导AI编码代理的标准实践，然而其有效性的证据相互矛盾。我们对两个前沿代理（Claude Code和Codex）进行了上下文注入策略的受控消融实验，涉及来自3个代码库的17个真实任务（15个共享+2个仅Codex），以及288次带有黄金测试评估的评估运行。上下文策略在任一代理上都没有可测量的正确性提升（通过等价性检验限定在<=10-15个百分点）。失败模式分诊揭示了原因：代理失败在于实现技能——功能设计、模式选择、精确连接——而非上下文文件可以提供缺失的代码库知识；操作探针证实，真实的AGENTS.md在任一代理上都不能将接近成功转化为通过。我们进一步表明，临界任务难度是代理特定的（Spearman rho=0.75），这为先前的矛盾提供了一个候选解释：单代理研究从不同代理的信息带中抽取任务。我们公开了所有代码、数据和分析。

Original abstract: Persistent context files (AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md) are standard practice for guiding AI coding agents, yet evidence for their effectiveness is contradictory. We present a controlled ablation of context-injection strategy across two frontier agents (Claude Code and Codex), 17 real tasks from 3 repositories (15 shared + 2 Codex-only), and 288 evaluated runs with gold-test evaluation. Context strategy does not measurably move correctness on either agent (bounded to <=10-15pp via equivalence testing). A failure-mode triage reveals why: agents fail on implementation skill---feature design, pattern selection, exact wiring---not missing repository knowledge that a context file could supply; a manipulation probe confirms the real AGENTS.md never converts a near-miss to a pass on either agent. We further show that borderline task difficulty is agent-specific (Spearman rho=0.75), offering a candidate explanation for prior contradictions: single-agent studies draw tasks from different agents' informative bands. We release all code, data, and analysis.

</details>


### [58] [AgentS4D: Benchmarking Runtime Risks across the Execution Lifecycle of LLM-Based Workspace Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27294)
*Jiajun Zhou,Zhaoxuan Ke,Jihang Ye,Xuanze Chen,Shanqing Yu,Qi Xuan*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出 AgentS4D，一个基于四维框架的沙盒基准，通过 328 个风险注入用例和 6,560 次运行评估 LLM 智能体运行时安全，发现 68% 触发不安全信号，且任务完成与安全脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体安全基准缺乏对风险来源、诱导策略、目标危害和执行证据的统一刻画，难以全面评估生命周期中的运行时安全。

Method: 构建四维运行时安全框架，包含六个风险进入来源、六种诱导策略、九种目标危害和七个生命周期检查点；开发 AgentS4D 基准，含 328 个风险注入用例，并组合 4 种框架与 5 种 LLM 后端进行大规模评估。

Result: 在 6,560 次运行中，68.0% 触发不安全信号；66.22% 不安全但任务完整；安全性随配置和风险引入方式显著变化，不同风险载体和策略下行为差异明显。

Conclusion: 任务完成不能证明运行时安全；仅测试单一风险形式会掩盖弱点。评估应覆盖完整配置、多样化风险条件，并在执行全程保留证据。

中文摘要：基于大语言模型（LLM）的工作区智能体（workspace agent）会在异构资源、外部工具和持久状态上执行有状态的、多步骤工作流。因此，其安全性必须根据整个执行过程中的动作、副作用和状态变化来评估。尽管最近的基准测试推动了可执行安全性测试和轨迹感知验证的进展，但它们很少统一说明风险在何处进入、如何诱发不安全行为、针对哪些危害，以及支持证据在执行过程中出现在哪里。我们引入了 AgentS4D，一个用于全生命周期运行时安全性评估的沙盒基准。其四维运行时安全框架使用六个风险进入来源、六种诱导策略和九种目标危害来指导用例构建，同时七个生命周期检查点组织运行后证据。AgentS4D 包含 328 个风险注入用例。我们评估了四种框架（Hermes、OpenClaw、Claude Code 和 Codex）与五种 LLM 后端（GPT-5.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro、DeepSeek-V4-Pro、MiniMax-M3 和 Qwen3.7-Plus）的所有 20 种组合，共产生 6,560 次运行。总体而言，4,461 次运行（68.0%）触发了预先指定的不安全信号。在 20 种配置中，智能体系统的可观测安全性随其框架-LLM 配对以及风险引入方式的不同而变化。当相同的诱导策略通过不同的风险载体到达时，智能体系统表现出显著不同的安全行为。当相同的目标危害通过不同的载体和策略实现时，它们也会做出不同的响应。此外，4,344 次运行（总体 66.22%）不安全但完整。因此，任务完成不能证明运行时安全性，仅测试一种风险形式可能掩盖重要弱点。评估应检查各种风险条件下的完整智能体配置，并在整个执行过程中保留证据。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based workspace agents execute stateful, multi-step workflows across heterogeneous resources, external tools, and persistent state. Their safety must therefore be assessed from actions, side effects, and state changes throughout execution. Although recent benchmarks have advanced executable safety testing and trajectory-aware verification, they rarely provide a unified account of where risks enter, how they elicit unsafe behavior, which harms they target, and where supporting evidence appears during execution. We introduce AgentS4D, a sandboxed benchmark for lifecycle-wide runtime safety evaluation. Its four-dimensional runtime-safety framework uses six risk-entry sources, six induction strategies, and nine target harms to guide case construction, while seven lifecycle checkpoints organize post-run evidence. AgentS4D contains 328 risk-injected cases. We evaluate all 20 combinations of four harnesses (Hermes, OpenClaw, Claude Code, and Codex) and five LLM backends (GPT-5.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, DeepSeek-V4-Pro, MiniMax-M3, and Qwen3.7-Plus) on these cases, yielding 6,560 runs. Overall, 4,461 runs (68.0%) trigger prespecified unsafe signals. Across the 20 configurations, the observed safety of an agent system varies with both its harness-LLM pairing and how risk is introduced. Agent systems exhibit markedly different safety behavior when the same induction strategy reaches them through different risk carriers.
