They also respond differently to the same target harm when it is realized through different carriers and strategies. Moreover, 4,344 runs (66.22% overall) are unsafe yet complete. Thus, task completion cannot establish runtime safety, and testing only one form of a risk can conceal important weaknesses. Evaluations should examine complete agent configurations across diverse risk conditions and retain evidence throughout execution.

</details>


### [59] [A Taxonomy of Human-Robot Teamwork Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27302)
*Anastasia Mavridou,Hazel M. Taylor,Sandy Lozito,Louise A. Dennis,Michael Fisher,Marie Farrell*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个人机团队协作（HRT）要求的两级层次分类法，基于对文献和标准的分析，并通过专家评估和独立语料库演示进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机团队协作要求分散在不同来源中，缺乏一个统一框架来处理协作式人机任务的复杂性，因此需要构建一个系统化的分类法来整合这些要求。

Method: 从14个跨领域来源提取361条要求构建语料库，通过迭代分类和细化开发出包含6个高层类别和21个低层子类别的两级层次分类法，然后通过5位专家评估和独立语料库（448条要求，19个来源，6个领域）进行验证。

Result: 形成的分类法包含6个高层类别（信息提供、关系控制、决策支持、安全机制、性能监控、基础系统能力）和21个低层子类别，专家评估和实用性演示均证实了其有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的HRT要求分类法为统一和标准化人机团队协作要求提供了框架，有助于该领域的系统设计与评估。

中文摘要：自主系统越来越多地部署在安全和关键任务领域中，在这些领域中，人类和机器人必须作为一个团队来操作以完成复杂任务。现有的对人机团队协作要求在不同来源之间仍然支离破碎，没有统一的框架来处理协作式人机任务的复杂性。我们通过提出一个源自对（学术和工业）文献、标准和监管指南分析的人机团队协作（HRT）要求分类法来解决这一差距。我们从14个跨领域来源中提取了一个包含361条要求的构建语料库。通过迭代分类和细化，我们开发了一个两级层次分类法，包含6个高层类别和21个低层子类别，区分了信息提供、关系控制、决策支持、安全机制、性能监控和基础系统能力。我们通过5位领域专家的评估和对一个独立构建的、来自19个来源、涵盖六个HRT领域的448条要求语料库的实用性演示来验证该分类法。

Original abstract: Autonomous systems are increasingly deployed in safety- and mission-critical domains where humans and robots must operate as a team to complete complex tasks. Existing requirements for Human-Robot teamwork remain fragmented across disparate sources, with no unified framework that addresses complexities of collaborative Human-Robot tasks. We address this gap by presenting a taxonomy of Human-Robot Teamwork (HRT) requirements derived from analysis of (academic and industrial) literature, standards and regulatory guidance. We extracted a construction corpus of 361 requirements from 14 cross-domain sources. Through iterative classification and refinement, we develop a two-level hierarchical taxonomy comprising 6 high-level categories and 21 low-level subcategories that distinguish information provision, relational control, decision support, safety mechanisms, performance monitoring, and foundational system capabilities. We validate the taxonomy through expert evaluation with 5 domain specialists and a utility demonstration on an independently assembled corpus of 448 requirements drawn from 19 sources spanning six HRT domains.

</details>


### [60] [SIGIL: Compiling Agent Skills into Typed Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27309)
*Jayanaka Dantanarayana,Savini Kashmira,Lingjia Tang,Jason Mars*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出技能编译方法（SIGIL），将散文式技能编译为可执行测试装置，通过类型化中间表示AG-IR分离认知与机制，使步骤执行率从56%提升至86%，完整流程完成率提升2.3倍，token消耗降低42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散文式技能在运行时未被编码，模型可能跳过强制验证步骤，导致执行率低（仅56%）。手工编写测试装置虽能解决，但繁琐且丧失技能创作优势，需要自动编译方法。

Method: 引入技能编译（SIGIL），将散文技能编译为可执行harness。核心是AG-IR，一种类型化agentic中间表示，将模型认知（决策）与代码机制（执行）分离，使流程成为程序结构。

Result: 编译后harness执行86%强制步骤（vs 散文56-68%），完整流程完成率提高2.3倍，token需求减少42%（0.58x），且模型无关性显著：两代模型均稳定在86%。

Conclusion: 技能编译有效替代散文式技能，显著提升过程遵循率与效率，且鲁棒性更强，为构建可靠AI智能体提供新路径。

中文摘要：AI集成智能体日益通过技能获取能力：技能是加载到模型上下文中的散文式流程文件，并由工具调用循环运行。技能被描述给运行时，但从未编码进运行时，因此模型每次运行都重新推导其控制流，并可能跳过强制验证。在30个技能和两代模型上，散文式智能体仅执行了其自身技能强制要求的步骤的56%，同时产生的工件却通过了输出检查。解决方案是已知的：编写一个测试装置（harness），在其中流程即程序结构。然而，手工编写测试装置繁琐且丢弃了使技能成功创作界面。为解决这一局限，我们引入了技能编译（Skill Compilation），在SIGIL中实现，将散文式技能编译为可执行的测试装置。其核心是AG-IR，一种类型化的智能体中间表示，将模型拥有的认知与代码拥有的机制分离。编译后的测试装置执行了86%的强制步骤，完整完成整个流程的频率提高2.3倍，且所需token仅为0.58倍。值得注意的是，该保证与模型无关：在两代模型上，测试装置保持在86%，而散文式从56%波动到68%。

Original abstract: AI-Integrated agents increasingly acquire capability from skills: prose procedure files loaded into a model's context and run by a tool-calling loop. A skill is described to the runtime but never encoded in it, so the model re-derives its control flow on every run and may skip mandated verification. Across 30 skills and two model generations, a prose agent performs only 56% of the steps its own skill mandates, while producing artifacts that pass output checks. The remedy is known: write a harness, in which the procedure is program structure. However, hand-writing harnesses is tedious and discards the authoring surface that made skills succeed. To address this limitation, we introduce Skill Compilation, realized in SIGIL, which compiles a prose skill into an executable harness. At its center is AG-IR, a typed agentic intermediate representation separating model-owned cognition from code-owned mechanism. Compiled harnesses perform 86% of mandated steps, complete the full procedure 2.3x as often, and require 0.58x the tokens. Notably, the guarantee is model-independent: the harness holds at 86% across two model generations while prose swings from 56% to 68%.

</details>


### [61] [TrustChain-Review: A Risk-Adaptive Blockchain and Game-Theoretic Framework for Trustworthy AI-Assisted Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27310)
*Mohammad Naserameri*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: TrustChain-Review 是一个结合区块链证据、博弈论激励和风险自适应治理的框架，用于提升AI辅助代码审查的可信度；实验表明全证据配置效果最好但成本高，风险自适应配置在成本效率上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助软件开发加速了编码和审查，但导致责任认定困难：开发者可能提交未充分验证的代码，审查者可能有限检查即批准，且集中式声誉记录难以审计，需要更可信的代码审查机制。

Method: 提出TrustChain-Review框架，包含基于区块链的证据层、开发者-审查者-平台三方博弈模型，以及按预期收益与成本权衡动态调整治理强度的规则；使用Diff Quality Estimation数据集校准的受控模拟，比较六种治理配置在30次独立运行中的表现。

Result: 全证据配置在声誉准确性、信任收敛、恶意/表面审查检测上表现最强，但治理成本最高；风险自适应配置通过选择性加强控制降低了成本并提高了成本效率，但信任和检测结果略低于全证据配置。

Conclusion: 强证据治理适用于高风险或审计敏感变更，而选择性（风险自适应）治理对常规或低风险贡献更为实用，能在可信度与运营成本间取得更好平衡。

中文摘要：背景：AI辅助软件开发可以加速编码和审查，但也使得责任认定更加困难。开发者可能提交未经充分验证的代码，审查者可能在检查有限的情况下批准更改，而集中式声誉记录可能难以审计。目标：本研究提出TrustChain-Review，一个结合可验证证据、战略激励和风险敏感治理的框架，以支持更可信的代码审查。方法：该框架包括基于区块链的证据层、面向开发者、审查者和平台的三方博弈论模型，以及一条规则：当预期收益证明其成本合理时，应用更强的治理。评估使用经Diff Quality Estimation数据集校准的受控模拟。在30次独立运行中比较了六种治理配置，指标包括声誉准确性、信任收敛、恶意审查检测、表面审查检测、平台净效用、治理成本和成本效率。结果：全证据配置在声誉、信任和检测结果方面最强，但其治理成本也最高。风险自适应配置通过选择性应用更强的控制来降低成本并提高成本效率，尽管其信任和检测结果低于全证据设置。结论：强基于证据的治理最适合高风险或审计敏感的变化。对于常规或较低风险的贡献，选择性治理在可信度与运营成本之间提供了更实用的平衡。

Original abstract: Context: AI-assisted software development can speed up coding and review, but it also makes accountability harder to establish. Developers may submit insufficiently verified code, reviewers may approve changes with limited inspection, and centralized reputation records may be difficult to audit. Objectives: This study introduces TrustChain-Review, a framework that combines verifiable evidence, strategic incentives, and risk-sensitive governance to support more trustworthy code review. Methods: The framework includes a blockchain-based evidence layer, a three-player game-theoretic model for developers, reviewers, and the platform, and a rule that applies stronger governance when the expected benefit justifies its cost. The evaluation uses a controlled simulation calibrated with the Diff Quality Estimation dataset. Six governance configurations are compared over 30 independent runs using reputation accuracy, trust convergence, malicious-review detection, superficial-review detection, net platform utility, governance cost, and cost-efficiency. Results: The full-evidence configuration produces the strongest reputation, trust, and detection results, but it also has the highest governance cost. The risk-adaptive configuration lowers this cost and improves cost-efficiency by applying stronger controls selectively, although its trust and detection results are lower than those of the full-evidence setting. Conclusion: Strong evidence-based governance is most appropriate for high-risk or audit-sensitive changes. For routine or lower-risk contributions, selective governance offers a more practical balance between trustworthiness and operational cost.

</details>


### [62] [PROGRESS: Property-Guided Regression Search for Semantic Falsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27359)
*Davis Tocheuk Mo,Noshin Ulfat,Matthew B. Dwyer,Soneya Binta Hossain*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: PROGRESS 将意图驱动的属性测试与覆盖引导的搜索相结合，在保持结构覆盖率的同时检测深层缺陷，在 25 个大型 Java 系统上检测出 58% 的当前版本缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回归测试生成只依据被测系统自身行为构造断言，无法暴露已存在故障；而属性测试虽有独立语义先知，但难以指导到达深层状态或满足选择性前置条件，且依赖高质量属性。

Method: PROGRESS 从代码上下文提取意图，用语言模型生成可执行 jqwik 属性；扩展 DynaMOSA 为每个属性引入搜索目标和属性感知适应度函数；将属性参数绑定到演化测试序列，引导生成同时面向覆盖和缺陷检测。

Result: 在 25 个大型 Java 系统上，PROGRESS 检测出 562 个当前版本缺陷中的 328 个（58%），回归测试生成检测出 0 个；满足 150 个难以到达属性中的 70 个前置条件，独立 jqwik 仅满足 18 个；消融证实文档和调用上下文是关键。

Conclusion: PROGRESS 有效融合属性测试与搜索生成，在保持结构探索能力的同时显著提升缺陷暴露能力，表明意图驱动属性能补充传统回归测试生成的不足。

中文摘要：基于搜索的回归测试生成能有效探索复杂程序结构，获得较高的结构覆盖率，但其测试先知来源于被测系统：已存在的故障会被记录为预期行为而非被暴露。基于属性的测试提供独立的语义先知，但依赖于高质量的属性，并且难以指导到达深层状态或满足选择性前置条件。我们提出了 PROGRESS（PROperty-Guided REgression Search for Semantic Falsification），将意图驱动的属性集成到覆盖引导的、基于搜索的进化测试生成中，以到达深层程序状态并检测预期行为的违反。PROGRESS：(1) 提取承载意图的代码上下文，并使用语言模型流水线生成可执行的 jqwik 属性，同时限制实现泄漏；(2) 用每个属性的搜索目标和属性感知适应度函数扩展 EvoSuite 的 DynaMOSA，奖励通过前置条件的进展并优先考虑伪造执行；(3) 绑定属性参数并使用 jqwik 提供的生成器将量化输入连接到演化的测试序列，将生成引导向覆盖和缺陷检测目标。我们在 25 个大型 Java 系统上对 PROGRESS 进行了评估，与回归测试生成、独立属性测试和上下文消融进行了比较。PROGRESS 检测出 562 个当前版本缺陷中的 328 个（58%），而回归测试生成检测出 0 个；并在 150 个难以到达的属性中满足了 70 个的所有前置条件，而独立 jqwik 仅满足 18 个。消融实验表明，文档和调用者/被调用者上下文是生成有效可执行属性的关键。PROGRESS 在保留结构探索的同时，暴露了回归派生断言遗漏的故障；我们发布了一个全面的工件包。

Original abstract: Search-based regression-test generation effectively explores complex program structures, yielding high structural coverage, but its oracles are derived from the system under test: faults already present are recorded as expected behavior rather than exposed. Property-based testing offers independent semantic oracles, but depends on high-quality properties and gives little guidance for reaching deep states or satisfying selective preconditions.
  We present PROGRESS (PROperty-Guided REgression Search for Semantic Falsification), integrating intent-driven properties into coverage-guided, search-based evolutionary test generation to reach deep program states and detect violations of intended behavior. PROGRESS (1) extracts intent-bearing code context and uses a language-model pipeline to generate executable jqwik properties while limiting implementation leakage; (2) extends EvoSuite's DynaMOSA with a search objective and property-aware fitness function per property, rewarding progress through preconditions and prioritizing falsifying executions; and (3) binds property parameters and uses jqwik-provided generators to connect quantified inputs to evolving test sequences, steering generation toward coverage and bug-detection goals.
  We evaluate PROGRESS on 25 large-scale Java systems against regression-test generation, standalone property-based testing, and context ablations. PROGRESS detects 328/562 current-version bugs (58%) versus none for regression-test generation, and satisfies all preconditions for 70/150 hard-to-reach properties versus 18 for standalone jqwik. Ablations show documentation and caller/callee context are key to generating valid executable properties. PROGRESS preserves structural exploration while exposing faults missed by regression-derived assertions; we release a comprehensive artifact package.

</details>


### [63] [From Backlog Items to Security Guidance: Towards Continuous Security Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27374)
*Ignacio García Núñez,Florian Angermeir,Fabiola Moyón Constante*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出一种基于NLP的待办事项丰富系统，结合分类器和RAG，自动检测安全相关待办事项并链接安全需求，在工业环境中评估，能帮助工程师早期识别安全需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管领域的持续软件工程中，安全需求难以在待办事项中显式化，工程师需从简短描述中推断安全相关性且缺乏及时指导。

Method: 结合安全相关性分类器（面向召回率）与基于安全需求文档的检索增强生成（RAG）流程，构建四阶段RAG管道，并发布标注数据集。

Result: 分类器在分布内F2=0.774，零样本G-measure约0.65；RAG检索的24个条款中12个获得4/5以上相关性评分。

Conclusion: NLP-based待办事项丰富可支持工程师早期识别安全需求，有助于在持续软件工程中主动引入安全要求，促进持续安全合规。

中文摘要：在受监管领域的持续软件工程中，工程团队需要在整个开发生命周期中处理安全问题。然而，使安全需求在待办事项中显式化仍然存在问题。工程师必须从简短、自由格式的描述中推断待办事项的安全相关性，并且往往缺乏关于适用需求的及时指导。我们提出了一种基于NLP的待办事项丰富系统，用于检测与安全相关的待办事项，并将其链接到相关的安全需求。该方法结合了安全相关性分类器和基于安全需求文档的检索增强生成（RAG）流程。该方法在高度受监管领域的一家大型企业中进行了开发和评估。我们提出三项贡献。首先，我们发布了一个包含288个待办事项的数据集，这些事项由九位安全从业者标记了安全相关性，具有高度一致性（Fleiss' κ=0.787）。其次，一个面向召回率的分类器，在分布内达到F2=0.774，在五个已建立基准上的平均零样本G-measure约为0.65，匹配或超过了大多数已发表的经典机器学习和开源GPT基线。第三，我们初步评估了一个四阶段的安全需求文档接地RAG流程，由两位从业者在工业待办事项上使用公司内部安全策略和CIS基准进行测试。在检索到的24个条款中，有12个被评为至少4/5的相关性。我们的发现提供了初步指标，表明基于NLP的产品待办事项丰富可以支持工程师在开发过程早期识别安全需求。通过这项工作，我们旨在通过主动在持续软件工程中引入安全需求来促进持续的安全合规性。

Original abstract: Continuous software engineering in regulated domains requires engineering teams to address security throughout the development lifecycle. Yet making security requirements explicit in backlog items is still problematic. Engineers must instead infer security relevance of backlog items from brief, free-form descriptions and often lack timely guidance on applicable requirements. We present an NLP-based backlog enrichment system that detects security-relevant backlog items and links them to relevant security requirements. The approach combines a security-relevance classifier with a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline over security requirements documents. The approach was developed and evaluated in the context of a large enterprise in highly regulated domains. We present three contributions. First, we release a dataset of 288 backlog items labeled for security relevance by nine security practitioners, with substantial agreement (Fleiss' $κ=0.787$). Second, a recall-oriented classifier achieving $F2=0.774$ in-distribution and mean zero-shot G-measure $\approx 0.65$ across five established benchmarks, matching or outperforming most published classical-ML and open-source GPT baselines. Third, we preliminarily evaluated a four-stage security requirements document-grounded RAG pipeline with two practitioners on industrial backlogs using company-internal security policies and CIS Benchmarks. Of the retrieved 24 clauses, 12 were rated at least 4/5 for relevance. Our findings provide first indicators that NLP-based product backlog enrichment can support engineers in identifying security requirements early in the development process. With this work we aim to facilitate continuous security compliance through proactive introduction of security requirements in continuous software engineering.

</details>


### [64] [SWE-NFI: Studying and Benchmarking Coding Agents for Non-Functional Improvements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27409)
*Pengyu Xue,He Yang Yuan,Xin Wang,Junkai Chen,Haonan Zhang,Boyuan Chen,Zishuo Ding,Zhenhao Li,Weiyi Shang*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出了SWE-NFI基准，用于评估编码智能体在功能正确性之外的非功能性改进能力，发现现有智能体整体NFI能力不如人类开发者，尤其在结构性代码改进方面差距明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体评估主要关注功能正确性，缺乏对非功能性改进（NFI）的评估，而实际开发中NFI很重要，因此需要一个新的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了包含188个任务（来自真实合并的PR）的SWE-NFI基准，将NFI操作化为92条可执行规则，并设计了结合功能正确性测试与规则评估的综合性评估套件。

Result: 最佳智能体功能正确率为70.0%，但所有智能体的整体NFI能力低于人类开发者；在结构性代码改进上，智能体NFI得分0.0-1.3，人类参考为1.5。

Conclusion: SWE-NFI为评估编码智能体超越功能正确性提供了可复现的基础，揭示了当前智能体在NFI方面的不足，为未来发展指明方向。

中文摘要：尽管编码智能体在面向正确性的基准测试中取得了令人印象深刻的性能，但它们进行保持行为不变的非功能性改进（NFI）的能力仍未得到充分探索。在现实世界的软件开发中，开发者会不断地在不改变可观察行为的情况下提高软件质量，然而现有基准主要评估功能正确性，对评估这些非功能性改进的支持有限。在本文中，我们提出了SWE-NFI，一个用于评估编码智能体在功能正确性之外的NFI能力的基准。我们的基准包含从开源Python项目中真实合并的拉取请求构建的188个任务。我们将面向开发者的NFI操作化为92条可执行规则，并开发了一个综合评估套件，将功能正确性测试与基于规则的NFI评估相结合。我们评估了最先进的商业和开源编码智能体。尽管性能最佳的智能体实现了70.0%的功能正确率，但所有被评估的智能体在整体NFI能力上普遍不如人类开发者。这一差距在结构性代码改进方面尤为明显，智能体的NFI得分范围为0.0到1.3，而人类参考为1.5。我们的基准和发现为评估和推进编码智能体超越功能正确性提供了可复现的基础。

Original abstract: Although coding agents have achieved impressive performance on correctness-oriented benchmarks, their ability to make behavior-preserving non-functional improvements (NFIs) remains underexplored. In real-world software development, developers continuously improve software quality without changing observable behavior, yet existing benchmarks primarily evaluate functional correctness and provide limited support for assessing these non-functional improvements.
  In this paper, we present SWE-NFI, a benchmark for evaluating coding agents on NFIs beyond functional correctness. Our benchmark contains 188 tasks constructed from real merged pull requests in open-source Python projects. We operationalize developer-oriented NFIs into 92 executable rules and develop a comprehensive evaluation suite that combines functional correctness testing with rule-based NFI evaluation.
  We evaluate state-of-the-art commercial and open-source coding agents. Although the best-performing agent achieves a 70.0\% functional correctness rate, all evaluated agents generally fall short of human developers in overall NFI capability. The gap is particularly evident for structural code improvements, where agents' NFI scores range from 0.0 to 1.3, compared with 1.5 for the human reference. Our benchmark and findings provide a reproducible foundation for evaluating and advancing coding agents beyond functional correctness.

</details>


### [65] [LimICE: Integrating LLM into ICE Framework for Efficient Loop Invariant Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27606)
*Kai Fan,ShiWen Yu,GuangSheng Fan,HaoAng Chi,WanWei Liu,Ji Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出一种名为Incremental ICE的新型增量学习框架，结合IC3的增量思想与ICE框架，并实例化工具LimICE，利用LLM生成有序引理序列，在循环不变量合成任务上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的循环不变量合成方法大多采用整体式方法，因无法严格约束学习过程，难以同时考虑所有必要条件并生成完整不变量；而循环不变量本质上是有序引理序列，因此需要增量式合成方法。

Method: 提出Incremental ICE框架，将IC3的增量哲学融入ICE框架，通过定义引理特定的学习目标并引入反例过滤机制实现可靠的增量学习；实例化工具LimICE，利用LLM生成有序引理序列，并以ICE-DT作为回退机制补充引理序列。

Result: 在367个线性基准和50个非线性基准上，LimICE分别解决349个和47个问题，平均耗时15.2秒和8.8秒；相比最先进的LLM基线，多解决12%-24%实例，同时快36%-63%；相比强非LLM基线，在线性和非线性基准上至少多解决86个和27个实例。

Conclusion: 实验证明所提的增量学习框架及LimICE工具能有效提升循环不变量合成的性能与效率，验证了将增量思想与LLM结合用于引理序列生成的优越性。

中文摘要：循环不变量合成是程序验证中的一个基本问题，然而其固有的不可判定性使其极具挑战性。近期研究越来越多地采用各种机器学习技术来生成循环不变量。然而，这些方法大多采用整体式方法。由于无法严格约束学习过程，基于学习的方法在解决复杂问题时难以同时考虑所有必要条件并生成完整的不变量。事实上，循环不变量通常是一个有序的引理序列，而非单一的不变量公式。这促使我们提出Incremental ICE，一种用于增量合成的新型学习框架。我们的框架将IC3的增量哲学融入通用不变量学习框架ICE中。通过定义特定于引理的学习目标并引入反例过滤机制，我们能够实现可靠的增量学习。在该框架下，我们实例化了一个循环不变量合成工具LimICE，它利用大语言模型（LLM）生成有序引理序列，并集成ICE-DT作为回退机制以补充引理序列。在367个线性基准和50个非线性基准上的实验证明了所提方法的有效性。LimICE平均用15.2秒解决了349个（共367个）线性问题，平均用8.8秒解决了47个（共50个）非线性问题。与最先进的基于LLM的基线相比，我们的方法在线性和非线性基准上多解决12%至24%的实例，同时运行速度快36%至63%。LimICE还持续优于强非LLM基线，并在线性和非线性基准上分别至少多解决86个和27个额外实例。

Original abstract: Loop invariant synthesis is a fundamental problem in program verification, yet the inherent undecidability makes it highly challenging. Recent studies have increasingly employed various machine learning techniques to generate loop invariants. However, most of these methods adopt a monolithic approach. Due to the inability to strictly constrain the learning process, learning-based methods struggle to simultaneously consider all necessary conditions and generate complete invariants when tackling complex problems. In fact, a loop invariant is often an ordered sequence of lemmas, rather than a single invariant formula. This motivates us to propose Incremental ICE, a novel learning framework for incremental synthesis. Our framework integrates the incremental philosophy of IC3 into the general invariant learning framework ICE. By defining a lemma-specific learning objective and introducing a counterexample filtering mechanism, we can achieve sound incremental learning. Under this framework, we instantiate a loop invariant synthesis tool, LimICE, which leverages LLMs to generate the ordered sequence of lemmas and incorporates ICE-DT as a fallback mechanism to complement the lemma sequence. Experiments on 367 linear benchmarks and 50 nonlinear benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach. LimICE solves 349 (out of 367) linear problems on an average of 15.2 seconds and 47 (out of 50) nonlinear problems on an average of 8.8 seconds. Compared to the state-of-the-art LLM-based baseline, our approach solves 12-24% more instances while running 36-63% faster across linear and nonlinear benchmarks. LimICE also consistently outperforms strong non-LLM baselines and solves at least 86 and 27 additional instances on the linear and nonlinear benchmarks, respectively.

</details>


### [66] [Not as Sweet by Another Name: An Empirical Study of Format Robustness in LLM Document Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27648)
*Xiaoyu Zhang,Xianyun Cheng,Tianlin Li,Yuwei Zheng,Yue Yang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出格式感知的蜕变测试框架，发现文档格式变化可导致LLM工作流准确率下降53.63%并触发41%的决策漂移，轻量级策略可恢复44.21%的漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试仅关注单提示字符串输入，未考虑文档工作流中同一内容以不同格式输入时的鲁棒性，导致格式变化对端到端LLM工作流可靠性的影响未知。

Method: 提出一个格式感知的蜕变测试框架，定义三个蜕变关系，并通过大规模实证研究评估四种LLM工作流、四个任务和四种文档格式的格式鲁棒性。

Result: 切换文档格式可使准确率下降高达53.63%，并在超过41%的实例中引起决策漂移；用户侧轻量级缓解策略能恢复最多44.21%的格式诱导决策漂移，无需模型重训练。

Conclusion: 文档格式并非中立的载物工具，而是影响LLM软件系统可靠性的关键因素，必须在高风险的现实部署中加以测试和防护。

中文摘要：由LLM驱动的软件系统正在从纯文本对话迅速演变为以文档为中心的端到端工作流，其中相同的语义内容可以通过文件上传界面以多种文档格式（例如CSV）交付。然而，现有的测试工作侧重于模型和系统的鲁棒性和可靠性，其输入是单一提示字符串，留下了一个关键问题未得到解答：当相同的内容以不同的文档格式到达时，这些文档工作流能否保持鲁棒的行为？为了填补这一空白，在本文中，我们提出了一个格式感知的蜕变测试框架，包含三个蜕变关系，以全面评估端到端LLM文档工作流的格式鲁棒性。基于该框架，我们进行了一项大规模实证研究，涵盖四个代表性的LLM工作流、四个真实世界任务和四种文档格式，共计48,000次工作流执行。我们的研究结果表明，格式变化构成了系统性和严重的威胁。仅仅切换格式就可能导致准确率下降高达53.63%，并在超过41%的实例中触发决策漂移。我们进一步从用户的角度设计了轻量级缓解策略，无需模型重训练即可恢复高达44.21%的格式引起的决策漂移。我们的研究表明，文档格式不是中立的包装，而是影响LLM软件系统可靠性的关键因素，呼吁在现实世界高风险场景的部署中采取相应的测试和保障措施。

Original abstract: LLM-driven software systems are rapidly evolving from plain-text conversations to document-centric end-to-end workflows, where the same semantic content can be delivered in diverse document formats (e.g., CSV) through file upload interfaces. Yet existing testing work focuses on the robustness and reliability of models and systems whose input is a single prompt string, leaving a critical question unanswered: Can these document workflows maintain robust behaviors when the same content arrives in a different document format? To fill the gap, in this paper, we propose a format-aware metamorphic testing framework with three metamorphic relations to comprehensively evaluate the format robustness of end-to-end LLM document workflows. Based on this framework, we conduct a large-scale empirical study spanning four representative LLM workflows, four real-world tasks, and four document formats, comprising a total of 48,000 workflow executions. Our findings reveal that format variation poses a systematic and serious threat. Merely switching formats can cause accuracy to drop by up to 53.63% and trigger decision drifts in over 41% of instances. We further design lightweight mitigation strategies from the users' perspective that recover up to 44.21% of format-induced decision drift without model retraining. Our study demonstrates that document format is not a neutral wrapper but a critical factor affecting the reliability of LLM software systems, calling for corresponding testing and safeguards in the deployment in real-world high-stakes scenarios.

</details>


### [67] [Can Large Language Models Resolve Real Java Merge Conflicts? An Evaluation with a Calibrated LLM-as-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27674)
*Bowen Shen*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用LLM解决Java合并冲突的问题，提出了一种生成-验证-重试智能体，并用校准的LLM评判器与确定性检查进行验证。在ConflictBench上，LLM求解器在约55%的真实冲突上与开发者匹配，显著优于传统工具AutoMerge，同时指出结构检查不能交由LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合并冲突是协作开发中的常见成本，传统合并工具在启发式不适用时经常弃权，而LLM可以为几乎所有冲突生成解决方案，但缺乏可扩展的高质量评估方法。

Method: 构建LLM求解器（生成-验证-重试智能体），使用冲突标记、Java解析器和重复声明检查作为推理时信号；采用双指标评估：G-Eval LLM评判器（经人类标签校准）和确定性结构有效性检查。

Result: 在292个人类标注案例上，评判器达到100%精确率、64.6%召回率；LLM求解器在约55%真实冲突上匹配开发者，覆盖公平比较下超过AutoMerge 18-22个百分点；4/5通过结构检查失败但被LLM评判器接受。

Conclusion: LLM求解器能有效解决传统工具弃权的冲突，提升覆盖率是主要优势；但结构正确性必须通过确定性检查保证，不能依赖LLM评判。

中文摘要：合并冲突是协作软件开发中反复出现的成本，传统的结构化与半结构化合并工具经常选择放弃：当它们的启发式方法不适用时，它们会将冲突留待解决。大型语言模型（LLM）反而可以为几乎任何冲突生成候选解决方案，但大规模衡量这些解决方案是否真的良好是困难的，因为为每个模型输出获取人类可取性判断无法扩展。我们共同研究了ConflictBench中真实Java合并冲突上的这两个问题。我们首先构建了一个LLM求解器，作为一个“生成-验证-重试”智能体，仅使用推理时信号（冲突标记、Java解析器和重复声明检查），并且从不查看开发者的答案。然后我们使用一个双指标套件评估其解决方案：（1）一个开发者匹配的LLM-as-judge，实现为G-Eval指标，并且关键是在使用前针对ConflictBench的人类标签进行校准，以及（2）一个不使用LLM的确定性结构有效性检查。在对292个人类标注案例的元评估中，该评判器在64.6%的召回率下达到100%的精确率（零误接受），因此每个接受都是可信的，每个下游比率都是保守的下界。在此验证的评判器下，LLM求解器在约55%的真实冲突（保守下限）上与开发者自己的解决方案匹配，而在覆盖公平比较下，LLM（55-59%）比最强的传统工具（AutoMerge，36.7%）高出约18-22个百分点；这一优势几乎完全来自覆盖率，而非原始准确性，因为这些工具在20-90%的冲突上弃权，而LLM在强制解决下从不弃权。最后，LLM评判器接受了未通过确定性结构检查的5个解决方案中的4个，这表明结构正确性不能委托给LLM。

Original abstract: Merge conflicts are a recurring cost of collaborative software development, and the traditional structured and semi-structured merge tools that address them frequently abstain: when their heuristics do not apply, they leave the conflict unresolved. Large language models (LLMs) can instead produce a candidate resolution for almost any conflict, but measuring whether those resolutions are actually good at scale is hard, because obtaining human desirability judgments for every model output does not scale. We study both problems together on real Java merge conflicts from ConflictBench. We first build an LLM solver as a generate-validate-retry agent that uses only inference-time signals (conflict markers, a Java parser, and duplicate-declaration checks) and never sees the developer's answer. We then evaluate its resolutions with a two-metric suite: (1) a developer-match LLM-as-judge implemented as a G-Eval metric and, crucially, calibrated against ConflictBench's human labels before use, and (2) a deterministic structural-validity check that uses no LLM. On a meta-evaluation of 292 human-labeled cases, the judge reaches 100% precision (zero false accepts) at 64.6% recall, so every acceptance is trustworthy and every downstream rate is a conservative lower bound. Under this validated judge, LLM solvers match the developer's own resolution on about 55% of true conflicts (conservative floor), and under a coverage-fair comparison the LLMs (55-59%) beat the strongest traditional tool (AutoMerge, 36.7%) by roughly 18-22 points; the edge comes almost entirely from coverage, not raw accuracy, since the tools abstain on 20-90% of conflicts while the LLM under forced resolution abstains on none. Finally, the LLM judge accepted 4 of the 5 resolutions that fail the deterministic structural check, evidence that structural correctness must not be delegated to an LLM.

</details>


### [68] [A comparative analysis of automated techniques for security bug report identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27893)
*Muhammad Laiq*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 通过基准数据集比较多种自动技术（传统ML与LLM）识别安全相关错误报告，发现SetFit表现最佳（F1=0.80），RoBERTa接近，GPT-5.2较差，且迁移学习效果因项目特性而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究碎片化，缺乏对不同技术在同一基准下的系统比较，导致难以评估其有效性并为实践提供选择指导。

Method: 使用四个基准数据集，对比评估逻辑回归、SVM、随机森林、GPT-5.2、BERT-base、RoBERTa和SetFit，并进行了跨项目实验。

Result: SetFit取得最高F1分数0.80，在三个数据集上最优；RoBERTa表现次之；传统ML（逻辑回归）在某些情境下仍强；GPT-5.2在零样本和少样本下均差；跨项目迁移学习对数据有限项目有益，但可能损害项目特定性强的项目。

Conclusion: 推荐SetFit用于安全相关缺陷报告识别，RoBERTa作为强替代，传统ML可作稳健基线；跨项目迁移需谨慎评估项目特性。

中文摘要：及时识别与安全相关的错误报告对于最小化软件系统中的漏洞窗口至关重要。手动筛选传入的错误报告以识别安全相关问题耗时、易出错，并且对于大规模软件系统而言不可扩展。因此，人们提出了各种自动化技术，包括传统机器学习（ML）技术和大型语言模型，以促进这一任务。然而，相关文献仍然分散。大多数研究引入或优化一种特定技术，并针对有限的基线集合进行评估，且通常采用不同的实验设置。因此，难以比较它们的结果并就现有方法的有效性得出可靠结论，使得研究人员和从业者缺乏关于哪些技术最适合该任务的清晰指导。为解决这一差距，我们使用基准数据集对几种有前景的自动化技术进行了比较分析，以识别与安全相关的错误报告。我们评估了逻辑回归、支持向量机、随机森林、OpenAI的GPT-5.2、BERT-base、RoBERTa和SetFit（一种最先进的少样本学习框架）。我们的结果表明，SetFit取得了最佳的整体性能，F1分数达到0.80，并在四个数据集中的三个上优于其他技术。RoBERTa表现具有竞争力，在某些项目中接近SetFit，而传统ML技术，特别是逻辑回归，在特定情境下仍是强大的基线。相比之下，GPT-5.2在零样本和少样本设置中均表现不佳。此外，跨项目实验表明，迁移学习可以改善数据有限项目的性能，但可能降低具有强烈项目特定特征的项目的性能。

Original abstract: Timely identification of security-related bug reports is essential to minimize the window of vulnerabilities in software systems. Manually screening incoming bug reports to identify security-related issues is time-consuming, error-prone, and non-scalable for large-scale software systems. Thus, a variety of automatic techniques, including traditional machine learning (ML) techniques and large language models, have been proposed to facilitate this task. However, the literature remains fragmented. Most studies introduce or optimize a particular technique and evaluate it against a limited set of baselines, often under different experimental setups. As a result, it is difficult to compare their results and draw reliable conclusions about the effectiveness of existing approaches, leaving researchers and practitioners without clear guidance on which techniques are most suitable for the task.
  To address this gap, we conducted a comparative analysis of several promising automated techniques to identify security-related bug reports using benchmark datasets. We evaluated Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machines, Random Forest, OpenAI's GPT-5.2, BERT-base, RoBERTa, and SetFit (a state-of-the-art few-shot learning framework).
  Our results indicate that SetFit achieves the best overall performance, achieving an F1-score of 0.80 and outperforming other techniques on three of the four datasets. RoBERTa performs competitively and approaches SetFit in some projects, while traditional ML techniques, particularly Logistic Regression, remain a strong baseline in certain contexts. In contrast, GPT-5.2 performs poorly in both zero-shot and few-shot settings. In addition, cross-project experiments demonstrate that transfer learning can improve performance for projects with limited data, but may degrade results for projects with strong project-specific characteristics.

</details>


### [69] [The Case for Vibe Modeling: A Missing Step in AI-Based Trustworthy Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27923)
*Shalini Chakraborty,Michael Mittermaier,Judith Michael*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文提出vibe建模作为自然语言与代码生成之间的轻量级中间抽象，并通过学生调查评估其在AI辅助开发中的理解、验证、信任和有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI辅助开发过于关注直接生成代码，缺乏保留人类意图并支持行为推理的中间表示，导致理解、验证、信任和可追溯性问题。

Method: 进行了一项学生调查研究，考察在多种AI辅助开发场景下，参与者对LLM输出的理解、验证努力、信任度以及vibe建模的感知有用性。

Result: 研究获得了关于LLM输出理解、验证负担、信任水平和vibe建模潜在价值的感知数据，但具体结果未在摘要中详述。

Conclusion: 作者认为vibe建模有望为可信赖和可解释的AI软件工程提供方向，研究结果可为未来的相关研究提供参考。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）越来越多地被用于从自然语言提示生成软件工件。虽然这实现了快速原型设计并降低了软件创建的门槛，但也引入了与理解、验证、可追溯性和信任相关的挑战。在本文中，我们认为当前基于AI的开发实践过于专注于直接生成代码，而对保留人类意图并支持系统行为推理的中间表示关注不足。我们主张将vibe建模作为自然语言交互与代码生成之间的轻量级中间抽象。为了探索其潜力，我们进行了一项学生调查研究，考察了在多种AI辅助开发场景下，对LLM输出的理解、验证工作、信任度以及vibe建模的感知有用性。我们的结果旨在为未来通过vibe建模实现可信赖和可解释的基于AI的软件工程研究提供信息。

Original abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate software artifacts from natural language prompts. While this enables rapid prototyping and lowers the barrier to software creation, it also introduces challenges related to understanding, validation, traceability, and trust. In this paper, we argue that current AI-based development practices focus too heavily on the direct generation of code and insufficiently on intermediate representations that preserve human intent and support reasoning about system behavior. We argue for vibe modeling as a lightweight intermediate abstraction between natural language interaction and code generation. To explore its potential, we present a student survey study that examines perceptions of LLM output understanding, validation effort, trust and the perceived usefulness of vibe modeling across several AI-assisted development scenarios. Our results are intended to inform future studies for trustworthy and explainable AI-based software engineering via vibe modeling.

</details>


### [70] [Specification-Guided Synthesis of Deadlock-Free Communication Protocol Refinements with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27964)
*Yang Li,Ping Hou,Nobuko Yoshida*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: Syntropy是一个结合多方会话类型（MPST）规范与大型语言模型的框架，用于合成协议细化，通过将细化约束嵌入生成过程，实现高有效性（95.6%-99.5%）和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信协议中的行为正确性至关重要，但LLM生成协议时缺乏可靠的行为保证；形式化方法如MPST提供保证但难以自动构造细化，需要一种结合两者优势的方法。

Method: 提出Syntropy框架，利用MPST规范指导LLM生成协议细化，并将细化约束直接纳入生成过程，确保生成的变体满足正确性保证。

Result: 综合评估表明，Syntropy实现了95.6%-99.5%的有效性，同时保持高语法正确性，并在多个LLM上生成了多样化、非平凡的细化。

Conclusion: Syntropy成功结合了MPST的严格保证与LLM的生成能力，为自动合成正确且多样化的协议细化提供了有效方案。

中文摘要：确保通信协议中的行为正确性是分布式软件系统中的一个核心挑战，因为细微的不一致可能导致死锁。在这种情况下，协议细化——即安全地替换协议以保持正确性并与其他组件兼容——至关重要。大型语言模型（LLM）在代码生成和程序合成方面展现了强大的能力，但缺乏可靠地生成具有正确行为的输出的机制。形式化规范方法，如多方会话类型（MPST），提供了严格的保证，包括无死锁性，但在自动构造协议细化方面支持有限。在本文中，我们提出了Syntropy，一个由MPST规范和LLM引导的协议细化合成框架。它将细化约束直接纳入生成过程，确保生成的变体满足这些保证。我们的综合评估表明，Syntropy在保持高语法正确性的同时，实现了95.6%-99.5%的有效性，并在多个LLM上产生了多样化、非平凡的细化。

Original abstract: Ensuring behavioural correctness in communication protocols is a central challenge in distributed software systems, as subtle inconsistencies can lead to deadlocks. In such settings, protocol refinement - the safe substitution of a protocol that preserves correctness and compatibility with other components - is essential. Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in code generation and program synthesis, yet lack mechanisms to reliably produce outputs with correct behaviour. Formal specification approaches, such as multiparty session types (MPST), offer rigorous guarantees, including deadlock freedom, but provide limited support for automatically constructing protocol refinements. In this paper, we present Syntropy, a framework for synthesising protocol refinements guided by MPST specifications and LLMs. It incorporates refinement constraints directly into the generation process, ensuring the generated variants satisfy these guarantees. Our comprehensive evaluation indicates that Syntropy achieves 95.6%-99.5% validity while maintaining high syntactic correctness, and produces diverse, non-trivial refinements across multiple LLMs.

</details>


### [71] [Advancing Awkward Arrays for High-Performance CPU and GPU Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28145)
*Ianna Osborne,Manasvi Goyal*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Awkward Array的GPU后端最新进展，利用CUDA和CCCL库显著提升了不规则嵌套数据的处理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理中大量使用嵌套变长数据，而GPU加速此类不规则工作负载困难，需要高效执行。

Method: 提出基于CCCL的CUDA实现、优化内存管理和分段归约算法，并保留Python编程模型。

Result: 显著提高GPU吞吐量，并提供了基准测试对比。

Conclusion: 这些发展使得不规则工作负载在GPU上高效执行成为可能，同时保持易用性。

中文摘要：Awkward Array是一个Python库，用于表示和处理嵌套的、变长数据，在高能物理中被广泛使用。随着HL-LHC分析日益依赖加速器硬件，不规则工作负载的高效执行变得至关重要。虽然密集数值数组可以自然地映射到GPU，但嵌套和变长数据结构由于需要间接索引、分段操作和不规则内存访问模式，其加速仍然困难得多。我们介绍了Awkward Array GPU后端的最新进展，包括基于NVIDIA CUDA Core Compute Libraries (CCCL)构建的CUDA实现、优化的内存管理以及针对ragged数组的分段归约算法。这些进展保留了现有的Python编程模型，同时显著提高了不规则工作负载的GPU吞吐量。我们描述了后端架构、自动化验证框架以及比较CPU、CuPy和CUDA实现的基准测试结果。

Original abstract: Awkward Array is a Python library for representing and processing nested, variable-length data that is widely used in high-energy physics. As HL-LHC analyses increasingly rely on accelerator hardware, efficient execution of irregular workloads has become essential. While dense numerical arrays map naturally to GPUs, nested and variable-length data structures remain significantly more difficult to accelerate because they require indirect indexing, segmented operations, and irregular memory access patterns. We present recent developments in the Awkward Array GPU backend, including CUDA implementations built on NVIDIA CUDA Core Compute Libraries (CCCL), optimized memory management, and segmented reduction algorithms for ragged arrays. These developments preserve the existing Python programming model while substantially improving GPU throughput on irregular workloads. We describe the backend architecture, automated validation framework, and benchmark results comparing CPU, CuPy, and CUDA implementations.

</details>


### [72] [Integrating AI into Requirements Quality Learning in Software Engineering Education: A TPACK-Guided Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28176)
*Hansika Ekanayake Mudiyanselage,Rohan Jai Dharmaraj,Malik Abdul Sami,Zheying Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本研究通过TPACK框架将多智能体AI工具整合到硕士需求工程作业中，混合方法分析表明学生选择性使用AI辅助分析评估，在具体质量维度（如价值表达和可测试性）上改进显著，并报告了有条件的信任与质量意识提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在软件工程实践中快速普及，但在需求工程等概念密集型课程中缺乏教学法指导的整合方式，因此需要探索如何设计作业以引导负责任的AI使用并促进学习。

Method: 采用混合方法设计，对100名硕士生进行需求质量分析作业，其中72份提交被分析；通过TPACK框架指导作业设计，结合定量（对齐改进）和定性（感知与使用）数据分析学生行为与效果。

Result: 学生将AI主要用作分析评估支持而非自动化；价值表达和可测试性等结构性质量维度改进明显，可协商性效果混合；学生表现出有条件的信任、主动改进和质量标准意识提高，同时存在适度可用性问题。

Conclusion: TPACK引导的支架式设计能将AI功能与教学目标及RE内容对齐，为需求工程教育中负责任的AI整合提供实践设计指导。

中文摘要：生成式人工智能（AI）在软件工程（SE）实践中的快速普及，使得在SE教育中采用基于教学法的AI整合方法成为必要，尤其是在需求工程（RE）这类概念密集型课程中。本研究考察了一种TPACK引导的多智能体AI工具在硕士层次RE作业（需求质量分析）中的整合。采用混合方法设计（N=100；分析72份提交），我们研究了结构化作业设计如何塑造学生的AI使用、如何影响他们对用户故事质量标准的理解，以及如何影响他们对AI优点与局限的感知。结果表明，学生有选择地使用AI工具，主要作为分析和评估的辅助手段，而非自动化工具。在结构性具体的需求质量维度（如价值表达和可测试性）上，对齐改进最为明显，而可协商性则表现出混合效应。学生报告了有条件的信任、主动改进以及对质量标准意识的提高，同时也面临适度的可用性挑战。研究结果表明，TPACK引导的支架式设计可以将AI功能与教学目标和RE内容对齐，为RE教育中负责任的AI整合提供设计指导。

Original abstract: The rapid adoption of generative Artificial Intelligence (AI) in software engineering (SE) practice creates a need for pedagogically grounded approaches to AI integration in SE education, especially in conceptually intensive subjects such as requirements engineering (RE). This study examines a TPACK-guided integration of a multi-agent AI tool into a master-level RE assignment on requirements quality analysis. Using a mixed-methods design (N=100; 72 submissions analysed), we examine how structured assignment design shaped students' AI use, affected their understanding of user story quality criteria, and influenced their perceptions of AI's benefits and limitations. Results show that students used the AI tool selectively, mainly as support for analysis and evaluation rather than automation. Alignment improvements were most evident for structurally concrete requirements quality dimensions, such as value articulation and testability, while negotiability showed mixed effects. Students reported conditional trust, active refinement, and increased awareness of quality criteria, alongside moderate usability challenges. The findings show that TPACK-guided scaffolding can align AI affordances with pedagogical goals and RE content, offering design guidance for responsible AI integration in RE education.

</details>


### [73] [Agentic Metaverse Services: A New As-a-Service Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28242)
*Xiaofei Xu,Quan Z. Sheng,Zhongjie Wang,Boualem Benatallah,Xiao Wang,Ruipeng Han*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文提出了智能体元宇宙服务（AMServ）和元宇宙中的智能体即服务（Meta-AaaS）作为GenAI时代的新服务范式，概述了其演变、特征、原则、应用和未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI从聊天机器人转向智能体AI，产生了智能体服务，结合元宇宙需求，需要新的服务形式来支持虚拟世界的业务处理。

Method: 通过分析GenAI赋能的智能体和服务演化，提出AMServ和Meta-AaaS的概念框架，阐述其形式、特征和原则，并探讨应用实例。

Result: 提出了AMServ和Meta-AaaS的具体形式与原则，并指出其典型应用及未来研究趋势。

Conclusion: AMServ和Meta-AaaS将推动AI时代新兴服务业发展，带来重大机遇。

中文摘要：生成式人工智能（GenAI）正在重构数字虚拟世界，通过增强智能体在自主学习、多模态交互、内容生成和协同决策方面的能力来升级智能体。特别是，从对话式聊天机器人向智能体式人工智能的转变，这是GenAI最近最重大的技术突破，带来了一种新的服务形式——智能体服务和智能体即服务（AaaS），其中智能体的能力被封装，如感知、决策、执行、协作和内容生成，以向用户提供定制的智能体服务。元宇宙是一个供人类生活、工作、创造和娱乐的虚拟生态系统，由新一代数字技术支持。通过将智能体服务与元宇宙相结合，产生了一种用于元宇宙业务处理的智能体元宇宙服务，记为AMServ，作为元宇宙服务的一种新形式。元宇宙环境中的AaaS，记为Meta-AaaS，作为实现AMServ的一种方法，已成为智能体服务和服务计算的新范式。本文概述了GenAI赋能的智能体和服务的演变及新特征，揭示了智能体服务在元宇宙环境中的角色和原则，提出了AMServ和Meta-AaaS的形式、特征和原则，讨论了AMServ和Meta-AaaS的典型应用实例，最后指出了AMServ和Meta-AaaS的新趋势和研究方向。AMServ和Meta-AaaS将为人工智能时代的人类社会和服务带来巨大机遇，并促进未来新兴服务业的快速发展。

Original abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) is reconstructing the digital virtual world, upgrading agents through enhancing their abilities in autonomous learning, multi-modal interaction, content generation, and collaborative decision-making. In particular, the shift from conversational chatbots to agentic AI, the most recent significant technical breakthrough of GenAI, has brought a new form of services, agentic services and Agent-as-a-Service (AaaS), in which the agent's abilities are encapsulated, such as perception, decision-making, execution, collaboration, and content generation, to provide the customized agent services to users. The metaverse is a virtual ecosystem for human life, work, creation, and entertainment, supported by the new generation of digital technologies. Through combining agentic services and the metaverse, an Agentic Metaverse Service, denoted as AMServ, is produced for metaverse business processing, as a new form of metaverse service. The AaaS in the metaverse environment, denoted as Meta-AaaS, as an approach to realize AMServ, has become a new paradigm of agentic services and service computing. This paper overviews the evolution and new features of agents and services empowered by GenAI, reveals the roles and principles of agentic services in the metaverse environment, presents the forms, characteristics, and principles of the AMServ and the Meta-AaaS, discusses the typical application examples of the AMServ and the Meta-AaaS, and finally points out the new tendencies and research directions of the AMServ and the Meta-AaaS. The AMServ and the Meta-AaaS will bring great opportunities to human society and services in the AI era, and promote the rapid development of emerging service industries in the future.

</details>


### [74] [Agentic Method for Deterministic Validation of Legacy Code Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28271)
*Andras Ferenczi,Jordan Docherty,Mariya Bessonov,Matthew Findlay,Krishna Lingamneni*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出一种名为Locksmith Loop的智能体测试综合方法，通过迭代见证搜索和保持等价性的变异来提升COBOL到Java迁移的测试覆盖率，并使用确定性预言机验证生成代码的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留COBOL程序向Java迁移需要大量测试，但常因缺乏测试数据和难以验证所有边界情况而受阻，传统输入搜索方法容易陷入覆盖率平台期。

Method: 准备两个运行时环境（COBOL源和Java目标），各自用模拟对象插桩并在通用硬件上执行；然后进行迭代智能体循环：先进行见证搜索以穿透分支，再进行保持等价性的变异；当到达路由边界时，分析器识别锁定段落并指导后续探索。

Result: 在三个COBOL-Java案例（含开源和内部生产程序，430至4,114行代码）中，Locksmith持续提升覆盖率，开源程序接近全覆盖，内部程序达到91.90%分支覆盖率；生成Java在确定性检查下与COBOL参考完全匹配。

Conclusion: Locksmith Loop是一种有效验证智能体编码输出的新方法，利用确定性预言机克服了测试数据缺失和边界覆盖难题，展示了在COBOL迁移场景中的实用价值。

中文摘要：将遗留的COBOL程序迁移到Java需要进行大量测试以确保功能正确。这项工作常常因缺乏测试数据和验证所有边界情况的困难而变得复杂。在本文中，我们提出了一种新颖的基于智能体的测试综合方法，称为“Locksmith Loop”。该方法首先准备两个运行时环境：COBOL源代码和生成的Java目标均在通用硬件上于主机外执行，并分别用模拟对象（mocks）进行插桩；然后，一个迭代的智能体循环执行“见证搜索”（Witness Search），通过输入模拟对象来穿透程序分支，随后进行保持等价性的变异（parity-preserving mutations）。当到达路由边界时，分析器会识别出一个“锁定段落”（Locked Paragraph）：即阻止更深层探索的条件。在三个COBOL到Java的案例研究中，涵盖两个开源程序和一个内部生产级COBOL程序，源代码行数从430行到4,114行不等，Locksmith持续地提高了超出输入搜索平台期的覆盖率，在两个开源程序上几乎达到了完全覆盖，在内部生产级COBOL程序上达到了91.90%的分支覆盖率。生成的Java在所有接受的测试用例中均通过确定性等价检查，与COBOL参考实现匹配。通过这些发现，我们展示了据我们所知的一种使用确定性预言机（deterministic oracle）验证智能体编码输出的新方法。

Original abstract: Migration of legacy COBOL programs to Java requires extensive testing to ensure correct functionality. This effort is often complicated by the lack of test data and the difficulty of validating all corner cases. In this paper we propose a novel agentic test-synthesis method, the "Locksmith Loop," which is initiated by preparing two runtime environments: the COBOL source and the generated Java target are each instrumented with mocks and executed off-mainframe on commodity hardware, then an iterative agentic loop performs Witness Search over input mocks to penetrate program branches, followed by parity-preserving mutations. When routing boundaries are reached, an analyzer identifies a Locked Paragraph: a condition preventing deeper exploration. Across three COBOL-Java case studies, spanning two open-source programs and one internal production-like COBOL program and ranging from 430 to 4,114 source lines, Locksmith consistently improved coverage beyond input-search plateaus, reaching nearly complete coverage on the two open-source programs and 91.90% branch coverage on the internal production-like COBOL program. The generated Java matched the COBOL reference under deterministic parity checks in all accepted test cases. Through these findings we demonstrate, to the best of our knowledge, a novel approach for validating agentic coding output using a deterministic oracle.

</details>


### [75] [From Textual Requirements to Microservice Architectures - A Comprehensive Evaluation of LLM-Based Design Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28307)
*Danyllo Albuquerque,José Renan,Guillermo Rodríguez,Guillermo Rodríguez,Emanuel Dantas,Ademar França,Mirko Perkusich,Kyller Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 研究了OpenAI o3在零样本和少样本提示下从文本需求生成微服务架构的能力，发现少样本显著提高了服务识别和通信恢复的一致性，专家评估也支持少样本输出更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务架构分解传统上依赖代码，难以在仅有文本需求的设计早期使用；尽管LLM有进展，但缺乏其从需求生成完整架构的经验证据。

Method: 混合方法研究：使用OpenAI o3，在零样本和少样本提示下对Bookstore和PetClinic两个系统各执行一次；通过精确率、召回率、F1分数与参考架构比较，并让盲法专家评估正确性、完整性、模块性、合理性及开放反馈。

Result: 少样本在服务识别上F1=0.97（零样本=0.79）；通信恢复少样本F1=0.82（零样本=0.61），且减少了不支持的依赖；专家评估表明少样本架构更模块化、连贯和合理。

Conclusion: OpenAI o3在示例提示指导下有潜力实现需求驱动的微服务架构综合，但结果受模型和特定小系统影响，不能视为模型无关的普遍证明。

中文摘要：微服务架构已成为现代化单体系统的主流选择，但识别合适的服务仍然具有挑战性且 largely 依赖人工。现有的分解方法主要基于代码，限制了在仅有文本需求可用的早期设计阶段的适用性。尽管大型语言模型（LLMs）取得了进展，但关于其从自然语言需求中综合出完整微服务架构（包括服务定义和服务间交互）能力的经验证据仍然有限。本研究探讨了LLM是否能够弥合需求工程与架构设计之间的鸿沟，仅从文本需求生成架构，并评估结构一致性和结果的感知质量。我们使用OpenAI o3在零样本（ZS）和少样本（FS）提示下对两个系统（Bookstore、PetClinic）进行了混合方法研究，每个系统/条件下执行一次。架构通过以下方式评估：（i）与参考架构比较，使用精确率、召回率和F1分数进行服务识别和通信恢复；（ii）盲法专家评估正确性、完整性、模块性和合理性，以及开放式反馈综合。OpenAI o3在FS提示下识别服务的一致性更高（ZS的F1=0.79，FS的F1=0.97）。通信恢复更具挑战性：ZS产生密集架构，召回率高但精确率低（F1=0.61），而FS提高了一致性，达到F1=0.82并减少了不支持的依赖。专家评估证实了这些结果，FS架构比ZS输出更具模块性、连贯性和合理性。OpenAI o3在示例提示指导下显示出需求驱动综合的潜力。结果来自两个小型系统，具有模型和上下文特定性，并非模型无关的证据。

Original abstract: Microservice architectures have become dominant for modernizing monolithic systems, yet identifying appropriate services remains challenging and largely manual. Existing decomposition approaches are predominantly code-centric, limiting applicability in early design stages where only textual requirements are available. Despite advances in Large Language Models (LLMs), limited empirical evidence exists on their ability to synthesize complete microservice architectures from natural-language requirements, including service definitions and inter-service interactions. This study investigates whether an LLM can bridge requirements engineering and architectural design, generating architectures solely from textual requirements and evaluating structural agreement and perceived quality of results. We conduct a mixed-method study using OpenAI o3 under zero-shot (ZS) and few-shot (FS) prompting across two systems (Bookstore, PetClinic), one execution per system/condition. Architectures are evaluated through (i) comparison with reference architectures using precision, recall, and F1-score for service identification and communication recovery, and (ii) a blinded expert assessment of correctness, completeness, modularity, and plausibility, plus open feedback synthesis. OpenAI o3 identifies services with higher agreement under FS prompting (F1 = 0.79 for ZS versus = 0.97 for FS). Communication recovery is more challenging: ZS produces dense architectures with high recall but low precision (F1 = 0.61), while FS improves agreement, reaching F1 = 0.82 and reducing unsupported dependencies. Expert evaluation corroborates these results, with FS architectures perceived as more modular, coherent, and plausible than ZS outputs. OpenAI o3 shows potential for requirements-driven synthesis when guided by exemplar prompting. Results are model- and context-specific from two small systems, not model-independent proof.

</details>


### [76] [Structural Validation of LLM-Generated Microservice Decompositions Using Source-Code Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28331)
*Daniel Silva,Renan Alves,Emanuel Dantas Filho,Ademar Sousa Neto,Mirko Perkusich,Danyllo Wagner Albuquerque,Kyller Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文在控制映射覆盖率后发现，OpenAI o3生成的微服务分解在零样本和少样本提示下具有等效的结构符合性，TPD值在PetClinic和Bookstore上分别为68.0%和83.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM能生成语义合理的分解，但尚不清楚其是否保留源代码中的结构依赖，因此需要评估结构符合性。

Method: 提出基于静态依赖分析的自动化验证管道，比较零样本和少样本提示，使用TPD和TVD指标，并进行鲁棒性分析以控制类到服务映射覆盖率的差异。

Result: 归一化后，两种提示策略在PetClinic上TPD为68.0%，在Bookstore上TPD为83.3%，结构符合性等效。

Conclusion: 结构评估应明确控制映射覆盖率，否则提示策略之间的差异可能反映方法学偏差而非真实的架构质量。

中文摘要：将单体系统分解为微服务是软件现代化中的关键活动。尽管大语言模型（LLMs）能够根据文本需求生成语义上合理的分解方案，但这些方案是否保留了源代码中实现的结构依赖关系仍不清楚。本文评估了OpenAI o3为PetClinic和Bookstore系统生成的微服务分解的结构符合性。我们提出了一种基于静态依赖分析的自动化验证管道，并使用依赖保持（TPD）和依赖违反（TVD）指标比较了零样本和少样本提示。进行了鲁棒性分析以控制类到服务映射覆盖率的差异。归一化后，两种提示策略产生了等效的结构符合性，TPD值分别达到68.0%（PetClinic）和83.3%（Bookstore）。研究结果表明，对LLM生成的分解进行结构评估应明确控制映射覆盖率，因为提示策略之间的明显差异可能反映方法学偏差而非真实的架构质量。

Original abstract: Decomposing monolithic systems into microservices is a key activity in software modernization. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate semantically plausible decompositions from textual requirements, it remains unclear whether these proposals preserve the structural dependencies implemented in the source code. This paper evaluates the structural adherence of microservice decompositions generated by OpenAI o3 for the PetClinic and Bookstore systems. We propose an automated validation pipeline based on static dependency analysis and compare zero-shot and few-shot prompting using dependency preservation (TPD) and dependency violation (TVD) metrics. A robustness analysis was conducted to control for differences in class-to-service mapping coverage. After normalization, both prompting strategies produced equivalent structural adherence, achieving TPD values of 68.0% (PetClinic) and 83.3% (Bookstore). The findings demonstrate that structural evaluations of LLM-generated decompositions should explicitly control for mapping coverage, as apparent differences between prompting strategies may otherwise reflect methodological bias rather than genuine architectural quality.

</details>


### [77] [Demystifying Solana Bots: From GitHub Blueprints to On-Chain Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28424)
*Xiaoye Zheng,Yujing Chen,Minghao Wu,David Lo,Difan Xie,Daoyuan Wu,Xiaohu Yang,Zhiyuan Wan*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 对Solana机器人进行了大规模实证研究，通过GitHub仓库和链上交易数据，提出了15类机器人分类法和五阶段操作流程，揭示了交易行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏对Solana机器人实际实现及其链上表现的系统性理解，现有研究多关注性能、安全或特定现象。

Method: 收集586个GitHub机器人仓库和200个Solana机器人地址（超4400万笔交易），从实现和链上行为两个视角进行实证分析。

Result: 得出15类机器人分类法（分属5个领域），识别出共享的五阶段操作流程，并发现不同平台和资产上的交易行为系统性差异。

Conclusion: 提供未来研究方向和Solana机器人构建与运行的实用建议。

中文摘要：Solana是一个新兴的区块链平台，旨在实现高吞吐量和低交易费用，使得大规模提交交易的成本低廉，从而增加了机器人垃圾邮件及相关金融利用的风险。Solana机器人通常是链下软件系统，通过构建和提交交易在竞争激烈的链上执行环境中运行，截至2026年1月，去中心化交易所上与机器人相关的交易日均交易量超过2.5亿美元。先前关于Solana的研究考察了系统性能、智能合约安全以及特定的链上现象。然而，我们仍然缺乏对Solana机器人在实践中实现什么以及这些实现如何表现为可观察的链上执行指纹的系统性理解。为弥补这一空白，我们从两个互补视角对Solana机器人进行了大规模实证研究：（i）从GitHub收集的586个机器人仓库，以及（ii）Solana上的200个机器人地址，包含超过4400万笔链上交易。我们的研究得出一个基于实现的Solana机器人分类法，包含15个类别，分为五个领域（例如，交易操作、MEV和链上分析），识别出机器人实现中普遍存在的五阶段操作流程，并揭示了Solana机器人在不同交易平台和资产上的链上交易行为的系统性差异。基于我们的发现，我们指出了未来的研究方向，并为在Solana区块链上构建和运行机器人提供了建议。

Original abstract: Solana is an emerging blockchain platform designed for high throughput and low transaction fees, making it inexpensive to submit transactions at scale and, consequently, increasing exposure to bot spamming and related financial exploitation. Solana bots are typically off-chain software systems that operate in a competitive on-chain execution environment by constructing and submitting transactions, and the bot-related transactions on the decentralized exchanges exceed 250 million dollars in daily trading volume in January 2026. Prior studies on Solana have examined system performance, smart-contract security, and specific on-chain phenomena. However, we still lack a systematic understanding of what Solana bots implement in practice and how these implementations manifest as observable on-chain execution fingerprints. To address this gap, we performed a large-scale empirical study of Solana bots from two complementary views: (i) 586 bot repositories collected from GitHub, and (ii) 200 bot addresses on Solana, with over 44 million on-chain transactions. Our study derives an implementation-grounded taxonomy of Solana bots comprising 15 categories grouped into five domains (e.g., Trading Operations, MEV, and On-chain Analytics), identifies a largely shared five-stage operational pipeline manifested in bot implementations, and uncovers systematic variation in on-chain trading behaviors of Solana bots across diverse trading platforms and assets. Based on our findings, we highlight future research directions, and provide recommendations for building and operating bots on the Solana blockchain.

</details>


### [78] [Emerging Challenges in Threat Modeling for GenAI-Augmented Systems: A View from the Trenches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28431)
*Nicolás E. Díaz Ferreyra,Manish Mahesh Kumar,Nohemí Villarreal,Pankaj Pantel,Immo Brueggemann,Riccardo Scandariato*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文在中小型企业环境中探索性评估了三种GenAI感知的威胁建模方法，发现现有方法在识别GenAI特定风险（如供应链和人为因素）方面支持有限，并报告了从业者的可用性看法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式人工智能（GenAI）在软件系统中的普及，传统威胁建模方法（如STRIDE）不足以评估GenAI特有的风险，因此需要在中小型企业（SME）环境中探索和评估适应GenAI的威胁建模方法。

Method: 作者进行了快速文献综述以选择相关技术，然后系统地将三种入围方法应用于一个涉及GenAI增强系统的工业案例研究，并收集从业者对可用性和集成性的看法。

Result: 不同方法识别的威胁存在差异，且现有方法对某些GenAI特定风险类别（特别是软件供应链和以人为中心的安全问题）支持有限。从业者报告了感知到的努力和采用挑战。

Conclusion: 现有GenAI感知威胁建模方法在SME环境中存在差距，需要进一步改进以更好地支持GenAI特定风险，同时考虑实际工作流程中的可用性和集成性。

中文摘要：威胁建模仍然是安全软件工程中的一项核心任务，因为它能够从系统架构中识别安全问题。随着生成式人工智能（GenAI）在软件系统中日益普及，传统的威胁建模方法（如STRIDE）已不足以评估新兴的GenAI特定风险。在这项工作中，我们展示了在中小型企业（SME）环境中对GenAI感知威胁建模方法的探索性评估的首批结果。为此，我们进行了快速文献综述以选择相关技术，并系统地将三种入围方法应用于涉及GenAI增强系统的工业案例研究。结果突出了每种技术所识别威胁的差异，并揭示了对某些GenAI特定风险类别的有限支持，特别是与软件供应链和以人为中心的安全问题相关的风险。我们还报告了从业者对这些方法在SME开发工作流程中的可用性和集成性的看法，包括他们感知到的努力和采用挑战。

Original abstract: Threat modeling remains a central task in secure software engineering, as it enables the identification of security issues from system architectures. As Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) becomes increasingly pervasive across software systems, traditional threat modeling methods (e.g., STRIDE) are insufficient to assess emerging GenAI-specific risks. In this work, we present the first results from an exploratory assessment of GenAI-aware threat modeling methods in a Small and Medium Enterprise (SME) setting. For this, we conducted a rapid literature review to select relevant techniques and systematically applied three shortlisted methods to an industrial case study involving a GenAI-augmented system. The results highlight differences in the threats identified by each technique and reveal limited support for certain GenAI-specific risk categories, particularly those related to software supply chains and human-centered security issues. We further report practitioners' perceptions of the usability and integration of these methods in SME development workflows, including their perceived effort and adoption challenges.

</details>


### [79] [CoGate: Confidence-Gated Co-Decoding for Secure Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28529)
*Minghao Hu,Lannan Luo,Allen Roush,Phillip Howard*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出了CoGate，一种基于置信度门控的协同解码方法，通过根据专家模型置信度控制其对代码生成的影响，生成更安全的代码，在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同解码方法未考虑专家模型置信度，在未见模式或OOD上下文下专家不自信时，其引导可能产生误导。

Method: CoGate基于专家模型的置信度，动态控制其在协同解码中的影响，在解码时结合专家模型与目标模型生成安全代码。

Result: 在多个LLM后端和基准上评估，CoGate优于CoSec+等方法，在CWEval上Func-Sec@10提升高达12.6%。

Conclusion: CoGate通过置信度门控有效提升代码生成安全性，优于现有协同解码方法。

中文摘要：大型语言模型广泛用于代码生成，但它们也可能因为预训练数据中学到的模式而产生不安全的程序。解码时引导已成为解决此问题的重要方法：一个小的专家模型与目标模型在每一步结合以生成更安全的代码，这被称为协同解码（co-decoding）。然而，现有协同解码方法的接受规则未考虑专家模型的置信度。当安全专家由于未见过的模式或分布外（OOD）上下文而不自信时，其引导可能会产生误导。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了CoGate，一种基于置信度门控的协同解码方法，根据专家模型的置信度控制其在协同解码过程中的影响。我们在多个LLM后端（CodeGen、DeepSeek-Coder、Qwen-Coder、StarCoder）以及多个代码生成基准（HumanEval、security suite、CWEval）上实现了我们的方法并进行了评估。我们的方法在多个基准上优于现有协同解码方法（CoSec+），在CWEval上实现了高达12.6%的Func-Sec@10提升。

Original abstract: Large language models are widely used for code generation, but they can also produce insecure programs due to patterns learned from their pretraining data. Decoding-time steering has become an important solution to this problem: a small expert model is combined with the target model at each step to generate more secure code, which is referred to as co-decoding. However, the acceptance rule for existing co-decoding approaches does not consider the expert model's confidence. When the security expert is unconfident due to unseen patterns or out-of-distribution (OOD) contexts, its guidance can therefore be misleading. To address the challenge, we propose CoGate, a confidence-gated co-decoding approach that controls the expert's influence on the co-decoding process based on its confidence. We implement our approach and evaluate it across multiple LLM backends (CodeGen, DeepSeek-Coder, Qwen-Coder, StarCoder) on several code generation benchmarks (HumanEval, security suite, and CWEval). Our approach outperforms existing co-decoding methods (CoSec+) across multiple benchmarks, achieving up to a 12.6% gain of Func-Sec@10 on CWEval.

</details>


### [80] [PAIChecker: Uncovering and Checking PR-Issue Misalignment in SWE-Bench-Like Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28587)
*Manyi Wang,Junjielong Xu,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出PAIChecker多智能体系统，用于检测SWE-bench类基准中PR-Issue错位问题，在SWE-Gym和SWE-bench Multilingual上分别达到92.12%和91.67%的二元准确率，优于所有对比的LLM骨干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SWE-bench类基准的构建流程常因PR-Issue配对错位而产生噪声，系统性研究发现13.6%的实例存在五种模式的错位，影响评估可靠性，因此需要一种能准确、可扩展地检测这种错位的方法。

Method: 提出PAIChecker，一个多智能体系统，采用三阶段设计：特定模式识别、跨智能体标签合成和代码级验证，以结合模式发现、多角度判断和代码证据，实现更准确、通用且逐步验证的检测。

Result: 在SWE-Gym和SWE-bench Multilingual上的实验中，PAIChecker在所有四个LLM骨干网络上均取得最佳性能，二元准确率分别达到92.12%和91.67%。

Conclusion: PAIChecker能够有效检测SWE-bench类基准中的PR-Issue错位，为未来可靠且可扩展地构建此类基准提供了支持，同时展现出良好的泛化能力。

中文摘要：类似SWE-bench的基准测试被广泛用于评估大语言模型（LLM）的问题解决能力。它们通常遵循一个通用的构建流程：通过从PR描述中提取问题引用，将每个PR（拉取请求）与其关联的问题配对；问题描述作为问题陈述，PR补丁作为测试预言。然而，由于大型仓库开发和维护的固有复杂性，这种PR-Issue配对在实践中经常出现错位。在本工作中，我们系统地研究了SWE-bench Verified实例，发现13.6%的实例在十一个细粒度场景中表现出五种模式的错位。为了在未来能够可靠且可扩展地构建这些基准测试，我们提出了PAIChecker，一个用于检查类似SWE-bench基准中PR-Issue错位的多智能体系统。具体来说，PAIChecker采用三阶段设计，结合了特定模式识别、跨智能体标签合成和代码级验证，从而实现更准确、更通用且逐步验证的检测。在SWE-Gym和SWE-bench Multilingual上的实验表明，PAIChecker在所有四个LLM骨干网络上均取得了最佳性能，二元准确率分别达到92.12%和91.67%。

Original abstract: SWE-bench-like benchmarks are widely used for evaluating LLM's issue resolution capability. They typically follow a common construction pipeline: each PR (Pull Request) is paired with its linked issue by extracting issue references from the PR description; the issue description is used as the problem statement, and the PR patch serves as the test oracle. However, due to the inherent complexity of developing and maintaining large repositories, such PR-Issue pairings are often misaligned in practice. In this work, we systematically study SWE-bench Verified instances, finding that 13.6% exhibit misalignment across five patterns in eleven fine-grained scenarios. To enable reliable and scalable construction of those benchmarks in the future, we propose PAIChecker, a multi-agent system for checking PR-Issue misalignment in SWE-bench-like benchmarks. Specifically, PAIChecker adopts a three-phase design that combines specific pattern identification, cross-agent label synthesis, and code-level validation, thereby enabling more accurate, generalizable, and progressively verified detection. Experiments on SWE-Gym and SWE-bench Multilingual show that PAIchecker achieves the best performance across all four LLM backbones, reaching up to 92.12% and 91.67% binary accuracy, respectively.

</details>


### [81] [Change2Task: From Repository Changes to Executable Coding Agent Tasks and Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28591)
*Haomin Qi,Xingliang Wang,Xuanqi Gao,Baihui Sang,Xin Zhang,Minghua Ma,Pengfei Gao,Yu Kang,Qingwei Lin,Saravan Rajmohan,Dongmei Zhang,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: Change2Task利用仓库历史将合并的拉取请求转化为现代仓库上的已验证编码任务，以79.6%的成功率构建出可执行数据，并显著降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体的扩展需要持续供应可执行的数据，但手动构建任务成本高且难以规模化，因此需要一种自动从真实仓库历史中生成验证任务的方法。

Method: Change2Task通过多种策略（补丁反转、代码映射、智能体重建）将历史拉取请求适配到现代仓库状态，并验证从健康基线到任务状态再到恢复状态的完整生命周期，从而生成可执行任务。

Result: 在1130个源变更上实现了79.6%的验证任务构建成功率，比基于拉取请求的基线多恢复29.2%的任务，历史与重建案例在智能体评估中达到98.0%的一致性，同时整个管线开销降低10.8%。

Conclusion: Change2Task展示了从仓库历史自动构造高质量编码任务数据的可行性，可有效支持编码智能体的训练与评估，并显著减少资源消耗。

中文摘要：扩展编码智能体需要持续供应可执行的数据，用于训练、基准测试和持续评估。每个任务必须将真实的软件状态与规格说明、开发工具和可靠的验证相结合。为了扩大这一供应，我们提出了Change2Task，一个基于仓库历史的系统，将已合并的拉取请求转换为同一仓库健康现代版本上的已验证任务。它将历史证据与演化的代码对齐，通过补丁反转、代码映射或智能体重建来重构任务状态，并验证从健康基态到任务状态以及恢复状态的完整生命周期。通过从维护良好的环境中提取基于开发者证据的多个任务，Change2Task为编码智能体的训练和评估提供了可执行的数据，同时减少了重复的环境搭建、存储和任务构建工作。我们通过五个常见且广泛采用的编码智能体任务族来评估该系统：漏洞修复、功能添加、测试生成、应用程序编程接口迁移和安全修复。从1130个符合构建条件的源变更开始，Change2Task在这些任务族中实现了79.6%的验证任务构建成功率。在匹配的候选集上，它比基于拉取请求的构建基线多恢复了29.2%的已验证任务。在智能体评估下，历史案例和重建案例达到了高达98.0%的匹配结果一致性，而现代基态的复用使整个管线的测量开销降低了10.8%。

Original abstract: Scaling coding agents requires a continuing supply of executable data for training, benchmarking, and continuous evaluation. Each task must couple a realistic software state with a specification, development tools, and reliable verification. To expand this supply, we present Change2Task, a system grounded in repository history that converts merged pull requests into verified tasks on healthy modern revisions of the same repository. It aligns historical evidence with evolved code, reconstructs task states through Patch Reversal, Code Mapping, or Agent Reconstruction, and validates the lifecycle from a healthy base to a task state and a restored state. By deriving multiple tasks grounded in developer evidence from maintained environments, Change2Task provides executable data for coding agent training and evaluation while reducing repeated environment setup, storage, and task construction effort. We evaluate the system through five common and widely adopted coding agent task families: Bug Fix, Feature Addition, Test Generation, Application Programming Interface Migration, and Security Repair. Starting from 1,130 source changes eligible for construction, Change2Task achieves 79.6% verified task construction success across these task families. On a matched candidate set, it recovers 29.2% more verified tasks than a construction baseline based on pull requests. Historical and reconstructed cases achieve up to 98.0% matched outcome agreement under agent evaluation, while reuse of modern bases reduces measured expenditure across the complete pipeline by 10.8%.

</details>
